# List 24

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spent</b></td><td>/spent/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精疲力竭的；用废的</td><td>utterly spent after work<br>工作后精疲力竭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Utterly spent after the double shift, he fell asleep on the sofa.  连上两班后精疲力竭，他在沙发上睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beaten</td><td>/ˈbiːtən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疲惫不堪的；被打败的</td><td>look thoroughly beaten<br>显得疲惫不堪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He looked beaten after twelve hours on the road.  在路上跑了十二小时后他显得疲惫不堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bleary</td><td>/ˈblɪri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（眼）疲倦模糊的</td><td>bleary with fatigue<br>疲倦得双眼模糊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She arrived bleary and yawning after the night flight.  夜间航班后她双眼倦怠、连连打呵欠地到了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 done</td><td>/ˈdʌn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>累垮的；完成的</td><td>completely done in<br>彻底累垮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 By the last mile the runners were completely done.  跑到最后一英里时选手们彻底累垮了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drained</td><td>/ˈdreɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>精疲力竭的；被排空的</td><td>feel completely drained<br>感到精疲力竭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the twelve-hour shift she felt utterly drained.  上了十二小时的班，她感到彻底精疲力竭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enervate</del></td><td>/ˈenərveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>enervate the whole team<br>使全队疲弱无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long heat wave enervated the whole team before the final match.  漫长的热浪在决赛前使全队疲弱无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exhausted</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːstɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疲惫不堪的；耗尽的</td><td>exhausted after the climb<br>登山后疲惫不堪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hikers arrived exhausted but happy at the mountain hut.  徒步者们疲惫却愉快地到达了山中小屋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fatigued</td><td>/fəˈtiːɡd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疲劳的，疲倦的</td><td>fatigued muscles<br>疲劳的肌肉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fatigued climbers rested before the final ascent.  疲倦的登山者在最后攀登前休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jaded</td><td>/ˈdʒeɪdɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>厌倦的，没兴趣没热情的</td><td>a jaded traveler<br>厌倦旅行的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After twenty countries, even the most enthusiastic traveler becomes jaded and restless.  走过二十个国家后，就算最热情的旅行者也会厌倦而烦躁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prostrate</td><td>/ˈprɑːstreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>平躺的，俯卧的；使极度虚弱</td><td>lie prostrate on the ground<br>平躺在地上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exhausted runner lay prostrate on the ground after crossing the line.  那名精疲力竭的跑者冲线后平躺在地上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tired</td><td>/ˈtaɪərd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>疲倦的；厌倦的</td><td>tired of waiting<br>厌倦了等待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 By midnight everyone was too tired to argue further.  到午夜时，所有人都累得无法再争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wearied</td><td>/ˈwɪrid/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疲惫的，厌倦的</td><td>wearied by the journey<br>被旅途累坏了</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearied by months of travel, he sold the van and flew home.  被数月旅行累垮后，他卖掉面包车飞回家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worn-out</td><td>/ˌwɔːrn ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>精疲力竭的；破旧的</td><td>a worn-out nurse<br>精疲力竭的护士</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The worn-out nurse slept through her alarm.  精疲力竭的护士睡过了闹钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consumed</td><td>/kənˈsuːmd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>耗尽的；被（情绪）吞噬的</td><td>consumed by jealousy<br>被嫉妒吞噬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was consumed by guilt for weeks after the accident.  事故之后他被负罪感折磨了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 depleted</td><td>/dɪˈpliːtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>耗尽的，枯竭的</td><td>severely depleted fish stocks<br>严重枯竭的鱼类资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of overfarming left the soil badly depleted of nutrients.  多年过度耕作使土壤养分严重枯竭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 active</td><td>/ˈæktɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活跃的，活动的</td><td>an active volcano<br>活火山</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stays active by swimming every morning.  她每天早上游泳以保持活跃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 energetic</td><td>/ˌenərˈdʒetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的</td><td>an energetic performance<br>活力四射的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even at seventy she remains remarkably energetic and full of plans.  即使七十岁了，她依然精力充沛，计划满满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invigorated</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪɡəreɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的，充满活力的</td><td>feel invigorated afterwards<br>之后感到精力充沛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt invigorated after a cold swim in the lake.  在湖中冷水游泳后他感到精力充沛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 peppy</td><td>/ˈpepi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的，活泼的</td><td>a peppy little tune<br>活泼的小曲子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A peppy tune played while the children marched in.  孩子们走进来时播放着一首活泼的曲子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 strengthened</td><td>/ˈstreŋθənd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>被加强的，增强的</td><td>a strengthened resolve<br>增强的决心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The setback left him with a strengthened resolve to continue.  这次挫折让他继续下去的决心更强了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 strong</td><td>/strɔːŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强壮的，坚固的</td><td>strong arms<br>强壮的臂膀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge is strong enough to carry heavy trucks.  这座桥足够坚固，能承载重型卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tireless</td><td>/ˈtaɪərləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不疲倦的，不懈的</td><td>a tireless campaigner<br>不懈的活动家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was a tireless campaigner for clean water.  她是一位不懈争取清洁饮水的活动家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vitalized</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəlaɪzd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的，被激活的</td><td>a vitalized local economy<br>被激活的地方经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vitalized town grew quickly after the new railway opened.  新铁路通车后，这座重新充满活力的小镇迅速发展。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 weariless</del></td><td>/ˈwɪriləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不疲倦的，不厌倦的</td><td>weariless devotion<br>不倦的投入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His weariless devotion to the project impressed everyone.  他对这个项目不倦的投入让所有人钦佩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>slump</b></td><td>/ˈslʌmp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>暴跌，急剧下降</td><td>a sharp economic slump<br>急剧的经济衰退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Housing prices suffered a sharp slump after interest rates rose.  利率上升后，房价急剧下跌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recession</td><td>/rɪˈseʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>经济衰退；后退</td><td>slide into recession<br>陷入衰退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country slid into recession after the currency collapsed.  货币崩溃后该国陷入衰退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 depression</td><td>/dɪˈpreʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>沮丧，抑郁；萧条</td><td>sink into deep depression<br>陷入深度抑郁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After losing his job he slid into a long depression.  失业之后，他陷入了长久的抑郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 boom</td><td>/ˈbuːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>繁荣，激增；轰鸣</td><td>an economic boom<br>经济繁荣期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tourism boomed after the new airport opened last spring.  新机场去年春天启用后，旅游业迅速繁荣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spiny</b></td><td>/ˈspaɪni/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>棘手的，麻烦的；多刺的</td><td>a spiny political question<br>棘手的政治问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor avoided that spiny political question during the entire debate.  市长在整场辩论中都避开那个棘手的政治问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 catchy</td><td>/ˈkætʃi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>棘手的；易记的</td><td>a catchy question<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The examiner slipped a catchy question into the paper.  主考人在试卷里塞了一道棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delicate</td><td>/ˈdeləkət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微妙的，精细的；脆弱的</td><td>a delicate distinction<br>微妙的区别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a delicate ear for the subtle differences between dialects.  她对方言之间的细微差别有很灵敏的耳朵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 difficult</td><td>/ˈdɪfəkəlt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>困难的，费力的</td><td>a difficult climb<br>艰难的攀登</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final section of the trail is genuinely difficult.  这条小径的最后一段确实艰难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 knotty</td><td>/ˈnɑːti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，困难的</td><td>a knotty question<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor posed a knotty question that nobody could answer confidently.  教授提出了一个没人能自信回答的棘手问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 problematic</td><td>/ˌprɑːbləˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有问题的，成问题的</td><td>a problematic assumption<br>有问题的假设</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The data set is problematic because many records are missing.  该数据集有问题，因为许多记录缺失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sensitive</td><td>/ˈsensətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏感的，灵敏的</td><td>sensitive to light<br>对光敏感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This instrument is sensitive enough to detect the faintest vibration.  这台仪器灵敏到能测出最微弱的震动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sticky</td><td>/ˈstɪki/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粘的，有粘性的</td><td>sticky fingers<br>粘乎乎的手指</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children's hands were sticky with melted chocolate candy.  孩子们的手被融化的巧克力糖弄得粘乎乎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ticklish</td><td>/ˈtɪklɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>棘手的，需小心处理的</td><td>a ticklish negotiation<br>棘手的谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two governments face a ticklish negotiation over fishing rights.  两国政府面临一场关于捕鱼权的棘手谈判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thorny</td><td>/ˈθɔːrni/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>棘手的</td><td>a thorny problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council postponed a thorny problem about school funding again.  议会又一次推迟了学校经费这个棘手问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tough</td><td>/ˈtʌf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>艰难的，费力的；坚韧的</td><td>a tough assignment<br>艰巨的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editing that manuscript proved a tough assignment.  编辑那份手稿被证明是项艰巨的任务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 troublesome</td><td>/ˈtrʌbəlsəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>麻烦的，令人烦恼的</td><td>a troublesome detail<br>麻烦的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One troublesome clause held up the whole contract.  一条麻烦的条款拖住了整份合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 easy</td><td>/ˈiːzi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易的，不费力的</td><td>an easy question<br>简单的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The homework looked easy, but it took him three hours to finish.  作业看起来容易，可他花了三个小时才做完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 effortless</td><td>/ˈefərtləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻松的，不费力的</td><td>effortless grace<br>毫不费力的优雅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her swimming looked effortless even in the rough water.  即使在波涛中她的游泳也显得毫不费力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 manageable</td><td>/ˈmænɪdʒəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易管理的，好对付的</td><td>a manageable workload<br>可应付的工作量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Split the task into smaller, manageable pieces first.  先把任务拆成较小、好处理的部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 painless</td><td>/ˈpeɪnləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无痛的，不费力的</td><td>a painless procedure<br>无痛的程序</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dentist promised a quick and painless procedure.  牙医承诺这是个快速无痛的过程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 simple</td><td>/ˈsɪmpəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单的，容易的</td><td>a simple task<br>简单的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Follow these simple steps and the machine will restart.  按这些简单步骤操作，机器就会重启。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 straightforward</td><td>/ˌstreɪtˈfɔːrwərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单明了的；坦率的</td><td>a straightforward procedure<br>简单直接的流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The instructions are straightforward enough for a child to follow.  说明简单明了，连孩子都能照着做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uncomplicated</td><td>/ˌʌnˈkɑːmplɪkeɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不复杂的，简单的</td><td>an uncomplicated design<br>不复杂的设计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He preferred an uncomplicated life in a quiet mountain village.  他偏爱在宁静山村过简单的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undemanding</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈmændɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>要求不高的，轻松的</td><td>an undemanding course<br>要求不高的课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She chose an undemanding role so she could rest more.  她选了个轻松的角色以便多休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spleen</b></td><td>/spliːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>怒气，怨恨</td><td>vent one's spleen<br>发泄怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He vented his spleen on anyone who dared question the decision.  谁敢质疑这个决定，他就冲谁发泄怒气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 angriness</del></td><td>/ˈæŋɡrinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>愤怒，怒气</td><td>a flash of angriness<br>一阵怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sudden angriness crept into his otherwise calm voice.  一股突然的怒气渗入他原本平静的声音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 choler</del></td><td>/ˈkɑːlər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>怒气，易怒</td><td>rouse his choler<br>激起他的怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The insult roused the old general's choler at once.  这句侮辱立刻激起了老将军的怒气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 furor</td><td>/ˈfjʊrɔːr/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>喧闹，狂怒；激动，狂热</td><td>cause a public furor<br>引起公众哗然</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting caused a public furor when it was first shown.  这幅画首次展出时引起公众哗然。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fury</td><td>/ˈfjʊri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>暴怒</td><td>fly into a fury<br>勃然大怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He flew into a fury when he saw the broken window.  他看到破碎的窗户时勃然大怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indignation</td><td>/ˌɪndɪɡˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>义愤，愤慨</td><td>public indignation<br>公众的愤慨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ruling provoked widespread public indignation.  这项裁决引发了广泛的公众愤慨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ire</td><td>/ˈaɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>愤怒，怒火</td><td>draw the ire of critics<br>引来批评者的怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new tax drew the ire of small business owners.  新税收招致了小企业主的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 madness</td><td>/ˈmædnəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>愤怒；疯狂</td><td>in a fit of madness<br>一时怒火中烧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He broke the window in a fit of madness.  他一时怒火中烧砸破了窗子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outrage</td><td>/ˈaʊˌtreɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>愤怒；暴行；激怒</td><td>public outrage<br>公众的愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The verdict caused outrage among the victim's family and friends.  判决引起受害者家人和朋友的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rage</td><td>/reɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>暴怒</td><td>fly into a rage<br>勃然大怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He flew into a rage when the waiter spilled the soup.  服务员把汤打翻时，他勃然大怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wrath</td><td>/ˈræθ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>暴怒，愤怒</td><td>incur his wrath<br>招致他的暴怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clumsy apology only incurred his wrath again.  笨拙的道歉只是再次招致了他的暴怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delight</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使高兴，使愉快；愉悦</td><td>delight the audience<br>使观众高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist delighted the audience with a lively encore.  这位年轻钢琴家用一首活泼的加演曲取悦了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pleasure</td><td>/ˈpleʒər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>愉快，快乐</td><td>take pleasure in reading<br>以阅读为乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She takes great pleasure in tending her small garden.  她非常喜爱照料自己的小花园。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>splice</b></td><td>/splaɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>接合，叠接</td><td>splice two cables together<br>把两根电缆接合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer spliced the two cables together and sealed the joint carefully.  工程师把两根电缆接合起来，仔细封好接头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 associate</td><td>/əˈsoʊʃieɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，结交；联想</td><td>associate with the group<br>与该团体结交</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to associate himself with such a risky plan.  他拒绝与这样冒险的计划有牵连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coalesce</td><td>/ˌkoʊəˈles/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>合并，融合</td><td>coalesce into a group<br>合并成一个团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scattered protests coalesced into a national movement.  分散的抗议汇合成一场全国性运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 combine</td><td>/kəmˈbaɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>结合，联合</td><td>combine two elements<br>结合两种元素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The recipe combines sweet and sour flavors very successfully.  这道菜谱把甜味和酸味结合得很成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conjoin</td><td>/kənˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>结合，连接</td><td>conjoin the two parts<br>把两部分接合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge conjoins the island and the mainland neatly.  这座桥把小岛与大陆巧妙相连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conjugate</td><td>/ˈkɑːndʒəɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>结合，配对；成对的</td><td>conjugate two molecules<br>使两个分子结合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers conjugate the drug to an antibody for precise delivery.  研究者把药物与抗体结合以实现精准投递。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 connect</td><td>/kəˈnekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，联结</td><td>connect the two wires<br>连接两根导线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A narrow tunnel connects the two halves of the building.  一条窄隧道把大楼的两半连起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 couple</td><td>/ˈkʌpəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>连接，结合；一对</td><td>couple the two carriages<br>连接两节车厢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers coupled the new carriage to the waiting train.  工人把新车厢挂到等候的列车上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fuse</td><td>/ˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>融合，熔合</td><td>fuse two traditions<br>融合两种传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her music fuses jazz rhythms with traditional folk melodies.  她的音乐把爵士节奏与传统民谣旋律融为一体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 interfuse</del></td><td>/ˌɪntərˈfjuz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使混合，交融</td><td>interfuse the two styles<br>交融两种风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The composer interfuses folk melodies with modern harmony.  作曲家把民间旋律与现代和声交融在一起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 marry</td><td>/ˈmeri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>结合，使联合；结婚</td><td>marry tradition with innovation<br>把传统与创新结合</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unify</td><td>/ˈjuːnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>统一，使成一体</td><td>unify the country<br>统一国家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A common language helped unify the scattered tribes into one nation.  共同的语言帮助把分散的部落统一为一个民族。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disassociate</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈsoʊʃieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分离，使脱离关系</td><td>disassociate oneself from<br>与…划清界限</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disconnect</td><td>/dɪskəˈnekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>断开，使分离</td><td>disconnect the power<br>切断电源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please disconnect the printer before you open its back panel.  打开后盖前请先断开打印机的连接。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disjoin</del></td><td>/dɪsˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使分开，使分离</td><td>disjoin the two parts<br>把两部分分开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer had to disjoin the pipes to replace a valve.  工程师得把管子分开才能更换阀门。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 divide</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分开，划分；分歧</td><td>divide into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher divided the class into four groups of five.  老师把班级分成四个五人小组。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sever</td><td>/ˈsevər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>切断，断绝</td><td>sever all ties<br>断绝一切关系</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 split</td><td>/ˈsplɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分裂，分开</td><td>split into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee split into two camps over the proposal.  委员会因该提案分裂成两派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sunder</td><td>/ˈsʌndər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分开，割断</td><td>sunder the alliance<br>使联盟破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bitter dispute sundered an alliance that had lasted decades.  这场激烈的争端割断了维持数十年的联盟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spontaneous</b></td><td>/spɑːnˈteɪniəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自发的，不经思索的</td><td>a spontaneous burst of applause<br>自发的一阵掌声</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 automatic</td><td>/ˌɔːtəˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>机械的，不经思索的；自动的</td><td>an automatic apology<br>机械的道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He murmured an automatic apology and hurried past me.  他机械地含糊道了个歉，匆匆从我身边走过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impulsive</td><td>/ɪmˈpʌlsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冲动的，鲁莽的</td><td>an impulsive decision<br>冲动的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Buying that car was an impulsive act he later regretted.  买那辆车是他后来后悔的冲动之举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instinctive</td><td>/ɪnˈstɪŋktɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>本能的，直觉的</td><td>an instinctive reaction<br>本能的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her instinctive distrust of the offer proved entirely correct.  她对这份提议本能的不信任完全正确。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 involuntary</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlənteri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非自愿的；无意识的</td><td>an involuntary reaction<br>不自觉的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Blinking is an involuntary response to sudden light.  眨眼是对突然强光的不自觉反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mechanical</td><td>/məˈkænɪkəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>机械的；敷衍呆板的</td><td>a mechanical response<br>机械敷衍的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a mechanical nod without listening to a single word.  他机械地点了点头，一个字也没听。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 natural</td><td>/ˈnætʃərəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自然的，正常的</td><td>a natural reaction<br>自然的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is natural to feel nervous before a big exam.  大考前紧张是很自然的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 calculated</td><td>/ˈkælkjuleɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>经过深思熟虑的，有意的</td><td>a calculated risk<br>经过权衡的风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investing now was a calculated risk that finally paid off.  现在投资是经过盘算的风险，最终有了回报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deliberate</td><td>/dɪˈlɪbərət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的，故意的</td><td>a deliberate attempt<br>故意的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay was deliberate, designed to weaken our position.  这次延误是故意的，目的是削弱我们的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intentional</td><td>/ɪnˈtenʃənəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>故意的，有意的</td><td>an intentional foul<br>故意犯规</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The omission was intentional rather than a simple oversight.  这一遗漏是故意的，而非单纯的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 planned</td><td>/ˈplænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>计划好的，有计划的</td><td>a carefully planned trip<br>精心策划的旅行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole ceremony was planned down to the last detail.  整个典礼被计划到了最后一个细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 predetermined</td><td>/ˌpriːdiˈtɜːrmɪnd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>预先确定的，事先决定的</td><td>a predetermined outcome<br>预先确定的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics said the vote had a predetermined outcome.  批评者说这次投票的结果早已预定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 premeditated</td><td>/priˈmedəˌteɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>预谋的，事先策划的</td><td>premeditated murder<br>预谋杀人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Prosecutors argued that the robbery was carefully premeditated.  检察官认为这起抢劫是精心预谋的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 studied</td><td>/ˈstʌdid/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的；刻意的</td><td>studied indifference<br>刻意装出的冷淡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered with studied calm that impressed the whole panel.  他以刻意保持的镇定作答，让整个评审组印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spoof</b></td><td>/spuːf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>轻松幽默的模仿，小恶搞</td><td>a spoof of the news<br>对新闻的幽默模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The show aired a clever spoof of the evening news broadcast.  节目播出了对晚间新闻的一段巧妙恶搞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burlesque</td><td>/bɜːrˈlesk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>滑稽夸张的模仿讽刺作品；恶搞</td><td>a burlesque of politics<br>对政治的滑稽恶搞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play is a clever burlesque of modern political debate.  这部戏是对当代政治辩论的巧妙滑稽模仿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 caricature</td><td>/ˈkærɪkətʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>讽刺画，夸张的滑稽模仿</td><td>draw a caricature<br>画一幅讽刺画</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cartoonist drew a cruel caricature of the new mayor.  漫画家画了一幅刻薄的新市长讽刺画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mimicking</td><td>/ˈmɪmɪkɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模仿的，仿效的</td><td>mimicking his gestures<br>模仿他的手势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comedian won laughs by mimicking the mayor's walk.  这位喜剧演员靠模仿市长走路的样子赢得笑声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parody</td><td>/ˈpærədi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（以嘲笑原作作者的）模仿作品，恶搞</td><td>a parody of the film<br>对该片的恶搞模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students wrote a hilarious parody of the morning speech at assembly.  学生们写了一篇滑稽的作品，恶搞集会上的早间讲话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 travesty</td><td>/ˈtrævəsti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>拙劣的、嘲弄性模仿</td><td>a travesty of justice<br>对正义的拙劣讽刺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the hasty trial a travesty of justice.  批评者称这场匆忙的审判是对正义的拙劣讽刺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sporadic</b></td><td>/spəˈrædɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>断断续续的</td><td>sporadic gunfire<br>断续的枪声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sporadic gunfire continued through the night on the edge of town.  镇子边缘整夜断断续续传来枪声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 few</td><td>/ˈfjuː/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>很少的，少数的</td><td>very few visitors<br>极少的来访者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few visitors reach the island in winter.  冬天很少有游客到达这座岛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infrequent</td><td>/ˌɪnˈfriːkwənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀少的，不常发生的</td><td>infrequent visits<br>难得的探访</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His visits home became increasingly infrequent.  他回家的次数越来越少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 occasional</td><td>/əˈkeɪʒənəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偶尔的，间或的</td><td>occasional showers<br>偶有阵雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He makes occasional visits to his hometown in autumn.  他秋天偶尔回故乡看看。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 odd</td><td>/ˈɑːd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>奇怪的；奇数的</td><td>an odd pair of socks<br>不成对的袜子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seemed odd that nobody answered the door at noon.  中午没人应门，这显得有点奇怪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rare</td><td>/ˈrer/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀少的，罕见的</td><td>a rare bird<br>罕见的鸟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is rare to see snow here in April.  四月这里下雪很罕见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scarce</td><td>/ˈskers/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀缺的，不足的</td><td>in scarce supply<br>供应短缺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh water became scarce during the drought.  旱灾期间淡水变得稀缺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seldom</td><td>/ˈseldəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>副词</td><td>很少，不常</td><td>seldom if ever<br>几乎从不</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He seldom answers the phone before ten in the morning.  他早上十点前很少接电话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncommon</td><td>/ʌnˈkɑːmən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不常见的，罕有的</td><td>not uncommon<br>并不罕见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such mistakes are not uncommon among beginners.  这类错误在初学者中并不罕见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frequent</td><td>/ˈfriːkwənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>频繁的，经常的</td><td>frequent visits<br>频繁的探访</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Frequent power cuts made it hard to finish the work.  频繁停电让工作难以完成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 habitual</td><td>/həˈbɪtʃuəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>习惯性的，惯常的</td><td>his habitual morning walk<br>他惯常的晨间散步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Habitual lateness eventually cost him the job.  惯常迟到最终让他失去了工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 repeated</td><td>/rɪˈpiːtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反复的，屡次的</td><td>repeated warnings<br>反复的警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated warnings from the inspector were simply ignored.  检查员反复的警告被完全无视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sprawl</b></td><td>/sprɔːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>杂乱无序地发展；蔓生，蔓延</td><td>sprawl across the countryside<br>向乡间蔓延扩张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap housing developments sprawled outward across the once quiet farmland.  廉价住宅区向外蔓延，铺满曾经安静的农田。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ramble</td><td>/ˈræmbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>漫谈，东拉西扯；漫步</td><td>ramble on about<br>喋喋不休地扯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rambled on about his childhood for a full hour.  他絮絮叨叨讲了整整一小时童年往事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scramble</td><td>/ˈskræmbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>使混乱，打乱；争抢</td><td>scramble the signal<br>打乱信号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The software scrambles the letters so the message stays private.  该软件打乱字母顺序以保证消息私密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 straddle</td><td>/ˈstrædəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>跨坐，跨越</td><td>straddle the border<br>横跨边界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old town straddles the border between two provinces.  这座老城横跨两省的边界。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sprightly</b></td><td>/ˈspraɪtli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼的，充满活力的</td><td>a sprightly old man<br>一位活泼的老人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sprightly old man led the hikers up the steep mountain path.  一位活泼的老人带着徒步者爬上陡峭的山路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 active</td><td>/ˈæktɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活跃的，活动的</td><td>an active volcano<br>活火山</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stays active by swimming every morning.  她每天早上游泳以保持活跃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 airy</td><td>/ˈeri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽敞通风的</td><td>a light airy room<br>明亮通风的房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They rented an airy apartment with tall windows.  他们租了一间有高窗、通风良好的公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 animated</td><td>/ˈænəˌmeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活跃的，生气勃勃的</td><td>an animated discussion<br>一场热烈的讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The class turned into an animated debate about ethics.  这堂课变成了一场关于伦理的热烈辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brisk</td><td>/brɪsk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满生机的，有活力的</td><td>a brisk walk<br>快步散步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brisk walk before breakfast leaves me alert all morning.  早餐前快走一趟能让我整个上午都很清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 energetic</td><td>/ˌenərˈdʒetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的</td><td>an energetic performance<br>活力四射的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even at seventy she remains remarkably energetic and full of plans.  即使七十岁了，她依然精力充沛，计划满满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frisky</td><td>/ˈfrɪski/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼欢腾的</td><td>a frisky puppy<br>一只活泼的小狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The frisky colt raced across the field at dawn.  黎明时那匹活泼的小马驹在田野上奔跑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gay</td><td>/ˈɡeɪ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，欢快的</td><td>gay laughter<br>欢快的笑声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gay music and bright lanterns filled the village square that evening.  那晚欢快的音乐和明亮的灯笼充满了村中广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jaunty</td><td>/ˈdʒɔːnti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻快的，活泼的</td><td>a jaunty stride<br>轻快的步伐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He walked into the office with a jaunty step and a broad smile.  他步伐轻快、笑容满面地走进了办公室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 kinetic</td><td>/kəˈnetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的；运动的</td><td>kinetic energy<br>动能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dancer's kinetic style filled the stage with motion.  这位舞者充满动感的风格让舞台生机勃勃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 mettlesome</del></td><td>/ˈmɛtəlsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>精神饱满的，勇猛的</td><td>a mettlesome young horse<br>精神饱满的小马</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mettlesome colt refused to stand still.  这匹精神饱满的小马驹不肯站定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 racy</td><td>/ˈreɪsi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼生动的</td><td>a racy narrative style<br>生动的叙述风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her racy narrative style keeps readers turning pages late into the night.  她生动活泼的叙述风格让读者深夜还在翻页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spirited</td><td>/ˈspɪrɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>生气勃勃的，活跃的</td><td>a spirited debate<br>一场热烈的辩论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidates held a spirited debate about tax policy.  候选人就税收政策展开了热烈的辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vital</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的；极重要的</td><td>full of vital energy<br>充满生命活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise keeps older people vital and mentally alert.  经常锻炼使老年人保持活力和头脑清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dead</td><td>/ˈded/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>死的；毫无生气的</td><td>a dead town at night<br>夜里死寂的小镇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The resort is completely dead in the winter months.  这个度假地在冬季几个月里毫无生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inactive</td><td>/ɪnˈæktɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不活动的，不活跃的</td><td>remain inactive for months<br>数月毫无动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The volcano has been inactive for over two hundred years.  这座火山已有两百多年没有活动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inanimate</td><td>/ɪnˈænɪmət/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无生命的</td><td>an inanimate object<br>无生命的物体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children often speak to inanimate objects as though they could reply.  孩子常对无生命的物体说话，仿佛它们能够回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lackadaisical</td><td>/ˌlækəˈdeɪzɪkəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>懒散的，无兴趣的</td><td>a lackadaisical attitude<br>懒散的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lackadaisical attitude toward homework worried both parents.  他对作业懒散的态度让父母都很担心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 languid</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡwɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没精打采的，虚弱的</td><td>a languid summer afternoon<br>慢悠悠的夏日午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A languid summer afternoon passed with nobody willing to leave the shade.  一个慢悠悠的夏日午后过去了，没人愿意离开树荫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 languishing</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡwɪʃɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的，无精打采的</td><td>a languishing economy<br>萎靡不振的经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The languishing plants revived after a week of rain.  那些萎靡的植物在一周雨后复活了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 leaden</td><td>/ˈledən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉重的，无生气的；铅灰的</td><td>a leaden sky<br>铅灰色的天空</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He walked home with leaden steps after the painful defeat.  痛苦的失败后，他步履沉重地走回家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lifeless</td><td>/ˈlaɪfləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无生命的；无生气的</td><td>a lifeless performance<br>毫无生气的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The actors gave a lifeless performance to a bored audience.  演员们在无聊的观众前演得毫无生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 listless</td><td>/ˈlɪstləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无精打采的，倦怠的</td><td>feel listless and tired<br>感到倦怠疲乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fever left her listless for nearly a week.  发烧让她将近一周都无精打采。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 spiritless</del></td><td>/ˈspɪrɪtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无精打采的，没有生气的</td><td>a spiritless performance<br>毫无生气的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team gave a spiritless performance in the second half.  球队在下半场表现得毫无生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vapid</td><td>/ˈvæpɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣的，乏味的</td><td>a vapid conversation<br>乏味的谈话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party ended in vapid conversation about traffic and the weather.  聚会最后只剩下关于交通和天气的乏味闲谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spur</b></td><td>/spɜːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>刺激物</td><td>a spur to growth<br>增长的刺激因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising exports acted as a powerful spur to industrial growth.  出口增长成为工业发展的强大刺激因素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 boost</td><td>/ˈbuːst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>提高，促进；推动</td><td>boost sales<br>促进销售</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax cut is expected to boost consumer spending.  减税预计会促进消费者支出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 catalyst</td><td>/ˈkætələst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>促成因素；催化剂</td><td>a catalyst for change<br>变革的促成因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike became a catalyst for sweeping labor reform.  这次罢工成为全面劳工改革的促成因素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impetus</td><td>/ˈɪmpətəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>推动力，促进因素</td><td>give fresh impetus to<br>为……提供新动力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The grant gave fresh impetus to their stalled research.  这笔资助为他们停滞的研究提供了新动力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incentive</td><td>/ɪnˈsentɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>刺激，诱因</td><td>a tax incentive<br>税收上的激励</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tax breaks gave farmers a strong incentive to plant native trees.  税收减免给了农民种植本土树木的强烈诱因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incitement</td><td>/ɪnˈsaɪtmənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动，刺激因素</td><td>incitement to violence<br>对暴力的煽动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with incitement to riot after the speech.  演讲之后他被控煽动骚乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instigation</td><td>/ˌɪnstɪˈɡeɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动，教唆</td><td>at his instigation<br>在他的教唆下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike began at the instigation of two union leaders.  罢工是在两位工会领袖的煽动下开始的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 momentum</td><td>/moʊˈmentəm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>动力；势头</td><td>gather momentum<br>积聚势头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign gathered momentum after the debate and won three states easily.  这场竞选在辩论后积聚了势头，轻松赢下三个州。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 motivation</td><td>/ˌmoʊtəˈveɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>动机，动力</td><td>lack of motivation<br>缺乏动力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor pay explains the team's total lack of motivation.  报酬低解释了团队完全缺乏动力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provocation</td><td>/ˌprɑːvəˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>刺激，挑动；激怒</td><td>without any provocation<br>毫无缘由地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog bit him without the slightest provocation.  那狗毫无缘由就咬了他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stimulus</td><td>/ˈstɪmjələs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>刺激物，促进因素</td><td>an economic stimulus<br>经济刺激措施</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap loans acted as a stimulus to small businesses.  低息贷款成为小企业的刺激因素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arouse</td><td>/əˈraʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>唤起，激起；唤醒</td><td>arouse public suspicion<br>引起公众怀疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The missing files aroused suspicion among the auditors.  丢失的文件引起了审计人员的怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excite</td><td>/ɪkˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，引发；使兴奋</td><td>excite public interest<br>激起公众兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech excited strong resentment among the workers.  他的演讲激起了工人们的强烈不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exhort</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːrt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>敦促，力劝</td><td>exhort them to persevere<br>力劝他们坚持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speaker exhorted the students to persevere despite repeated failures.  演讲者勉励学生们即使屡遭失败也要坚持下去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 goad</td><td>/ɡoʊd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>刺激，驱使，激发</td><td>goad him into action<br>激他采取行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His teammates goaded him into accepting the ridiculous bet.  队友们激他接受了那个荒唐的赌约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instigate</td><td>/ˈɪnstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>煽动，激起</td><td>instigate a rebellion<br>煽动叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several officers were accused of helping to instigate the rebellion in the province.  几名军官被指控参与煽动该省的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prod</td><td>/prɑːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促使…行动</td><td>prod her into action<br>促使她行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her friends had to prod her into applying for the scholarship.  朋友们不得不促使她去申请那份奖学金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stir</td><td>/ˈstɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，煽动；搅拌</td><td>stir up anger<br>激起怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The article stirred up anger among local residents.  这篇文章激起了当地居民的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 urge</td><td>/ˈɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>催促，力劝；强烈欲望</td><td>urge him to resign<br>力劝他辞职</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her friends urged her to accept the job in Boston.  朋友们力劝她接受波士顿的那份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deterrent</td><td>/dɪˈtɜːrənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>遏制因素，威慑物</td><td>a strong deterrent<br>强有力的威慑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy fines act as a deterrent to careless drivers.  重罚对粗心司机起到威慑作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disincentive</td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈsentɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>抑制因素，负激励</td><td>a disincentive to invest<br>对投资的抑制因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 High taxes act as a disincentive to small businesses.  高税收对小企业起到抑制作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shackle</td><td>/ˈʃækl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>束缚；限制，阻碍</td><td>shackle free trade<br>束缚自由贸易</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New tariffs would shackle trade between the two neighboring countries.  新关税会束缚这两个邻国之间的贸易。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spurious</b></td><td>/ˈspjʊriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假的，伪造的</td><td>spurious claims<br>虚假的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The website spread spurious claims about the new vaccine.  该网站传播关于新疫苗的虚假说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apocryphal</td><td>/əˈpɑːkrɪfl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假的</td><td>an apocryphal story<br>一个虚假的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The story about his childhood is almost certainly apocryphal.  关于他童年的那个故事几乎肯定是假的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bogus</td><td>/ˈboʊɡəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>伪造的，假冒的</td><td>a bogus claim<br>虚假的索赔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police discovered a stack of bogus passports in the van.  警方在货车里发现一叠伪造护照。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 counterfeit</td><td>/ˈkaʊntərfɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>仿制的，假冒的；仿制</td><td>counterfeit banknotes<br>伪造的钞票</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police seized thousands of counterfeit banknotes hidden in the delivery van.  警方缴获了藏在送货车里的数千张假钞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fake</td><td>/ˈfeɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词、动词</td><td>假的，伪造的；伪造品</td><td>a fake diploma<br>假文凭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gallery unknowingly displayed a fake for almost ten years.  这家画廊不知情地展出了一件赝品近十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forged</td><td>/ˈfɔːrdʒd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>伪造的，假冒的</td><td>a forged signature<br>伪造的签名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bank noticed a forged signature on the withdrawal slip.  银行发现取款单上有伪造的签名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 phony</td><td>/ˈfoʊni/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>假的，冒充的；骗子</td><td>a phony accent<br>假装的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He used a phony name when renting the small apartment.  他租那间小公寓时用了假名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sham</td><td>/ʃæm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>掩饰，假装；虚假的</td><td>a sham marriage<br>假婚姻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The couple admitted their marriage was a sham arranged for the visa.  这对夫妻承认他们的婚姻是为签证安排的假婚姻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 authentic</td><td>/ɔːˈθentɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，非仿造的</td><td>authentic Italian cuisine<br>正宗的意大利菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum confirmed the letter was an authentic original.  博物馆确认这封信是真实的原件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bona fide</td><td>/ˌboʊnə ˈfaɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真诚的，真实的，善意的</td><td>a bona fide offer<br>真诚的报价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only bona fide students may borrow books from this library.  只有真正的在校学生才能从本馆借书。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genuine</td><td>/ˈdʒenjəwən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真正的，真实的；真诚的</td><td>genuine leather<br>真皮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Experts confirmed that the signature on the letter was genuine.  专家确认信上的签名是真的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 real</td><td>/ˈriːl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，真正的</td><td>a real event<br>真实事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film is based on a real event from 1943.  这部电影基于1943年的一件真实事件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spurn</b></td><td>/spɜːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>摈弃，拒绝</td><td>spurn an offer<br>断然拒绝提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spurned the generous offer without giving any explanation at all.  她断然拒绝了这个优厚的提议，且不作任何解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decline</td><td>/dɪˈklaɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝；下降，衰退</td><td>decline an invitation<br>谢绝邀请</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She politely declined the invitation to speak at the ceremony.  她礼貌地谢绝了在典礼上发言的邀请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disapprove</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈpruːv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>不赞成，反对</td><td>disapprove of the plan<br>不赞成该计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents strongly disapprove of the plan to drop out.  她父母强烈不赞成退学的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negative</td><td>/ˈneɡətɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>否决，反驳；负面的</td><td>negative the proposal<br>否决这项提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee negatived the motion after a short debate.  委员会经短暂辩论后否决了该动议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refuse</td><td>/rɪˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，不肯；废弃物</td><td>refuse to cooperate<br>拒绝合作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness refused to answer any further questions in court.  证人拒绝在法庭上回答任何进一步的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reject</td><td>/rɪˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，驳回；废品</td><td>reject the proposal<br>否决提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee voted to reject the proposal without further discussion.  委员会投票否决了该提案，不再讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprobate</td><td>/ˈreprəbeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>堕落者，道德败坏的人</td><td>an old reprobate<br>一个老无赖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel's hero is a charming reprobate with no money.  小说的主角是个迷人却身无分文的堕落者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repudiate</td><td>/rɪˈpjuːdieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝接受</td><td>repudiate a claim<br>否认一项主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author repudiated the views quoted in the article.  作者否认了文章中引用的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accede</td><td>/əkˈsiːd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>同意，答应；就任</td><td>accede to a request<br>答应请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government finally acceded to the union's demands.  政府最终同意了工会的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accept</td><td>/əkˈsept/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>接受，同意，承认</td><td>accept responsibility<br>承担责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She accepted the award on behalf of her entire research team.  她代表整个研究团队接受了这个奖项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agree</td><td>/əˈɡriː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>同意，赞同；一致</td><td>agree to the terms<br>同意这些条款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides finally agreed to the new safety rules.  双方最终同意了新的安全规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 approve</td><td>/əˈpruːv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>批准；赞成</td><td>approve the budget<br>批准预算</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee finally approved the plan after three long meetings.  委员会经过三次长会终于批准了该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assent</td><td>/əˈsent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>assent to the proposal<br>赞同该提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee assented to the proposal without further debate.  委员会未经进一步辩论就同意了该提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 consent</td><td>/kənˈsent/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>give one's consent<br>表示同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children cannot travel without their parents' consent.  没有父母的同意，孩子们不能出行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 embrace</td><td>/ɪmˈbreɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>乐于接受；拥抱</td><td>embrace change<br>乐于接受变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young workers quickly embraced the new technology.  年轻员工迅速接受了这项新技术。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sanity</b></td><td>/ˈsænəti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>心智健全，神志正常</td><td>question his sanity<br>质疑他的神志是否正常</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lawyers questioned his sanity at the time of the robbery.  他的律师质疑他在抢劫时神志是否正常。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reason</td><td>/ˈriːzən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>理由，原因；推理</td><td>a good reason for<br>…的充分理由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave no reason for leaving the meeting early.  他没有给出提前离开会议的理由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 saneness</del></td><td>/ˈseɪnnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>神志健全，明智</td><td>question his saneness<br>怀疑他神志是否健全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defense lawyer questioned the witness's saneness at the time.  辩护律师质疑证人当时的神志是否健全。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mind</td><td>/ˈmaɪnd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>留意，注意；介意</td><td>mind the step<br>留意台阶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please mind the gap between the train and the platform.  请注意列车与站台之间的空隙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dementia</td><td>/dɪˈmenʃiə/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>痴呆，精神错乱</td><td>suffer from dementia<br>患有痴呆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her grandmother has suffered from dementia for three years.  她祖母患痴呆症已三年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insanity</td><td>/ɪnˈsænəti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>精神失常，疯狂</td><td>a plea of insanity<br>精神失常的抗辩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lawyer entered a plea of insanity at trial.  他的律师在庭审时提出精神失常抗辩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 madness</td><td>/ˈmædnəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>愤怒；疯狂</td><td>in a fit of madness<br>一时怒火中烧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He broke the window in a fit of madness.  他一时怒火中烧砸破了窗子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mania</td><td>/ˈmeɪniə/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>热衷，狂热</td><td>a mania for collecting<br>对收藏的狂热</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her mania for collecting antique clocks filled every room of the old house.  她对收集古董钟表的狂热塞满了老房子的每个房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>squalid</b></td><td>/ˈskwɑːlɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>污秽的，肮脏的</td><td>squalid living conditions<br>肮脏的居住条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The refugees endured squalid living conditions in the overcrowded camp.  难民在过度拥挤的营地里忍受肮脏的居住条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 black</td><td>/ˈblæk/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>污黑的，肮脏的；黑色的</td><td>black with soot<br>被煤灰弄得污黑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The miners' hands were black with coal dust.  矿工的手被煤尘弄得污黑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dirty</td><td>/ˈdɜːrti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>肮脏的；卑鄙的</td><td>dirty windows<br>脏污的窗子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children came home with dirty hands and happy faces.  孩子们回家时手很脏，脸上却很开心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 filthy</td><td>/ˈfɪlθi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>污秽的，极脏的</td><td>filthy old clothes<br>污秽的旧衣服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kitchen floor was filthy after the long noisy party.  漫长喧闹的聚会后厨房地板脏得一塌糊涂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foul</td><td>/ˈfaʊl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>玷污（名誉）；弄脏</td><td>foul one's good name<br>玷污自己的好名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal fouled the good name he had spent decades building.  这桩丑闻玷污了他数十年积累的好名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impure</td><td>/ɪmˈpjʊr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不纯的，掺杂的</td><td>impure water<br>不洁的水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sample was impure and had to be discarded.  样本不纯，只能丢弃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nasty</td><td>/ˈnæsti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的，肮脏的；恶劣的</td><td>a nasty smell<br>难闻的气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A nasty stain spread slowly across the pale carpet.  一块难看的污渍在浅色地毯上慢慢蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slipshod</td><td>/ˈslɪpʃɑːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗心的，随意的</td><td>slipshod workmanship<br>马虎的做工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The inspector criticized the slipshod workmanship on the newly built bridge.  检查员批评新建桥梁马虎的做工。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slovenly</td><td>/ˈslʌvnli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>邋遢的，不整洁的</td><td>slovenly appearance<br>邋遢的外表</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His slovenly desk made a bad impression on every visitor.  他邋遢的办公桌给每位来访者留下坏印象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unclean</td><td>/ʌnˈkliːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不洁的，脏的</td><td>unclean drinking water<br>不洁的饮用水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many villagers still rely on unclean water from the river.  许多村民仍靠河里不洁的水生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clean</td><td>/ˈkliːn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>干净的；打扫，清洗</td><td>a clean white shirt<br>干净的白衬衫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Keep the wound clean and change the bandage every day.  保持伤口干净，每天更换绷带。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immaculate</td><td>/ɪˈmækjələt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>洁净无瑕的；完美无缺的</td><td>an immaculate record<br>毫无瑕疵的记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After thirty years of service his record remained absolutely immaculate.  服务三十年后，他的记录依然毫无瑕疵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spotless</td><td>/ˈspɑːtləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>一点污迹也没有的</td><td>a spotless kitchen<br>一尘不染的厨房</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her spotless kitchen shone under the bright evening light.  她一尘不染的厨房在明亮的夜灯下发亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unsoiled</del></td><td>/ˌʌnˈsɔɪld/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未被弄脏的，洁净的</td><td>unsoiled bandages<br>洁净未污的绷带</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only one unsoiled napkin remained by the end of dinner.  晚餐结束时只剩一张未弄脏的餐巾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unsullied</td><td>/ʌnˈsʌlid/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>未被玷污的，纯洁的</td><td>an unsullied reputation<br>未被玷污的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unsullied honesty made her the obvious choice for treasurer.  她未受玷污的诚实使她成为财务的当然人选。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>squall</b></td><td>/skwɔːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>尖叫</td><td>squall in pain<br>痛得尖叫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The frightened child squalled in pain until the nurse finally arrived.  受惊的孩子疼得尖叫，直到护士终于赶来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cry</td><td>/ˈkraɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>呼喊，恳求；哭</td><td>a cry for help<br>求助的呼声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers sent out a desperate cry for outside help.  村民们发出绝望的呼声请求外界援助。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 howl</td><td>/ˈhaʊl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>长嚎，怒吼</td><td>howl at the moon<br>对月长嚎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wolves howled somewhere beyond the frozen ridge that night.  那晚狼在冰封山脊外某处长嚎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 screech</td><td>/ˈskriːtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>尖叫，发出刺耳声</td><td>screech to a halt<br>尖啸着停下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The car screeched to a halt inches from the wall.  汽车尖啸着停在离墙几英寸处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shout</td><td>/ˈʃaʊt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>喊叫，大声说</td><td>shout at someone<br>对某人大喊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please don't shout; I can hear you perfectly well.  请别喊，我听得很清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shriek</td><td>/ˈʃriːk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>尖叫，尖声喊</td><td>shriek with laughter<br>尖声大笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children shrieked as the roller coaster plunged downward.  过山车俯冲时孩子们尖叫起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shrill</td><td>/ˈʃrɪl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>尖利的，刺耳的</td><td>a shrill whistle<br>刺耳的哨声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A shrill alarm woke everyone in the building at midnight.  刺耳的警报半夜惊醒了楼里所有人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squeal</td><td>/ˈskwiːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>尖声叫喊</td><td>squeal with delight<br>高兴得尖叫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children squealed with delight when the clown appeared.  小丑一出现孩子们就高兴地尖叫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thunder</td><td>/ˈθʌndər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>怒吼，大声喊；雷</td><td>thunder at the crowd<br>对人群怒吼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sergeant thundered orders across the parade ground.  中士在操场上怒吼着下达命令。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 yelp</td><td>/ˈjelp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>短促尖叫，吠叫</td><td>yelp in surprise<br>惊叫一声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy yelped when someone stepped on its tail.  有人踩到尾巴时小狗尖叫了一声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 yell</td><td>/ˈjel/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>大叫，叫喊</td><td>yell out a warning<br>大声发出警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He yelled a warning just before the branch fell down.  就在树枝落下前他大声警告了一声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 murmur</td><td>/ˈmɜːrmər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低声抱怨，发牢骚</td><td>murmur a complaint<br>低声抱怨一句</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers murmured about the new rules but nobody complained openly.  工人们对新规定低声抱怨，却没人公开抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mutter</td><td>/ˈmʌtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低声抱怨</td><td>mutter under one's breath<br>低声嘟囔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He muttered under his breath as he carried the heavy boxes upstairs.  他把沉重的箱子搬上楼时低声嘟囔着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 whisper</td><td>/ˈwɪspər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>低声说，耳语</td><td>whisper in her ear<br>在她耳边低语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She whispered the answer so the teacher would not hear.  她低声说出答案，以免老师听见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>squander</b></td><td>/ˈskwɑːndər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浪费</td><td>squander an opportunity<br>浪费机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not squander this opportunity to study abroad for a year.  不要浪费这次出国留学一年的机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blow</td><td>/ˈbloʊ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>挥霍，乱花钱</td><td>blow one's savings<br>挥霍掉积蓄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He will blow his entire bonus on a week in Tokyo.  他会把整笔奖金挥霍在东京的一周上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissipate</td><td>/ˈdɪsɪpeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>驱散，消散；浪费</td><td>dissipate the fog<br>驱散雾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The morning mist dissipated as soon as the sun rose.  太阳一升起，晨雾就消散了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fritter</td><td>/ˈfrɪtər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浪费，挥霍</td><td>fritter away the afternoon<br>把下午浪费掉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She frittered away the whole grant on minor equipment.  她把整笔资助挥霍在零碎设备上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lavish</td><td>/ˈlævɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>奢侈的，大量的，无节制的；挥霍，浪费</td><td>a lavish banquet<br>奢华的宴会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They hosted a lavish banquet for three hundred guests in the garden.  他们在花园里为三百位客人举办了一场奢华的宴会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 misspend</del></td><td>/mɪsˈspend/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>滥用，浪费</td><td>misspend public funds<br>滥用公共资金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The audit showed the agency had misspent millions.  审计显示该机构浪费了数百万。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 waste</td><td>/ˈweɪst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词、动词</td><td>荒芜的；浪费；废物</td><td>waste land<br>荒地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing grows on this waste ground beside the old factory.  旧工厂旁的这片荒地上什么也长不出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conserve</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>节约，保存，保护</td><td>conserve energy<br>节约能源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We must conserve water during the long summer drought.  漫长的夏季干旱期间我们必须节约用水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 husband</td><td>/ˈhʌzbənd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>节俭，勤俭持家</td><td>husband his resources<br>节省他的资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village learned to husband its water carefully through the dry months.  村民学会了在旱季小心节省用水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>squat</b></td><td>/skwɑːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>又矮又胖的</td><td>a squat stone tower<br>又矮又粗的石塔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A squat stone tower guarded the entrance to the ancient harbor.  一座又矮又粗的石塔守着古港入口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chunky</td><td>/ˈtʃʌŋki/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>矮胖结实的；厚块的</td><td>a chunky little man<br>矮胖的小个子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A chunky man in overalls opened the gate for us.  一个穿工装的矮胖男人为我们开了门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dumpy</td><td>/ˈdʌmpi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>矮胖的，粗短的</td><td>a dumpy figure<br>矮胖的身材</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hated how the coat made her look dumpy.  她讨厌这件外套让她显得矮胖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heavyset</td><td>/ˌheviˈset/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>体格粗壮的，矮壮的</td><td>a heavyset guard<br>粗壮的警卫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A heavyset guard blocked the narrow doorway.  一名粗壮的警卫堵住了狭窄的门口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squatty</td><td>/ˈskwɑːti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>又矮又胖的，粗矮的</td><td>a squatty clay jar<br>粗矮的陶罐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A squatty clay jar held the flowers on the table.  桌上一只粗矮的陶罐插着花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stocky</td><td>/ˈstɑːki/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>矮壮的，结实的</td><td>a stocky wrestler<br>矮壮的摔跤手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stocky wrestler lifted his opponent with ease.  这位矮壮的摔跤手轻松举起了对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stout</td><td>/ˈstaʊt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，勇敢的；粗壮的</td><td>stout resistance<br>顽强的抵抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The townspeople put up a stout defense against the raiders.  镇民对袭击者进行了顽强的抵抗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stubby</td><td>/ˈstʌbi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短粗的，矮胖的</td><td>stubby fingers<br>短粗的手指</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His stubby fingers moved surprisingly fast over the keys.  他短粗的手指在键上动得出奇地快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stumpy</td><td>/ˈstʌmpi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短粗的，矮胖的</td><td>a stumpy little dog<br>矮胖的小狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A stumpy little dog waddled ahead of its owner.  一只矮胖的小狗摇摆着走在主人前面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thickset</td><td>/ˌθɪkˈsɛt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>矮壮的，粗壮的</td><td>a thickset farmer<br>粗壮的农夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A thickset farmer carried the sack on one shoulder.  一个粗壮的农夫单肩扛起那袋东西。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 gangling</del></td><td>/ˈɡæŋɡlɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>瘦长笨拙的</td><td>a gangling teenager<br>瘦长笨拙的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A gangling teenager ducked under the low doorway.  一个瘦长笨拙的少年低头钻过矮门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lanky</td><td>/ˈlæŋki/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>瘦长的，细高的</td><td>a lanky basketball player<br>瘦高的篮球运动员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lanky player reached the shelf without a stool.  那位瘦高的球员不用凳子就够到了架子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>softpedal</b></del></td><td>/sɔːftˈpedl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>降低…的力度</td><td>softpedal the criticism<br>淡化批评的力度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials tried to softpedal the criticism in their public statements.  官员们试图在公开声明中淡化批评。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 de-emphasize</td><td>/diˈɛmfəsaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不再强调，减轻重视</td><td>de-emphasize test scores<br>淡化考试分数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new policy de-emphasizes test scores in admissions decisions.  新政策在录取决定中不再强调考试分数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 downplay</td><td>/ˌdaʊnˈpleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>轻描淡写，不予重视</td><td>downplay the significance<br>淡化其重要性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He downplayed his own role in the company's success.  他淡化了自己在公司成功中的作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 play down</td><td>/pleɪ ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>轻描淡写，淡化</td><td>play down the risks<br>淡化风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials tried to play down the scale of the leak.  官员们试图淡化这次泄漏的规模。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>squelch</b></td><td>/skweltʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压制，镇压（运动）</td><td>squelch the rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Troops moved in quickly to squelch the rebellion in the province.  军队迅速开进，镇压该省的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crush</td><td>/krʌʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压碎，碾碎</td><td>crush garlic with a knife<br>用刀把蒜压碎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He crushed the dry leaves into powder between his palms.  他用手掌把干叶子搓成了粉末。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muffle</td><td>/ˈmʌfl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压低声音，包裹使消声</td><td>muffle the sound<br>把声音压低</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick curtains muffle the sound of traffic outside the window.  厚窗帘减弱了窗外的车流声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quell</td><td>/kwel/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压制；使平静，使安静</td><td>quell the unrest<br>平息动乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Troops were sent in to quell the growing unrest.  部队被派去平息日益扩大的动乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repress</td><td>/rɪˈpres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>镇压；阻止（正常的表达、活动或发展等）</td><td>repress a smile<br>忍住笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She struggled to repress a smile during the solemn ceremony.  在庄严的仪式上她努力忍住笑意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squash</td><td>/ˈskwɑːʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>镇压，压制；压扁</td><td>squash the rumor<br>压下谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company quickly squashed the rumor about layoffs.  公司迅速压下了关于裁员的谣言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subdue</td><td>/səbˈduː/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使顺从；征服</td><td>subdue the rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Government forces moved south to subdue the rebellion within a week.  政府军南下，一周内镇压了叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suppress</td><td>/səˈpres/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>用暴力终止，镇压；封锁，删除</td><td>suppress a rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime used the army to suppress a rebellion in the capital.  该政权动用军队镇压首都的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crack down</td><td>/kræk ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>严厉打击，镇压</td><td>crack down on smuggling<br>严厉打击走私</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government promised to crack down on tax evasion.  政府承诺严厉打击逃税行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abet</td><td>/əˈbet/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，教唆（做坏事）</td><td>aid and abet<br>帮助并教唆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with aiding and abetting the robbery.  他被控协助并教唆抢劫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aid</td><td>/ˈeɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>帮助，援助</td><td>come to someone's aid<br>前来帮助某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors came to her aid within minutes of the fire.  火灾发生几分钟内邻居们就来帮忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assist</td><td>/əˈsɪst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，协助</td><td>assist with the work<br>协助工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two nurses assisted the surgeon during the operation.  两名护士在手术中协助医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 back</td><td>/ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，资助</td><td>back a candidate<br>支持某位候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several investors agreed to back her new company.  几位投资人同意资助她的新公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 help</td><td>/ˈhelp/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>帮助，助益</td><td>a great help<br>很大的帮助</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your advice was a great help during the difficult week.  在那困难的一周里你的建议帮了大忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 support</td><td>/səˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，支撑</td><td>support a proposal<br>支持一项提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most members voted to support the new proposal.  大多数成员投票支持这项新提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prop up</td><td>/prɑːp ˈʌp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>支撑，扶持</td><td>prop up the failing bank<br>扶持濒危银行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government spent billions to prop up the failing bank.  政府花了数十亿来扶持这家濒临倒闭的银行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>secede</b></td><td>/sɪˈsiːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>脱离，退出（组织、团体、联盟等）</td><td>secede from the union<br>脱离联盟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two provinces threatened to secede from the federation over the new tax.  两个省威胁因新税而脱离联邦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 demit</del></td><td>/dɪˈmɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>辞职，退出</td><td>demit from the society<br>退出该协会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He chose to demit from the club rather than apologize.  他宁愿退出俱乐部也不道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disaffiliate</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈfɪlieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>脱离，解除隶属关系</td><td>disaffiliate from the party<br>脱离该党派</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several branches voted to disaffiliate from the national body.  几个分部投票决定脱离全国组织。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quit</td><td>/ˈkwɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>退出，辞去；停止</td><td>quit the organization<br>退出该组织</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She quit the committee after the second disagreement.  第二次分歧之后她退出了委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 withdraw</td><td>/wɪðˈdrɔː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>取出（款项）；撤回，撤退</td><td>withdraw money from the bank<br>从银行取钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stopped at the bank to withdraw money before the long trip.  他在长途旅行前去银行取钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enlist</td><td>/ɪnˈlɪst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>入伍，加入；争取</td><td>enlist in the army<br>参军入伍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He enlisted in the navy at the age of eighteen.  他十八岁时加入了海军。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enroll</td><td>/ɪnˈroʊl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>注册，报名入学</td><td>enroll in a course<br>报名参加课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She enrolled in evening classes to finish her degree.  她报名上夜校以完成学位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sign up</td><td>/ˈsaɪn ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>签约聘用；报名参加</td><td>sign up a new player<br>签下一名新球员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The club signed up three young goalkeepers last month.  俱乐部上个月签下了三名年轻门将。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stalwart</b></td><td>/ˈstɔːlwərt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定忠诚的；强壮的</td><td>a stalwart supporter<br>坚定的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained a stalwart supporter of the project through every setback.  历经挫折，她始终是这个项目的坚定支持者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adamant</td><td>/ˈædəmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，不可动摇的</td><td>adamant about the rules<br>对规则毫不通融</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach was adamant that no player would skip practice.  教练坚决要求没有球员可以缺席训练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstinate</td><td>/ˈɑːbstɪnət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的</td><td>an obstinate refusal<br>固执的拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her obstinate refusal to compromise delayed the agreement by several months.  她固执地拒绝妥协，使协议推迟了几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbending</td><td>/ʌnˈbendɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不亲切的；不妥协的</td><td>an unbending attitude<br>不肯通融的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old teacher was unbending about rules and manners.  这位老教师在规矩和礼节上毫不通融。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 willful</td><td>/ˈwɪlfl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，倔强的</td><td>willful disobedience<br>故意的违抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His willful refusal to ask for help delayed the repair by weeks.  他固执地拒绝求助，使维修推迟了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stamina</b></td><td>/ˈstæmɪnə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>耐力</td><td>build up stamina<br>增强耐力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long morning swims helped him build up remarkable stamina for racing.  长时间的晨泳帮他练出比赛所需的惊人耐力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endurance</td><td>/ɪnˈdʊrəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>忍耐力，耐久力</td><td>physical endurance<br>身体的耐力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long runs slowly built up her endurance over the winter.  长跑在整个冬天慢慢提高了她的耐力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tolerance</td><td>/ˈtɑːlərəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>容忍，宽容</td><td>tolerance for dissent<br>对异见的容忍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school shows little tolerance for cheating of any kind.  学校对任何作弊都毫不容忍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frailty</td><td>/ˈfreɪlti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>弱点；脆弱</td><td>human frailty<br>人性的弱点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel explores human frailty with unusual tenderness.  这部小说以罕见的温柔探讨人性的弱点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stammer</b></td><td>/ˈstæmər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>口吃，结巴</td><td>stammer out an apology<br>结结巴巴地道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nervous boy stammered out an apology and hurried from the room.  紧张的男孩结结巴巴地道歉，然后匆匆离开房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 falter</td><td>/ˈfɔːltər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>蹒跚；犹豫，踌躇</td><td>falter in his resolve<br>决心动摇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Mr. Garrison never faltered in his resolve to finish the project.  加里森先生完成这个项目的决心从未动摇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stutter</td><td>/ˈstʌtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>口吃，结巴地说</td><td>stutter over the words<br>说话时结巴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stuttered badly whenever he had to speak publicly.  每次必须公开讲话时他都严重结巴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 articulate</td><td>/ɑːrˈtɪkjuleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>清晰地表达；表达清晰的</td><td>articulate her concerns<br>清晰表达她的顾虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She managed to articulate her concerns clearly at the board meeting.  她在董事会上清晰地表达了自己的顾虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>startle</b></td><td>/ˈstɑːrtl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使吓一跳，使大吃一惊</td><td>startle the sleeping cat<br>惊动睡着的猫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sudden noise from the kitchen startled the sleeping cat awake.  厨房突然的声响惊醒了睡着的猫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amaze</td><td>/əˈmeɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使惊奇，使大为惊讶</td><td>amaze the audience<br>使观众惊叹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist amazed everyone with her memory.  这位年轻钢琴家的记忆力令所有人惊叹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 astonish</td><td>/əˈstɑːnɪʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使惊讶，使震惊</td><td>astonish his critics<br>令批评者惊讶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The result astonished even the most optimistic forecasters.  这个结果连最乐观的预测者都感到惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 astound</td><td>/əˈstaʊnd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使震惊，使目瞪口呆</td><td>astound the experts<br>使专家们震惊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His confession astounded the officers questioning him.  他的供认让审问他的警官们震惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 dumbfound</del></td><td>/dʌmˈfaʊnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使惊呆，使目瞪口呆</td><td>dumbfound the whole room<br>使满屋人惊呆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sudden resignation dumbfounded the entire department.  她突然辞职让整个部门惊呆了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rock</td><td>/ˈrɑːk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使震惊，动摇；摇动</td><td>rock the nation<br>震动全国</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal rocked the party just before the election.  这桩丑闻在选举前震动了该党。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scare</td><td>/ˈsker/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使害怕，惊吓</td><td>scare the children<br>吓到孩子们</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden thunder scared the children out of bed.  突然的雷声把孩子们从床上惊起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shock</td><td>/ˈʃɑːk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使震惊，使惊愕</td><td>shock the public<br>震惊公众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photographs shocked readers across the country.  这些照片震惊了全国的读者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stun</td><td>/ˈstʌn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使震惊；把…打昏</td><td>stun the audience<br>使观众震惊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The verdict stunned everyone waiting outside the courtroom.  判决让在法庭外等候的人都震惊了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stupefy</td><td>/ˈstuːpəfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使惊呆，使麻木</td><td>stupefy the listeners<br>使听众惊呆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sheer scale of the fraud stupefied investigators.  这起欺诈的规模之大让调查人员惊呆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
