<tr><td>↳反 reassure</td><td>/ˌriːəˈʃʊr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使安心，打消疑虑</td><td>reassure worried parents<br>安抚焦虑的父母</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The principal tried to reassure worried parents after the accident.  事故之后，校长努力安抚焦虑的父母。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>static</b></td><td>/ˈstætɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>静态的；停滞的</td><td>a static economy<br>停滞的经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wages have remained static while the cost of housing keeps rising.  房价不断上涨，工资却一直停滞不动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inactive</td><td>/ɪnˈæktɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不活动的，不活跃的</td><td>remain inactive for months<br>数月毫无动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The volcano has been inactive for over two hundred years.  这座火山已有两百多年没有活动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fixed</td><td>/ˈfɪkst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固定的，不变的</td><td>a fixed monthly rate<br>固定的月费率</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tenants pay a fixed rent for the first three years.  租户前三年支付固定租金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immobile</td><td>/ɪˈmoʊbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不动的，固定的</td><td>remain immobile<br>保持不动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The injured hiker lay immobile until help arrived.  受伤的徒步者一动不动地躺着直到救援到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 immotile</del></td><td>/ɪˈmoʊtəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不能运动的，静止的</td><td>immotile cells<br>不能运动的细胞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sample contained mostly immotile cells under the microscope.  显微镜下样本中多为不能运动的细胞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immovable</td><td>/ɪˈmuːvəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的；不可动摇的</td><td>immovable on the point<br>在此点上不可动摇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was immovable on the question of the deadline.  在截止日期问题上他毫不动摇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inert</td><td>/ɪˈnɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（人）懒惰缺乏活力的；（物品）惰性的</td><td>an inert gas<br>惰性气体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Helium is an inert gas, so it rarely reacts with other elements.  氦是一种惰性气体，因此很少与其他元素发生反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stable</td><td>/ˈsteɪbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>稳定的；马厩</td><td>a stable income<br>稳定的收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His condition has been stable since the operation last Tuesday.  自上周二手术以来他的病情一直稳定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stagnant</td><td>/ˈstæɡnənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>停滞不前的</td><td>a stagnant economy<br>停滞的经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The economy stayed stagnant for a decade despite repeated government reforms.  尽管政府反复改革，经济十年停滞不前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stationary</td><td>/ˈsteɪʃəˌneri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>静止的，固定不动的</td><td>remain stationary<br>保持静止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic on the bridge stayed stationary for almost two hours.  桥上的车流几乎两小时一动不动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 still</td><td>/ˈstɪl/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>寂静无声的；寂静</td><td>the still of night<br>夜晚的寂静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The air was still and the lake looked like glass.  空气静止无风，湖面像玻璃一样。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 active</td><td>/ˈæktɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活跃的，活动的</td><td>an active volcano<br>活火山</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stays active by swimming every morning.  她每天早上游泳以保持活跃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dynamic</td><td>/daɪˈnæmɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有活力的，动态的</td><td>a dynamic leader<br>有活力的领导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city has a dynamic arts scene that keeps changing.  这座城市有着不断变化的活跃艺术圈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lively</td><td>/ˈlaɪvli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>活跃的，生气勃勃的</td><td>a lively debate<br>热烈的辩论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A lively debate broke out over the proposed budget.  围绕拟定预算爆发了一场热烈的辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vibrant</td><td>/ˈvaɪbrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的；鲜艳的</td><td>a vibrant city<br>一座充满活力的城市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The neighborhood became a vibrant hub for young artists.  这个街区成了年轻艺术家的活力中心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stature</b></td><td>/ˈstætʃər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>高度，身高；才干，水平</td><td>a scholar of great stature<br>水平极高的学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a scholar of great stature in the field of linguistics.  他是语言学界水平极高的学者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 altitude</td><td>/ˈæltəˌtuːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>海拔，高度</td><td>at high altitude<br>在高海拔处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Breathing becomes difficult at an altitude above four thousand meters.  在四千米以上的海拔，呼吸会变得困难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elevation</td><td>/ˌeləˈveɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>高度，海拔；提升</td><td>an elevation of 2000 meters<br>两千米的高度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village sits at an elevation of two thousand meters.  这个村子位于海拔两千米处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inch</td><td>/ɪntʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）慢慢移动</td><td>inch forward slowly<br>缓慢向前挪动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heavy truck had to inch forward slowly across the snowy bridge.  那辆重卡只能缓慢地挪过积雪覆盖的桥梁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 merit</td><td>/ˈmerət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>优点，价值</td><td>the merits of the plan<br>该方案的优点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee debated the merits of the plan for hours.  委员会就该方案的优点讨论了数小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quality</td><td>/ˈkwɑːləti/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>品质，水平；特性</td><td>work of high quality<br>高水平的作品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judges praised the quality of her early work.  评委称赞了她早期作品的水准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 value</td><td>/ˈvæljuː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>价值，重要性</td><td>of little value<br>价值不大</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old coin turned out to be of great value.  那枚旧硬币结果很有价值。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 virtue</td><td>/ˈvɜːrtʃu/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>美德，德行；优点</td><td>a life of virtue<br>有德行的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patience is a virtue that few travelers keep at airports.  耐心是少有旅客能在机场保持的美德。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worth</td><td>/ˈwɜːrθ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>价值，才干；值得的</td><td>prove one's worth<br>证明自己的才干</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young engineer soon proved his worth to the firm.  这位年轻工程师很快向公司证明了自己的才干。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>steadfast</b></td><td>/ˈstedfæst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，忠诚的</td><td>steadfast in his belief<br>信念坚定不移</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained steadfast in her belief despite years of public ridicule.  尽管多年遭受公众嘲笑，她依然坚定地坚持自己的信念。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 constant</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不变的，恒定的；忠诚的</td><td>a constant friend<br>忠诚不变的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her support remained constant through every difficult year.  她的支持在每个艰难年头都不曾改变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dedicated</td><td>/ˈdedəkeɪtəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>献身的，尽心的；专用的</td><td>a dedicated teacher<br>尽心尽力的教师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is dedicated to protecting the birds of this marsh.  她致力于保护这片沼泽的鸟类。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devoted</td><td>/dɪˈvoʊtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>投入的；忠诚的</td><td>a devoted follower<br>忠诚的追随者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is a devoted teacher who remembers every student by name.  她是位投入的教师，能叫出每个学生的名字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devout</td><td>/dɪˈvaʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虔诚的，笃信的</td><td>a devout believer<br>虔诚的信徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His devout aunt attended the little chapel every single morning.  他虔诚的姑妈每天早晨都去那间小教堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loyal</td><td>/ˈlɔɪəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忠诚的，忠实的</td><td>a loyal customer<br>忠实的客户</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained loyal to the team even during its worst season.  即使在最糟的赛季，他仍然忠于这支球队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pious</td><td>/ˈpaɪəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（信仰上）虔诚的</td><td>a pious old woman<br>虔诚的老妇人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pious old woman attended the early service every single morning.  那位虔诚的老妇人每天早上都参加早祷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 staunch</td><td>/stɔːntʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忠诚的，坚定的</td><td>a staunch supporter<br>坚定的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A staunch supporter of reform, she never wavered under public pressure.  作为改革的坚定支持者，她从未在公众压力下动摇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 steady</td><td>/ˈstedi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稳定的，持续不变的</td><td>a steady stream of visitors<br>络绎不绝的来访者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales have shown steady growth for eight straight quarters.  销售额已连续八个季度稳定增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 true-blue</td><td>/ˌtruː ˈbluː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>忠诚的，坚定不移的</td><td>a true-blue supporter<br>忠诚的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained a true-blue supporter through every losing season.  每个失利的赛季他都是忠诚的支持者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disloyal</td><td>/dɪˈslɔɪəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不忠的，背信的</td><td>disloyal to a friend<br>对朋友不忠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sharing those emails was disloyal to everyone on the team.  分享那些邮件是对团队所有人的背叛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perfidious</td><td>/pərˈfɪdiəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>背信弃义的，奸诈的</td><td>a perfidious friend<br>背信弃义的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 History remembers him as a perfidious partner in every treaty.  历史记住他是每份条约中背信弃义的伙伴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 recreant</del></td><td>/ˈrɛkriənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>背叛者，怯懦的叛徒</td><td>branded a recreant<br>被打上叛徒烙印</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ballad calls him a recreant who fled the battle.  这首民谣称他是逃离战场的怯懦叛徒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 traitorous</td><td>/ˈtreɪtərəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>叛国的，背叛的</td><td>traitorous conduct<br>背叛的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was executed for traitorous dealings with the enemy.  这名军官因与敌人的叛国交易被处死。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 treacherous</td><td>/ˈtretʃərəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>背信弃义的；（路况等）危险的</td><td>a treacherous ally<br>背信弃义的盟友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A treacherous adviser sold the king's battle plans to the enemy.  一个背信弃义的谋士把国王的作战计划卖给了敌人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfaithful</td><td>/ʌnˈfeɪθfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不忠的，不忠实的</td><td>an unfaithful servant<br>不忠的仆人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He proved unfaithful to the promises he had already signed.  他背弃了自己已签下的承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stealth</b></td><td>/stelθ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐形的；秘密行动的</td><td>a stealth attack<br>秘密的袭击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The base was destroyed by a stealth attack just before dawn.  基地在黎明前被一次隐形突袭摧毁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 backstairs</td><td>/ˈbækˌsterz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>秘密的，暗中进行的</td><td>backstairs negotiations<br>暗中的谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The deal was arranged through backstairs negotiations.  这笔交易是通过暗中谈判安排的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clandestine</td><td>/klænˈdestɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>秘密的，暗中的</td><td>a clandestine meeting<br>秘密的会面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They arranged a clandestine meeting in an empty parking garage.  他们在一个空停车场安排了秘密会面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 covert</td><td>/ˈkoʊvərt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐蔽的，秘密的</td><td>covert operations<br>秘密行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The agency ran covert operations in several neighboring countries.  该机构在数个邻国进行秘密行动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 furtive</td><td>/ˈfɜːrtɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>鬼鬼祟祟的；秘密的</td><td>a furtive glance<br>偷偷的一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cast a furtive glance at the answers on her desk.  他偷偷瞥了一眼她桌上的答案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 private</td><td>/ˈpraɪvət/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>私下的，秘密的</td><td>a private meeting<br>私下的会面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They held a private meeting before the public hearing.  他们在公开听证会前举行了私下会面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sneak</td><td>/ˈsniːk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>偷偷地走，溜</td><td>sneak into the room<br>偷偷溜进房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children sneaked out of the house after midnight.  孩子们午夜后偷偷溜出了房子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stealthy</td><td>/ˈstelθi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐秘的，偷偷的</td><td>a stealthy approach<br>隐秘的接近</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cat made a stealthy approach toward the sleeping bird.  猫悄悄地靠近那只睡着的鸟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surreptitious</td><td>/ˌsɜːrəpˈtɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偷偷摸摸的，保密的</td><td>a surreptitious glance<br>偷偷的一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a surreptitious glance at his watch during the meeting.  会上他偷偷看了一眼手表。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undercover</td><td>/ˌʌndərˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>秘密的，暗中的</td><td>an undercover agent<br>卧底特工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An undercover officer spent two years inside the smuggling ring.  一名卧底警官在走私团伙里待了两年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 underground</td><td>/ˈʌndərɡraʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>秘密的；地下的</td><td>an underground movement<br>地下运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The group published an underground newspaper during the occupation.  该团体在占领期间出版了一份地下报纸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 open</td><td>/ˈoʊpən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>公开的，坦率的</td><td>open opposition<br>公开的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was open opposition to the plan at the meeting.  会上有人公开反对这项计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 overt</td><td>/oʊˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，公开的</td><td>overt hostility<br>公开的敌意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The policy met overt hostility from local business owners.  该政策遭到当地商户的公开敌视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 public</td><td>/ˈpʌblɪk/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>公开的；公众的</td><td>make it public<br>予以公开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company made its findings public earlier this month.  公司本月早些时候公开了调查结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>steep</b></td><td>/stiːp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>浸泡，浸渍</td><td>steep tea leaves in hot water<br>把茶叶泡在热水里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Steep the dried leaves in boiling water for about ten minutes.  把干叶子在沸水里浸泡约十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abrupt</td><td>/əˈbrʌpt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>突然的；言语生硬的</td><td>an abrupt reply<br>生硬的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His abrupt answer ended the conversation before it really began.  他生硬的回答让谈话还没开始就结束了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 precipitate</td><td>/prɪˈsɪpɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>匆忙的；促使，导致</td><td>a precipitate departure<br>匆忙的离去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His precipitate resignation surprised colleagues who expected a much longer transition.  他匆忙的辞职让原以为过渡期会长得多的同事大吃一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sheer</td><td>/ˈʃɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全的，纯粹的</td><td>sheer nonsense<br>纯粹的胡说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What he wrote about the treaty was sheer nonsense.  他关于那份条约写的全是胡说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exorbitant</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːrbɪtənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度的</td><td>exorbitant prices<br>过高的价格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tourists complained about exorbitant prices for bottled water near the temple.  游客抱怨寺庙附近瓶装水价格过高。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extravagant</td><td>/ɪkˈstrævəɡənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挥霍的；过度的，不必要的</td><td>extravagant spending<br>挥霍的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their extravagant spending on parties left the charity nearly broke.  他们在宴会上的挥霍开支让这家慈善机构几乎破产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extreme</td><td>/ɪkˈstriːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>极端的，极度的；极端</td><td>extreme views<br>极端的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His extreme views cost him most of his old friends.  他极端的观点让他失去了大部分老朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immoderate</td><td>/ɪˈmɑdərət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无节制的，过度的</td><td>immoderate drinking<br>无节制的饮酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her immoderate praise made the young writer distinctly uneasy.  她过度的赞美让这位年轻作家明显不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inordinate</td><td>/ɪnˈɔːrdənət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度的，过分的</td><td>an inordinate amount of time<br>过多的时间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spends an inordinate amount of time arranging his desk.  他花过多时间整理书桌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insane</td><td>/ɪnˈseɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒唐的，疯狂的</td><td>an insane plan<br>疯狂的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Driving in this thick fog would be insane.  在这种大雾里开车太疯狂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lavish</td><td>/ˈlævɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>奢侈的，大量的，无节制的；挥霍，浪费</td><td>a lavish banquet<br>奢华的宴会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They hosted a lavish banquet for three hundred guests in the garden.  他们在花园里为三百位客人举办了一场奢华的宴会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overdue</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈduː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过期的，逾期的；早该发生的</td><td>an overdue payment<br>逾期的付款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library fined her for three books that were badly overdue.  图书馆因三本严重逾期的书对她罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overweening</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈwiːnɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，专横的</td><td>overweening pride<br>过度的骄傲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His overweening confidence led him to ignore every warning from the engineers.  他过度的自信使他忽视了工程师的每一次警告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 plethoric</del></td><td>/pləˈθɔrɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过多的，过剩的</td><td>a plethoric supply<br>过剩的供应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market suffers from a plethoric supply of cheap steel.  市场因廉价钢材供应过剩而受困。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stiff</td><td>/stɪf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵硬的，无法弯曲的；艰苦的，费力的</td><td>a stiff leather collar<br>硬挺的皮质衣领</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stiff leather collar rubbed his neck raw during the long march.  长途行军中，硬挺的皮领把他的脖子磨破了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gradual</td><td>/ˈɡrædʒuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平缓的，渐进的</td><td>a gradual slope<br>平缓的坡道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path rises in a gradual slope toward the temple.  小路以平缓的坡度向寺庙升高。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 moderate</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>缓和，减轻；适度的</td><td>moderate one's demands<br>降低自己的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He agreed to moderate his tone during the debate.  他同意在辩论中缓和语气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 modest</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谦虚的；不大的，适度的</td><td>modest about her success<br>对成功很谦虚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed modest about his award and thanked his team.  他对自己的奖项很谦虚，还感谢了团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reasonable</td><td>/ˈriːzənəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合理的，通情达理的</td><td>a reasonable request<br>合理的请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That seems like a perfectly reasonable request.  那似乎是完全合理的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 temperate</td><td>/ˈtempərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（言行举止）有分寸的，有节制的</td><td>a temperate response<br>有分寸的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a temperate reply to a very rude question.  面对无礼的问题她的回答很有分寸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stench</b></td><td>/stentʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>臭气，恶臭</td><td>an unbearable stench<br>难以忍受的恶臭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An unbearable stench rose from the drains during the hot afternoon.  炎热的午后，下水道里升起难以忍受的恶臭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 fetor</del></td><td>/ˈfiːtər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恶臭，臭气</td><td>the fetor of decay<br>腐烂的恶臭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sickening fetor rose from the neglected pond.  一股恶心的臭气从无人管的池塘升起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 funk</td><td>/ˈfʌŋk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>臭气；恐惧</td><td>the funk of stale smoke<br>陈烟的臭气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The basement carried the funk of damp and old paint.  地下室弥漫着潮气和旧漆的臭味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 malodor</del></td><td>/mæˈloʊdər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恶臭，臭气</td><td>the malodor of stale grease<br>陈腐油脂的恶臭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A faint malodor drifted from the drain behind the restaurant.  餐馆后面的下水道飘来一股淡淡的恶臭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reek</td><td>/ˈriːk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>恶臭；发出臭味</td><td>the reek of fish<br>鱼的腥臭味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kitchen reeked of burnt oil all evening.  厨房整晚都散发着油烧焦的臭味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stink</td><td>/ˈstɪŋk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>大声抗议，抱怨；臭味</td><td>raise a stink about it<br>为此大吵大闹抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parents raised a stink about the sudden tuition increase.  家长们为学费突然上涨大闹抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aroma</td><td>/əˈroʊmə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>（尤指食物的）香气</td><td>the aroma of fresh coffee<br>新鲜咖啡的香气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The aroma of baking bread drew customers into the little shop.  烤面包的香气把顾客引进了这家小店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fragrance</td><td>/ˈfreɪɡrəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>香味，芬芳</td><td>the fragrance of fresh lilacs<br>新鲜紫丁香的芬芳</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fragrance of jasmine filled the courtyard every evening.  每天傍晚，茉莉的香味充满庭院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perfume</td><td>/ˈpɜːrfjuːm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>香水，芳香</td><td>the perfume of roses<br>玫瑰的芳香</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The perfume of roses filled the small courtyard.  玫瑰的芳香充满了小院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scent</td><td>/ˈsent/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>香味，气味</td><td>the scent of pine<br>松树的香气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scent of pine hung in the cool morning air.  松香在清冷的晨气中弥漫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stentorian</b></td><td>/stenˈtɔːriən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音洪亮的</td><td>a stentorian voice<br>洪亮的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His stentorian voice carried easily to the back of the hall.  他洪亮的嗓音轻松传到大厅后排。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blaring</td><td>/ˈblerɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>响亮刺耳的，轰鸣的</td><td>blaring sirens<br>刺耳的警笛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A blaring horn woke everyone in the street.  刺耳的喇叭声惊醒了街上所有人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blasting</td><td>/ˈblæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>震耳的，轰响的</td><td>blasting music<br>震耳的音乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Blasting music from the next flat kept me awake.  隔壁公寓震耳的音乐让我无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clamorous</td><td>/ˈklæmərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喧闹的；大声要求的</td><td>clamorous demands for reform<br>高声要求改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clamorous market made quiet conversation impossible.  喧闹的市场让人根本无法安静交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deafening</td><td>/ˈdefənɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>震耳欲聋的</td><td>deafening applause<br>雷鸣般的掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd's cheer was deafening as the winner appeared.  获胜者出现时人群的欢呼震耳欲聋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 earsplitting</del></td><td>/ˈɪrˌsplɪtɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>震耳欲聋的</td><td>an earsplitting shriek<br>震耳的尖叫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An earsplitting shriek from the whistle stopped every conversation on the platform.  哨子发出的震耳尖啸让站台上所有的交谈都停了下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 plangent</del></td><td>/ˈplændʒənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（声音）响亮而哀伤的</td><td>a plangent cry<br>哀伤的呼声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plangent cry of a gull echoed across the empty beach.  海鸥凄凉的叫声在空荡的海滩上回响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resounding</td><td>/rɪˈzaʊndɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>响亮的，回响的；巨大的</td><td>a resounding crash<br>响亮的撞击声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The door shut with a resounding bang behind him.  门在他身后砰然作响地关上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 roaring</td><td>/ˈrɔːrɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>咆哮的，轰鸣的</td><td>a roaring engine<br>轰鸣的引擎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We could hardly talk over the roaring waterfall.  在轰鸣的瀑布声中我们几乎无法交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sonorous</td><td>/ˈsɑːnərəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音洪亮的</td><td>a sonorous bell<br>一口洪亮的钟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The priest read the passage in a slow, sonorous voice.  神父用缓慢洪亮的声音朗读那段经文。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thunderous</td><td>/ˈθʌndərəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>雷鸣般的，轰响的</td><td>thunderous applause<br>雷鸣般的掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The performance ended in thunderous applause.  演出在雷鸣般的掌声中结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gentle</td><td>/ˈdʒentəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温和的，轻柔的</td><td>a gentle voice<br>温柔的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave the door a gentle push and stepped inside.  他轻轻推了一下门，走了进去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 low</td><td>/ˈloʊ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>低落的，沮丧的；低的</td><td>feel a bit low<br>感觉有点低落</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has been feeling low since her closest friend moved away.  自从最好的朋友搬走，她一直情绪低落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soft</td><td>/ˈsɑːft/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔和的，轻微的</td><td>a soft voice<br>轻柔的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a soft tone so the baby would not wake.  她轻声说话，以免把婴儿弄醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sterile</b></td><td>/ˈsterəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫瘠的；无菌的</td><td>sterile medical instruments<br>无菌医疗器械</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Surgeons must use sterile instruments to prevent infection during any operation.  外科医生必须使用无菌器械，以防手术中感染。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 barren</td><td>/ˈbærən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫瘠的；无成果的</td><td>barren land<br>贫瘠的土地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing grows on the barren land beyond the last village.  最后一个村子之外的贫瘠土地上什么都长不出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 effete</td><td>/ɪˈfiːt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的，衰落的</td><td>an effete aristocracy<br>衰落的贵族阶层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An effete aristocracy clung to its titles long after the wealth had vanished.  衰落的贵族阶层在财富早已消失后仍死守头衔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fruitless</td><td>/ˈfruːtləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无成效的，白费的</td><td>a fruitless search<br>毫无结果的搜寻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks of fruitless negotiation left both sides exhausted.  数周毫无结果的谈判让双方都疲惫不堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impotent</td><td>/ˈɪmpətənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无力的，无能的</td><td>impotent rage<br>无能的愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without funding the committee was impotent to change anything at all.  没有资金，委员会无力改变任何事情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infertile</td><td>/ɪnˈfɜːrtəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫瘠的；不育的</td><td>infertile ground<br>贫瘠的土地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of drought left the ground almost completely infertile.  多年干旱使土地几乎完全贫瘠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aseptic</td><td>/ˌeɪˈseptɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>消毒的，无菌的</td><td>aseptic surgical technique<br>无菌手术操作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Surgeons follow strict aseptic procedure before touching any instrument.  外科医生在接触任何器械前都遵守严格的无菌程序。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 germfree</del></td><td>/ˈdʒɜrmfri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无菌的，无病菌的</td><td>a germfree environment<br>无菌环境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lab maintains a germfree environment for breeding these mice.  实验室为饲养这些小鼠保持无菌环境。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hygienic</td><td>/haɪˈdʒiːnɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>卫生的，清洁的</td><td>hygienic food handling<br>卫生的食品操作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hospitals require hygienic practices in every ward.  医院要求每个病区都遵守卫生规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 verdant</td><td>/ˈvɜːrdnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（因长满植物而）翠绿的，郁郁葱葱的</td><td>verdant hills<br>翠绿的山丘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the rains the verdant valley filled with wildflowers.  雨后，翠绿的山谷开满了野花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insanitary</td><td>/ɪnˈsænəteri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不卫生的，肮脏的</td><td>insanitary living conditions<br>不卫生的居住条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Inspectors closed the kitchen for insanitary conditions.  检查员因卫生条件恶劣关闭了厨房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unhygienic</td><td>/ˌʌnhaɪˈdʒɛnɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不卫生的，有害健康的</td><td>unhygienic food handling<br>不卫生的食品操作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Storing raw meat that way is plainly unhygienic.  那样存放生肉显然不卫生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stickler</b></td><td>/ˈstɪklər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>坚持细节的人，一丝不苟的人</td><td>a stickler for rules<br>死守规则的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our new manager is a stickler for rules about punctuality.  我们的新经理在守时规则上一丝不苟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disciplinarian</td><td>/ˌdɪsɪplɪˈneriən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严格执行纪律的人</td><td>a strict disciplinarian<br>严厉守纪律的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their grandfather was a strict disciplinarian about bedtime.  他们的祖父在睡觉时间上极其严格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pedant</td><td>/ˈpedənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>书呆子，墨守成规之人</td><td>a fussy old pedant<br>一个爱挑剔的老书呆子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a pedant would object to ending a sentence with a preposition.  只有墨守成规之人才会反对句子以介词结尾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 purist</td><td>/ˈpjʊrɪst/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>纯粹主义者，拘守传统的人</td><td>a language purist<br>语言纯正主义者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Purists complain that the new translation is far too casual.  纯粹主义者抱怨新译本太随意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 martinet</td><td>/ˌmɑːrtnˈet/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>纪律严明之人</td><td>a strict martinet<br>极严厉的执纪之人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their new sergeant was a martinet who inspected every button before morning drill.  他们的新中士是个执纪严苛的人，晨操前连纽扣都要检查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stiff</b></td><td>/stɪf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵硬的，无法弯曲的；艰苦的，费力的</td><td>a stiff leather collar<br>硬挺的皮质衣领</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stiff leather collar rubbed his neck raw during the long march.  长途行军中，硬挺的皮领把他的脖子磨破了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 immalleable</del></td><td>/ɪˈmæliəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可延展的，僵硬的</td><td>an immalleable metal<br>不可延展的金属</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cold cast iron is brittle and largely immalleable.  冷铸铁很脆，基本不可延展。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 impliable</del></td><td>/ɪmˈplaɪəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不易弯曲的，僵硬的</td><td>an impliable steel rod<br>不易弯曲的钢棒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The impliable rod snapped instead of bending.  这根不易弯曲的棒子直接断了而没有弯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inflexible</td><td>/ˌɪnˈfleksəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不肯变通的，僵化的</td><td>an inflexible rule<br>不容变通的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager was inflexible about the submission deadline.  经理在提交截止日期上毫不通融。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 petrified</td><td>/ˈpetrəˌfaɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵住的，惊呆的；石化的</td><td>petrified with fear<br>被吓得僵住</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stood petrified as the snake crossed the path.  蛇穿过小路时她吓得僵在原地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rigid</td><td>/ˈrɪdʒəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，僵硬的</td><td>rigid discipline<br>严格的纪律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school enforces rigid rules about mobile phones.  学校对手机执行严格的规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbending</td><td>/ʌnˈbendɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不亲切的；不妥协的</td><td>an unbending attitude<br>不肯通融的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old teacher was unbending about rules and manners.  这位老教师在规矩和礼节上毫不通融。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unyielding</td><td>/ʌnˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不屈的，不让步的</td><td>unyielding opposition<br>毫不让步的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union remained unyielding on the question of wages.  工会在工资问题上依然毫不让步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arduous</td><td>/ˈɑːrdʒuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以做到的，费劲的</td><td>an arduous journey<br>艰苦的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The climb proved far more arduous than any of them expected.  这次攀登远比他们任何人预想的都费劲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 challenging</td><td>/ˈtʃæləndʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有挑战性的，费心力的</td><td>a challenging assignment<br>有挑战性的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor gave us a challenging assignment due next week.  教授给了我们一个下周交的有挑战性作业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 difficult</td><td>/ˈdɪfəkəlt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>困难的，费力的</td><td>a difficult climb<br>艰难的攀登</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final section of the trail is genuinely difficult.  这条小径的最后一段确实艰难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 formidable</td><td>/ˈfɔːrmɪdəbl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以对付的，令人敬畏的</td><td>a formidable opponent<br>难以对付的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She faced a formidable opponent in the final round.  她在最后一轮遇到了难以对付的对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grueling</td><td>/ˈɡruːəlɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>累人的，折磨人的</td><td>a grueling schedule<br>折磨人的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team endured a grueling schedule of twelve games in three weeks.  球队熬过了三周十二场比赛这种折磨人的赛程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laborious</td><td>/ləˈbɔːriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，艰苦的；勤奋的</td><td>a laborious process<br>费力的过程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Copying the manuscript by hand proved a slow and laborious process.  手抄这份手稿是一个缓慢而费力的过程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strenuous</td><td>/ˈstrenjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，艰巨的；奋力的</td><td>strenuous exercise<br>费力的运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors advised him to avoid strenuous exercise for at least a month.  医生建议他至少一个月内避免费力的运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sweaty</td><td>/ˈsweti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，累人的；出汗的</td><td>sweaty work<br>累人的活儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Moving the piano upstairs was hot, sweaty work.  把钢琴搬上楼是又热又累人的活儿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 testing</td><td>/ˈtestɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>艰难的，考验人的</td><td>a testing climb<br>艰难的攀登</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final stage was a testing climb in thin air.  最后一段是在稀薄空气中艰难的攀登。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 toilsome</del></td><td>/ˈtɔɪlsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>辛苦的，费力的</td><td>toilsome labor<br>辛苦的劳作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Copying manuscripts by hand was toilsome work that ruined many eyes.  手抄书稿是毁坏许多人眼睛的辛苦活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tough</td><td>/ˈtʌf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>艰难的，费力的；坚韧的</td><td>a tough assignment<br>艰巨的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editing that manuscript proved a tough assignment.  编辑那份手稿被证明是项艰巨的任务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flexible</td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 floppy</td><td>/ˈflɑːpi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>松软的，松垂的</td><td>a floppy hat<br>软塌塌的帽子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She wore a floppy straw hat to block the sun.  她戴着一顶软塌塌的草帽遮阳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pliable</td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pliant</td><td>/ˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的；顺从的</td><td>a pliant assistant<br>顺从的助手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted a pliant assistant who would never question his decisions.  他想要一个顺从的助手，永远不会质疑他的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 supple</td><td>/ˈsʌpl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的</td><td>supple leather<br>柔软的皮革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gloves were made of supple leather that fit her hands perfectly.  这双手套用柔软的皮革制成，和她的手完全贴合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 yielding</td><td>/ˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，易屈服的</td><td>a yielding personality<br>顺从的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her yielding nature made her an easy target for pushy salesmen.  她顺从的性格使她很容易被强势的推销员盯上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 easy</td><td>/ˈiːzi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易的，不费力的</td><td>an easy question<br>简单的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The homework looked easy, but it took him three hours to finish.  作业看起来容易，可他花了三个小时才做完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 effortless</td><td>/ˈefərtləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻松的，不费力的</td><td>effortless grace<br>毫不费力的优雅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her swimming looked effortless even in the rough water.  即使在波涛中她的游泳也显得毫不费力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 facile</td><td>/ˈfæsl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易做到的；肤浅轻率的</td><td>a facile explanation<br>肤浅的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report offers a facile explanation for a very complex problem.  这份报告对一个非常复杂的问题给出了肤浅的解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 light</td><td>/ˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻微的，不严重的</td><td>a light meal<br>一顿便餐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He suffered only light injuries in the accident.  他在事故中只受了轻伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mindless</td><td>/ˈmaɪndləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不需用脑的；愚蠢的</td><td>mindless routine work<br>不需用脑的例行工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted mindless work that let him rest his brain.  他想做点不用脑的活儿，让大脑休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 simple</td><td>/ˈsɪmpəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单的，容易的</td><td>a simple task<br>简单的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Follow these simple steps and the machine will restart.  按这些简单步骤操作，机器就会重启。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soft</td><td>/ˈsɑːft/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔和的，轻微的</td><td>a soft voice<br>轻柔的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a soft tone so the baby would not wake.  她轻声说话，以免把婴儿弄醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undemanding</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈmændɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>要求不高的，轻松的</td><td>an undemanding course<br>要求不高的课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She chose an undemanding role so she could rest more.  她选了个轻松的角色以便多休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stifle</b></td><td>/ˈstaɪfl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制（声音、呼吸等）；阻止，扼杀</td><td>stifle a yawn<br>忍住哈欠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She tried to stifle a yawn while the speaker droned on.  演讲者絮絮不休，她努力忍住哈欠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 choke</td><td>/ˈtʃoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>使窒息；压制</td><td>choke on the smoke<br>被烟呛住</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick smoke nearly choked the firefighters in the hallway.  浓烟几乎使走廊里的消防员窒息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muffle</td><td>/ˈmʌfl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压低声音，包裹使消声</td><td>muffle the sound<br>把声音压低</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick curtains muffle the sound of traffic outside the window.  厚窗帘减弱了窗外的车流声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mute</td><td>/mjuːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不说话的，缄默的</td><td>remain mute on the issue<br>对此事保持缄默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained mute on the issue despite repeated questions from reporters.  尽管记者反复提问，他对此事仍保持沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repress</td><td>/rɪˈpres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>镇压；阻止（正常的表达、活动或发展等）</td><td>repress a smile<br>忍住笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She struggled to repress a smile during the solemn ceremony.  在庄严的仪式上她努力忍住笑意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smother</td><td>/ˈsmʌðər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制（表达、说出），压制</td><td>smother a laugh<br>忍住不笑出声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He turned away to smother a laugh at her odd remark.  他转过身去，忍住对她那句怪话的笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strangle</td><td>/ˈstræŋɡəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扼制，压制；勒死</td><td>strangle dissent<br>压制异议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy regulation strangled the small firms within two years.  繁重的管制两年内就压垮了这些小公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suffocate</td><td>/ˈsʌfəkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使窒息</td><td>suffocate in the smoke<br>在烟中窒息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several residents nearly suffocated in the thick smoke from the fire.  几名居民在火灾的浓烟中几乎窒息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suppress</td><td>/səˈpres/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>用暴力终止，镇压；封锁，删除</td><td>suppress a rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime used the army to suppress a rebellion in the capital.  该政权动用军队镇压首都的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foment</td><td>/foʊˈment/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>助长，煽动</td><td>foment unrest<br>煽动动乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlets were designed to foment rebellion in the provinces.  这些小册子意在煽动各省叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incite</td><td>/ɪnˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>煽动，激起</td><td>incite a riot<br>煽动暴乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with inciting a riot outside the county courthouse.  他被指控在县法院外煽动暴乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 instigate</td><td>/ˈɪnstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>煽动，激起</td><td>instigate a rebellion<br>煽动叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several officers were accused of helping to instigate the rebellion in the province.  几名军官被指控参与煽动该省的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 provoke</td><td>/prəˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒；驱使，激起</td><td>provoke an angry response<br>引发愤怒的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark provoked an angry response from the whole audience.  他不慎的言论激起了全体观众的愤怒回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stir</td><td>/ˈstɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，煽动；搅拌</td><td>stir up anger<br>激起怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The article stirred up anger among local residents.  这篇文章激起了当地居民的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stigma</b></td><td>/ˈstɪɡmə/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>耻辱，污名</td><td>the stigma of failure<br>失败带来的污名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stigma of failure kept him from applying for another job.  失败带来的污名使他不敢再去申请工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blemish</td><td>/ˈblemɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>缺点，污点；损害，玷污</td><td>a blemish on his record<br>他履历上的污点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One late payment left a small blemish on his credit record.  一次逾期付款在他的信用记录上留下了小小的污点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brand</td><td>/ˈbrænd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>污名，耻辱印记；品牌</td><td>the brand of disgrace<br>耻辱的印记</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The verdict left a brand of shame on his reputation.  这个判决给他的名声留下了耻辱印记。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 onus</td><td>/ˈoʊnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>责任，负担；污名</td><td>the onus of proof<br>举证的责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The onus of proving the claim falls on the plaintiff.  证明该主张的责任落在原告身上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slur</td><td>/slɜːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>含糊不清地说；诋毁，污辱</td><td>slur one's words<br>说话含糊不清</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was so tired that he began to slur his words.  他累得开始说话含糊不清。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 smirch</del></td><td>/smɜrtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>玷污（名誉）</td><td>smirch someone's reputation<br>玷污某人名誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rumor smirched the reputation he had built over thirty years.  谣言玷污了他三十年建立起来的名誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smudge</td><td>/ˈsmʌdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>污迹，污点；弄脏</td><td>a smudge of ink<br>一处墨迹污点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A smudge of ink spoiled the otherwise perfect page.  一处墨迹污点毁了这本来完美的一页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spot</td><td>/ˈspɑːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>污点，斑点；地点</td><td>a spot on the shirt<br>衬衫上的污点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A grease spot on the sleeve ruined his only white shirt.  袖子上的油污弄坏了他唯一的白衬衫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stain</td><td>/ˈsteɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>沾污，玷污；污点</td><td>stain his reputation<br>玷污他的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal stained the family name for two whole generations.  这桩丑闻让这个家族的名声蒙污两代人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 taint</td><td>/teɪnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使（品质）污损</td><td>taint someone's reputation<br>损害某人名誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal tainted his reputation for the rest of his career.  这场丑闻在他余下的职业生涯中都损害着他的名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 award</td><td>/əˈwɔːrd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>授予，颁发；奖励</td><td>award a medal<br>颁发奖章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee awarded her a medal for outstanding service.  委员会因她的杰出贡献授予她一枚奖章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 credit</td><td>/ˈkredɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>表扬，赞扬；信任</td><td>take credit for<br>把功劳归于自己</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She deserves credit for keeping the project alive.  她值得称赞，因为她让这个项目坚持下来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 honor</td><td>/ˈɑːnər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>尊敬，给以荣誉；荣誉</td><td>honor the veterans<br>表彰老兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city honored the veterans with a quiet morning ceremony.  该市用一场安静的晨间仪式表彰老兵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stint</b></td><td>/stɪnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>吝惜，节省</td><td>stint on materials<br>在材料上省钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The builder refused to stint on materials even when costs rose.  即便成本上升，这位建筑商也不肯在材料上省钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scrimp</td><td>/skrɪmp/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>节省；吝啬；紧缩开支</td><td>scrimp and save<br>省吃俭用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They had to scrimp and save for years to buy the shop.  他们省吃俭用多年才买下这家店铺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skimp</td><td>/skɪmp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>节省花费，吝惜；克扣</td><td>skimp on materials<br>在材料上省钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Builders who skimp on materials always create problems for later owners.  在材料上省钱的建筑商总会给后来的业主留下麻烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blow</td><td>/ˈbloʊ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>挥霍，乱花钱</td><td>blow one's savings<br>挥霍掉积蓄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He will blow his entire bonus on a week in Tokyo.  他会把整笔奖金挥霍在东京的一周上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissipate</td><td>/ˈdɪsɪpeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>驱散，消散；浪费</td><td>dissipate the fog<br>驱散雾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The morning mist dissipated as soon as the sun rose.  太阳一升起，晨雾就消散了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fritter</td><td>/ˈfrɪtər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浪费，挥霍</td><td>fritter away the afternoon<br>把下午浪费掉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She frittered away the whole grant on minor equipment.  她把整笔资助挥霍在零碎设备上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lavish</td><td>/ˈlævɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>奢侈的，大量的，无节制的；挥霍，浪费</td><td>a lavish banquet<br>奢华的宴会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They hosted a lavish banquet for three hundred guests in the garden.  他们在花园里为三百位客人举办了一场奢华的宴会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 misspend</del></td><td>/mɪsˈspend/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>滥用，浪费</td><td>misspend public funds<br>滥用公共资金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The audit showed the agency had misspent millions.  审计显示该机构浪费了数百万。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 squander</td><td>/ˈskwɑːndər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浪费</td><td>squander an opportunity<br>浪费机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not squander this opportunity to study abroad for a year.  不要浪费这次出国留学一年的机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 waste</td><td>/ˈweɪst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词、动词</td><td>荒芜的；浪费；废物</td><td>waste land<br>荒地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing grows on this waste ground beside the old factory.  旧工厂旁的这片荒地上什么也长不出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stingy</b></td><td>/ˈstɪndʒi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>小气的，吝啬的</td><td>stingy with money<br>在钱上很吝啬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is stingy with money but surprisingly generous with his time.  他在钱上吝啬，在时间上却出奇大方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 closefisted</del></td><td>/ˌkloʊsˈfɪstɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，一毛不拔的</td><td>a closefisted landlord<br>吝啬的房东</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their closefisted landlord refused to fix the heater.  他们吝啬的房东拒绝修暖气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mean</td><td>/miːn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑贱的；吝啬的</td><td>a mean little room<br>简陋卑贱的小屋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family lived in a mean little room above a noisy printing shop.  一家人住在嘈杂印刷店楼上一间简陋的小屋里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 mingy</del></td><td>/ˈmɪndʒi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的；少得可怜的</td><td>a mingy tip<br>少得可怜的小费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He left a mingy tip after a very long meal.  吃了很久之后他只留下少得可怜的小费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 miserly</td><td>/ˈmaɪzərli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的</td><td>a miserly tip<br>吝啬的小费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He left a miserly tip after complaining about the service all evening.  他整晚抱怨服务，最后只留下吝啬的小费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 niggardly</td><td>/ˈnɪɡərdli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，少得可怜的</td><td>a niggardly raise<br>少得可怜的加薪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered a niggardly raise after record profits.  在创纪录盈利后公司给了少得可怜的加薪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parsimonious</td><td>/ˌpɑːrsəˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，过分节俭的</td><td>parsimonious with praise<br>极少给出赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The parsimonious landlord refused to replace the broken heater.  吝啬的房东拒绝更换坏了的暖气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 penurious</del></td><td>/pəˈnʊriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的；吝啬的</td><td>penurious circumstances<br>贫困的境况</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent a penurious youth in a crowded tenement.  他在拥挤的廉租房里度过了贫困的青年时期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sparing</td><td>/ˈsperɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>节俭的，节约的</td><td>sparing use of salt<br>节省地用盐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was sparing with salt because of his doctor's strict orders.  遵医嘱，他用盐十分节省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 stinting</del></td><td>/ˈstɪntɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，节省克扣的</td><td>stinting with praise<br>吝于赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was stinting with praise but generous with advice.  他吝于赞扬，却乐于给建议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 tightfisted</del></td><td>/ˌtaɪtˈfɪstɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的</td><td>a tightfisted employer<br>吝啬的雇主</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their tightfisted employer counted every paper clip.  他们吝啬的雇主连回形针都要数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bountiful</td><td>/ˈbaʊntɪfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>丰富的，充裕的</td><td>a bountiful harvest<br>丰盛的收成</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The valley gave them a bountiful crop of apples.  这个山谷给了他们丰盛的苹果收成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 charitable</td><td>/ˈtʃærətəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，仁慈的</td><td>a charitable view<br>宽容的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a charitable view of his student's careless mistake.  他对学生的粗心错误持宽容态度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 freehanded</del></td><td>/ˌfriːˈhændɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大方的，慷慨的</td><td>a freehanded donor<br>慷慨的捐赠者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A freehanded donor paid for the entire new wing.  一位慷慨的捐赠者出资建了整座新翼楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 generous</td><td>/ˈdʒenərəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>丰富的，大量的；慷慨的</td><td>a generous helping<br>满满的一份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She poured a generous glass of wine for each guest.  她给每位客人倒了满满一杯酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liberal</td><td>/ˈlɪbərəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想前卫的；慷慨的，大方的</td><td>liberal social views<br>前卫的社会观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His liberal social views often surprised the older members of the club.  他前卫的社会观念常常让俱乐部里年长的成员感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 munificent</td><td>/mjuːˈnɪfɪsnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慷慨的</td><td>a munificent donation<br>一笔慷慨的捐赠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The university received a munificent donation from a former engineering student.  这所大学收到了一位工程系校友的慷慨捐赠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 openhanded</del></td><td>/ˌoʊpənˈhændɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慷慨的，大方的</td><td>openhanded generosity<br>毫不吝惜的慷慨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The openhanded donor paid for the whole library wing.  这位慷慨的捐赠者出资建了整个图书馆侧楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sequel</b></td><td>/ˈsiːkwəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>后续；结果；续篇</td><td>the sequel of the war<br>战争的后果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Widespread famine was the tragic sequel of the long war.  大范围的饥荒是这场长期战争的悲惨后果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aftermath</td><td>/ˈæftərˌmæθ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>后果，余波，影响</td><td>in the aftermath of<br>在…的余波中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Aid arrived slowly in the aftermath of the flood.  洪水过后援助迟迟才到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conclusion</td><td>/kənˈkluːʒən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>结束；结论</td><td>bring to a conclusion<br>使结束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trial reached its conclusion after eleven long weeks.  审判在漫长的十一周后结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consequence</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsəkwens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>结果；重要性，价值</td><td>of little consequence<br>无关紧要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The full consequences of the new policy will not be clear for years.  这项新政策的全部后果多年内都不会明朗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fate</td><td>/ˈfeɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>命运，宿命；注定</td><td>seal someone's fate<br>决定某人的命运</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless remark sealed his political fate.  一句不慎的话决定了他的政治命运。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fruit</td><td>/ˈfruːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>成果，结果；水果</td><td>the fruit of his labor<br>他劳动的成果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This building is the fruit of ten years' planning.  这座楼是十年规划的成果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outcome</td><td>/ˈaʊtˌkʌm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>结果，结局</td><td>the likely outcome<br>可能的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody could predict the outcome of such a close election.  没人能预测这样势均力敌的选举结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 product</td><td>/ˈprɑːdəkt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>产物，结果；产品</td><td>a product of its time<br>时代的产物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The law was a product of decades of public pressure.  这项法律是数十年公众压力的产物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 result</td><td>/rɪˈzʌlt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>结果；导致，产生</td><td>as a result of<br>由于…的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay resulted from a simple error in the schedule.  这次延误源于日程上的一个小错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sequence</td><td>/ˈsiːkwəns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>顺序，序列；接续的事物</td><td>in the right sequence<br>按正确顺序</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please perform the steps in the correct sequence.  请按正确顺序执行这些步骤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 antecedent</td><td>/ˌæntəˈsiːdnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>先前的；前事，前情</td><td>antecedent events<br>先前发生的事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians study the antecedent causes of the revolution.  历史学家研究这场革命的先前起因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 causation</td><td>/kɔːˈzeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>起因，因果关系</td><td>prove causation<br>证明因果关系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Correlation alone does not establish causation.  仅有相关性并不能确立因果关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cause</td><td>/ˈkɔːz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>原因；导致，引起</td><td>the root cause<br>根本原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain caused the small stream to flood the road.  大雨使这条小溪淹了公路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 occasion</td><td>/əˈkeɪʒən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>起因，诱因；场合</td><td>the occasion of the quarrel<br>争吵的起因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A trivial remark was the occasion of their long feud.  一句琐碎的话成了他们长期不和的起因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reason</td><td>/ˈriːzən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>理由，原因；推理</td><td>a good reason for<br>…的充分理由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave no reason for leaving the meeting early.  他没有给出提前离开会议的理由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 5

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stipulate</b></td><td>/ˈstɪpjuleɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>规定，特定要求</td><td>stipulate in the contract<br>在合同中规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract stipulates that all repairs must be finished within thirty days.  合同规定所有维修须在三十天内完成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 claim</td><td>/ˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>声称，自称</td><td>claim to be innocent<br>自称无辜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He claims to have finished the whole course in one weekend.  他自称一个周末就上完了整门课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 designate</td><td>/ˈdezɪɡneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>任命；命名</td><td>designate an heir<br>指定继承人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council designated the whole valley a protected natural area.  议会将整个山谷指定为自然保护区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detail</td><td>/dɪˈteɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>详述；细节</td><td>detail the procedure<br>详述该流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manual details every step of the assembly process.  手册详述了装配流程的每一步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 particularize</del></td><td>/pərˈtɪkjələraɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>详述</td><td>particularize the charges<br>详述各项指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge asked the lawyer to particularize the charges against his client.  法官要求律师详述针对其委托人的各项指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 qualify</td><td>/ˈkwɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>限定；使有资格，使有能力</td><td>qualify for the finals<br>取得决赛资格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor qualified his praise by noting several serious flaws.  教授指出几处严重缺陷，对自己的赞扬作了限定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relinquish</td><td>/rɪˈlɪŋkwɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放弃（权力、职位）</td><td>relinquish control<br>放弃控制权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She refused to relinquish control of the family business.  她拒绝放弃对家族企业的控制权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 surrender</td><td>/səˈrendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>交出，放弃，投降</td><td>surrender to the police<br>向警方投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief finally surrendered to the police after a long chase.  经过长时间追捕，小偷终于向警方投降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 yield</td><td>/ˈjiːld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>产生，出产；屈服，让出</td><td>yield results<br>产生结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of patient testing finally yielded a usable vaccine.  数月耐心的试验终于产出可用的疫苗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 waive</td><td>/ˈweɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放弃，免除（要求）</td><td>waive the requirement<br>免除该项要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The university agreed to waive the application fee.  校方同意免除申请费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stitch</b></td><td>/stɪtʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>突然剧痛</td><td>a stitch in the side<br>肋侧的突然剧痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sharp stitch in his side forced him to stop running.  肋侧一阵剧痛让他不得不停下奔跑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ache</td><td>/ˈeɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>渴望；疼痛</td><td>ache for home<br>渴望回家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After three years abroad she ached for her hometown.  在国外三年后，她十分渴望回到故乡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pang</td><td>/ˈpæŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一阵剧痛，一阵痛楚</td><td>a pang of hunger<br>一阵饥饿感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sharp pang shot through his knee as he stood.  他站起时膝盖一阵剧痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prick</td><td>/ˈprɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>刺痛；刺，扎</td><td>the prick of a needle<br>针的刺痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt only a slight prick when the needle went in.  针进去时她只感到轻微的刺痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shoot</td><td>/ˈʃuːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>射出，发射；迅速射出（光线等）</td><td>shoot a glance at<br>朝…投去一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lighthouse shot a beam of light across the dark water.  灯塔把一束光射过漆黑的水面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smart</td><td>/ˈsmɑːrt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>聪明的；时髦的</td><td>a smart move<br>明智之举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Buying the tickets early was a very smart decision.  提前买票是个非常明智的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sting</td><td>/ˈstɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>刺痛；蜇，刺</td><td>the sting of the wound<br>伤口的刺痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The alcohol caused a fierce sting on the cut.  酒精让伤口一阵剧烈刺痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 throe</del></td><td>/θroʊ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>剧痛，阵痛</td><td>in the throes of pain<br>处于剧痛之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient lay in the throes of unbearable pain.  病人躺在难以忍受的剧痛之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tingle</td><td>/ˈtɪŋɡəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>刺痛感，酥麻感</td><td>a tingle in the fingers<br>手指的刺麻感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His frozen fingers began to tingle beside the stove.  他冻僵的手指在炉边开始发麻刺痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 twinge</td><td>/ˈtwɪndʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一阵刺痛；一阵内疚</td><td>a twinge in the back<br>背部一阵刺痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt a twinge in his back as he lifted the box.  他抬箱子时背部一阵刺痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 comfort</td><td>/ˈkʌmfərt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>舒适；安慰</td><td>live in comfort<br>过舒适的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old chair gave him a great deal of comfort.  那把旧椅子给他带来极大的舒适。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ease</td><td>/ˈiːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减轻，缓和；容易</td><td>ease the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine eased her headache within minutes of the first dose.  这药在服下第一剂几分钟内就缓解了她的头痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 easiness</td><td>/ˈiːzinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻松，舒适安逸</td><td>the easiness of the task<br>任务的轻松</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The easiness of the first exercises misled the students.  最初练习的轻松让学生们产生了误判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stock</b></td><td>/stɑːk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>普通的，常备的</td><td>a stock phrase<br>一句套话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The politician answered every question with the same stock phrase.  这位政客用同一句套话回答每个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 common</td><td>/ˈkɑːmən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>普遍的，常见的；共有的</td><td>a common mistake<br>常见的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Spelling errors are a common problem in student essays.  拼写错误是学生作文中常见的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conventional</td><td>/kənˈvenʃənəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>传统的，常规的</td><td>conventional wisdom<br>传统观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They chose a conventional design to please the older members.  他们选了常规设计以取悦年长的成员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 general</td><td>/ˈdʒenərəl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>总体的，普遍的；笼统的</td><td>in general terms<br>笼统地讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This book gives a general introduction to ancient Greek history.  这本书对古希腊史作了总体介绍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ordinary</td><td>/ˈɔːrdəneri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>普通的，平常的</td><td>just an ordinary weekday<br>只是平常的工作日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He looked like an ordinary commuter waiting for the morning bus.  他看上去就像个等早班公交的普通上班族。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prosaic</td><td>/prəˈzeɪɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调的，常见的</td><td>prosaic details<br>平淡的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Behind the legend lies a prosaic story about a lost receipt.  传说背后是一个关于遗失收据的平淡故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 routine</td><td>/ruˈtiːn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>例行的；常规程序</td><td>a routine check<br>例行检查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The problem was discovered during a routine inspection last week.  这个问题是上周例行检查时发现的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 standard</td><td>/ˈstændərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>标准的；标准，规范</td><td>standard procedure<br>标准流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Checking IDs is standard procedure at the entrance.  在入口查验证件是标准流程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 universal</td><td>/ˌjuːnəˈvɜːrsəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>普遍的，全体的</td><td>universal human rights<br>普世人权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Music seems to have a universal appeal across all cultures.  音乐似乎在所有文化中都有普遍的感染力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unique</td><td>/juˈniːk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>独一无二的，独特的</td><td>a unique opportunity<br>独一无二的机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Each fingerprint is unique to the person who made it.  每个指纹都是其主人独有的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sectarian</b></td><td>/sekˈteriən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狭隘的</td><td>sectarian prejudice<br>狭隘的派系偏见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His sectarian views blinded him to the merits of other traditions.  他狭隘的派系观念使他看不到其他传统的优点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illiberal</td><td>/ɪˈlɪbərəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>心胸狭窄的；不开明的</td><td>illiberal attitudes<br>不开明的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His illiberal views on the arts surprised his younger colleagues.  他对艺术的狭隘看法让年轻同事很惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insular</td><td>/ˈɪnsələr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（观念、想法等）孤立狭隘的</td><td>an insular attitude<br>狭隘的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His insular attitude toward foreign customs cost him many friendships abroad.  他对外国习俗的狭隘态度使他在国外失去了许多友谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 narrow-minded</td><td>/ˌnæroʊ ˈmaɪndɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>心胸狭窄的，偏狭的</td><td>narrow-minded prejudice<br>狭隘的偏见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His narrow-minded remarks embarrassed everyone at the table.  他狭隘的言论让全桌人尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parochial</td><td>/pəˈroʊkiəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狭隘的</td><td>a parochial outlook<br>狭隘的眼界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His parochial outlook kept him from appreciating other cultures.  他狭隘的眼界使他无法欣赏其他文化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provincial</td><td>/prəˈvɪnʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>狭隘（的）</td><td>provincial tastes<br>狭隘的品味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His provincial tastes made him suspicious of anything foreign or unfamiliar.  他狭隘的品味使他对一切外来或陌生的事物都怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 narrow</td><td>/ˈnæroʊ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>狭窄的；狭隘的</td><td>a narrow outlook<br>狭隘的眼界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His narrow reading habits left him ignorant of modern poetry.  他狭窄的阅读习惯使他对现代诗一无所知。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 broad-minded</td><td>/ˌbrɔd ˈmaɪndəd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想开明的，宽容的</td><td>a broad-minded editor<br>开明的编辑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her broad-minded parents let her study art abroad.  她开明的父母让她出国学艺术。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 catholic</td><td>/ˈkæθlɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>普遍的，包容的</td><td>catholic tastes<br>广泛的爱好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her catholic tastes in music surprised everyone at the party.  她在音乐上广泛的爱好让聚会上每个人都惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cosmopolitan</td><td>/ˌkɑːzməˈpɑːlɪtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有世界性眼光的，包容的</td><td>a cosmopolitan city<br>国际化的都市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years abroad gave her a cosmopolitan outlook her old classmates clearly lacked.  多年国外生活给了她老同学明显缺乏的世界性眼光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liberal</td><td>/ˈlɪbərəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想前卫的；慷慨的，大方的</td><td>liberal social views<br>前卫的社会观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His liberal social views often surprised the older members of the club.  他前卫的社会观念常常让俱乐部里年长的成员感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 open-minded</td><td>/ˈoʊpən ˈmaɪndɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想开明的</td><td>an open-minded editor<br>思想开明的编辑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good reviewer stays open-minded about unfamiliar musical styles.  好的评论者对陌生的音乐风格保持开明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stodgy</b></td><td>/ˈstɑːdʒi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>古板守旧的，乏味的</td><td>a stodgy old textbook<br>一本乏味的旧教材</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The course relied on a stodgy old textbook nobody enjoyed reading.  这门课用的是一本没人爱读的乏味旧教材。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arid</td><td>/ˈærɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>枯燥无趣的；干燥的</td><td>an arid lecture<br>枯燥的讲座</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor's arid lecture emptied half the seats before the break.  这位教授枯燥乏味的讲座在课间前就空了一半座位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dreary</td><td>/ˈdrɪri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调乏味的</td><td>a dreary afternoon<br>沉闷的下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent a dreary afternoon indoors watching rain streak the windows.  我们在室内度过一个沉闷的下午，看着雨水划过窗玻璃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dull</td><td>/dʌl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的；乏味的</td><td>a dull student<br>迟钝的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecture was so dull that half the class slept.  讲座乏味得让一半学生睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flat</td><td>/ˈflæt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调无光的，乏味的</td><td>a flat performance<br>平淡无味的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paint dried to a flat, lifeless gray.  油漆干成了一种无光而死板的灰色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 Jading</del></td><td>/ˈdʒeɪdɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌倦的，使人生厌</td><td>a jading routine<br>令人厌倦的日常</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of jading paperwork dulled his early enthusiasm.  多年令人厌倦的文书工作磨掉了他早年的热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jejune</td><td>/dʒɪˈdʒuːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣乏味的</td><td>a jejune argument<br>乏味浅薄的论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics dismissed the sequel as jejune and repetitive.  评论家认为这部续集乏味且重复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 monochromatic</td><td>/ˌmɑːnəkroʊˈmætɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单色的</td><td>a monochromatic palette<br>单色的配色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The designer chose a monochromatic scheme of soft grays.  设计师选了柔灰的单色方案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 monotonous</td><td>/məˈnɑːtənəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调的，无变化的</td><td>a monotonous voice<br>单调的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His monotonous voice made the afternoon lecture almost impossible to sit through.  他单调的声音让下午的讲座几乎难以听完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pedestrian</td><td>/pəˈdestriən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平庸无奇的，令人厌倦的</td><td>a pedestrian style<br>平庸的文风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reviewer called the plot pedestrian and the dialogue painfully flat.  评论者称其情节平庸，对话乏味得令人难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stale</td><td>/steɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈旧乏味的</td><td>stale jokes<br>老掉牙的笑话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His stale jokes made the audience shift restlessly in their seats.  他老掉牙的笑话让观众在座位上不安地挪动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tedious</td><td>/ˈtiːdiəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长乏味的</td><td>a tedious explanation<br>冗长乏味的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager gave a tedious explanation of the new filing system.  经理对新的归档系统作了冗长乏味的解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wearisome</td><td>/ˈwɪrisəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌倦的，乏味的</td><td>a wearisome journey<br>令人疲乏的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wearisome journey took three days by slow train.  这段疲乏的旅程坐慢车要三天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absorbing</td><td>/əbˈzɔːrbɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，吸引人的</td><td>an absorbing novel<br>引人入胜的小说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I found her account of the voyage utterly absorbing.  我觉得她对航行的叙述极其引人入胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 engaging</td><td>/ɪnˈɡeɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有吸引力的，讨人喜欢的</td><td>an engaging personality<br>讨人喜欢的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His engaging manner made strangers feel comfortable within minutes.  他讨人喜欢的举止让陌生人几分钟内就放松下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 engrossing</td><td>/ɪnˈɡroʊsɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，令人专注的</td><td>an engrossing story<br>引人入胜的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary was so engrossing that nobody left early.  这部纪录片如此引人入胜，没人提早离场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gripping</td><td>/ˈɡrɪpɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>扣人心弦的，紧张的</td><td>a gripping thriller<br>扣人心弦的惊悚片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final chapter is gripping from start to finish.  最后一章从头到尾都扣人心弦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 interesting</td><td>/ˈɪntrəstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有趣的，引人入胜的</td><td>an interesting proposal<br>一个有趣的提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She raised an interesting objection nobody had anticipated.  她提出了一个无人预料到的有趣反对意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intriguing</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtriːɡɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人好奇的，有趣的</td><td>an intriguing question<br>引人好奇的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She raised an intriguing point that nobody had considered.  她提出了一个无人考虑过的有趣观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 involving</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlvɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，扣人心弦的</td><td>an involving story<br>引人入胜的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary is more involving than any thriller this year.  这部纪录片比今年任何惊悚片都更扣人心弦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 riveting</td><td>/ˈrɪvɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吸引人的，极迷人的</td><td>a riveting account<br>引人入胜的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary offers a riveting account of life inside the mountain villages.  这部纪录片对山村生活的叙述引人入胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stoke</b></td><td>/stoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>增大，促进</td><td>stoke public anger<br>激起公众怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's careless remarks stoked public anger across the whole district.  市长轻率的言论激起全区公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggrandize</td><td>/əˈɡrændaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大（权力、财富、声望）</td><td>aggrandize one's power<br>扩张自己的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor used the crisis to aggrandize his own power.  市长利用危机扩大自己的权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amplify</td><td>/ˈæmpləˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大，增强；详述</td><td>amplify a signal<br>放大信号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you amplify your last remark a little?  你能把最后那句话再详述一下吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 augment</td><td>/ɔɡˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>增加，增强</td><td>augment one's income<br>增加收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She augments her salary by teaching evening classes.  她通过教夜校课程增加收入。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 boost</td><td>/ˈbuːst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>提高，促进；推动</td><td>boost sales<br>促进销售</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax cut is expected to boost consumer spending.  减税预计会促进消费者支出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enlarge</td><td>/ɪnˈlɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，增大</td><td>enlarge the photograph<br>放大照片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They enlarged the kitchen by removing one interior wall.  他们拆掉一道内墙把厨房扩大了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 escalate</td><td>/ˈeskəleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）（战争等）升级，扩大</td><td>escalate into violence<br>升级为暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The border dispute quickly escalated into a full-scale armed conflict.  边界争端迅速升级为全面武装冲突。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expand</td><td>/ɪkˈspænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，扩张；详述</td><td>expand the business<br>扩大业务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to expand into three new markets.  公司计划扩张到三个新市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extend</td><td>/ɪkˈstend/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>延长，扩展</td><td>extend a deadline<br>延长截止期限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They agreed to extend the deadline by two weeks.  他们同意把截止日期延长两周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 multiply</td><td>/ˈmʌltɪplaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>大量增加，繁殖；相乘</td><td>multiply rapidly<br>迅速繁殖增多</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bacteria multiply quickly in warm and damp conditions.  细菌在温暖潮湿的环境中迅速繁殖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 raise</td><td>/ˈreɪz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提出（问题）；举起，提高</td><td>raise an objection<br>提出反对意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He raised an objection just before the vote was taken.  就在投票前他提出了反对意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abate</td><td>/əˈbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，减弱；减少</td><td>the storm gradually abated<br>风暴逐渐减弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fever finally abated after two days of medication.  服药两天后，烧终于退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decrease</td><td>/dɪˈkriːs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减少，下降</td><td>a sharp decrease<br>急剧下降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic accidents decreased noticeably after the new speed limits appeared.  新限速出台后交通事故明显减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
