<tr><td>↳反 diminish</td><td>/dɪˈmɪnɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）减少；轻视，贬低</td><td>diminish in importance<br>重要性下降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His influence diminished steadily after he left the board.  他离开董事会后影响力持续减弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 downsize</td><td>/ˈdaʊnˌsaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>缩减规模，裁员</td><td>downsize the workforce<br>裁减员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The firm downsized its workforce after losing two major contracts.  公司在丢掉两个大合同后裁员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dwindle</td><td>/ˈdwɪndl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>逐渐减少</td><td>dwindle to nothing<br>逐渐减少至无</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their savings dwindled during the months of unemployment.  失业的几个月里他们的积蓄逐渐减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lessen</td><td>/ˈlesən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减少，减轻</td><td>lessen the risk<br>减少风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple exercises lessen the pain in his stiff shoulder.  简单的锻炼减轻了他僵硬肩膀的疼痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lower</td><td>/ˈloʊər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>降低，减少；较低的</td><td>lower the temperature<br>降低温度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bank lowered interest rates twice in one year.  银行一年内两次降低利率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 minify</del></td><td>/ˈmɪnəfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缩小，使变小</td><td>minify the risk<br>缩小该风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report tried to minify the scale of the leak.  这份报告试图缩小泄漏的规模。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reduce</td><td>/rəˈduːs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减少，降低</td><td>reduce the cost sharply<br>大幅降低成本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New machines reduced the factory's energy use by half.  新机器把工厂的能耗降低了一半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 subtract</td><td>/səbˈtrækt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减去，扣除</td><td>subtract the expenses<br>减去各项开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Subtract the shipping cost from the total price.  从总价中减去运费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stoic</b></td><td>/ˈstoʊɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐忍的，冷静的</td><td>stoic acceptance of pain<br>隐忍地承受痛苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her stoic acceptance of pain impressed even the veteran nurses.  她隐忍地承受痛苦，连资深护士都为之动容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aloof</td><td>/əˈluːf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷淡的，冷漠孤傲的</td><td>remain aloof from<br>对…保持冷淡疏远</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed aloof from office gossip and rarely joined lunch.  他对办公室的闲话保持冷淡，很少参加午餐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apathetic</td><td>/ˌæpəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠的，无兴趣的</td><td>apathetic voters<br>冷漠的选民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most students seemed apathetic about the election and did not vote.  大多数学生对这次选举漠不关心，也没有投票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detached</td><td>/dɪˈtætʃt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠超然的，不介入的</td><td>a detached attitude<br>超然冷漠的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She described the accident in a strangely detached voice.  她用一种异常冷漠的语气描述了那场事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forbearing</td><td>/fɔrˈbɛrɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，有耐心的</td><td>a forbearing teacher<br>宽容的老师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His forbearing manner calmed even the rudest customers.  他宽容的态度连最无礼的顾客都能安抚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indifferent</td><td>/ɪnˈdɪfrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不感兴趣的，冷漠的；平庸的</td><td>indifferent to criticism<br>对批评漠不关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is entirely indifferent to what her critics say.  她对批评者说什么完全无所谓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impassive</td><td>/ɪmˈpæsɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠的，无感情的</td><td>remain impassive under pressure<br>压力下仍无动于衷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His face stayed impassive as the verdict was read aloud.  宣读判决时，他的脸上始终毫无表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 phlegmatic</td><td>/fleɡˈmætɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静的，无感情的，淡漠的</td><td>a phlegmatic temperament<br>冷静的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her phlegmatic response to the crisis calmed the entire staff.  她对危机的冷静反应让全体员工镇定下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stolid</td><td>/ˈstɑːlɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无动于衷的，感情麻木的</td><td>a stolid unsmiling guard<br>麻木不笑的卫兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A stolid unsmiling guard stood beside the gate all afternoon.  一名麻木不笑的卫兵整个下午守在门旁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tolerant</td><td>/ˈtɑːlərənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，容忍的</td><td>tolerant of criticism<br>能容忍批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good editor is tolerant of odd but honest writing.  好编辑对古怪却真诚的文字是宽容的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unemotional</td><td>/ˌʌnɪˈmoʊʃənəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不动感情的，冷静的</td><td>an unemotional account<br>不带感情的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unemotional summary of the disaster unsettled the listeners.  她不带感情的灾难总结让听众不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 demonstrative</td><td>/dɪˈmɑːnstrətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>感情外露的</td><td>a demonstrative greeting<br>热情外露的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her demonstrative hugs surprised colleagues used to formal handshakes.  她热情的拥抱让习惯正式握手的同事很惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 emotional</td><td>/ɪˈmoʊʃənəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>感情丰富的；动情的</td><td>an emotional farewell speech<br>动情的告别演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He became emotional when describing his father's last letter.  描述父亲最后一封信时他动了感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fervent</td><td>/ˈfɜːrvənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满感情的，热情洋溢的</td><td>a fervent believer<br>热切的信徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is a fervent believer in free public libraries for everyone.  她热切地相信应该为所有人提供免费的公共图书馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fervid</td><td>/ˈfɜːrvɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的；热情的</td><td>a fervid supporter<br>热情的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fervid speech moved even the quietest people in the room.  他热情洋溢的演讲连房间里最安静的人都被感动了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impassioned</td><td>/ɪmˈpæʃənd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慷慨激昂的，充满激情的</td><td>an impassioned speech<br>慷慨激昂的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave an impassioned defense of public libraries.  她慷慨激昂地为公共图书馆辩护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 passionate</td><td>/ˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，充满激情的</td><td>a passionate defense of art<br>对艺术的热情辩护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is passionate about protecting the city's old neighborhoods.  她对保护城市的老街区充满热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vehement</td><td>/ˈviːəmənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（情感）强烈的，热情的</td><td>vehement opposition<br>强烈的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a vehement protest against the new tax.  她对新税提出了强烈的抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stolid</b></td><td>/ˈstɑːlɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无动于衷的，感情麻木的</td><td>a stolid unsmiling guard<br>麻木不笑的卫兵</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 catatonic</td><td>/ˌkætəˈtɑːnɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>呆滞的，木僵的</td><td>a catatonic stare<br>呆滞的目光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the news he sat in a catatonic silence.  听到消息后他呆滞地默坐着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deadpan</td><td>/ˈdedpæn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不带表情的，板着脸的</td><td>a deadpan expression<br>面无表情的神情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He told the joke with a deadpan face and nobody laughed.  他面无表情地讲了那个笑话，没有人笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 emotionless</td><td>/ɪˈmoʊʃənləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感情的，冷漠的</td><td>an emotionless voice<br>毫无感情的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He read the verdict in a flat, emotionless tone.  他用平板毫无感情的语调宣读了判决。</td></tr>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 Numb with grief, she answered the reporters' questions in a flat voice.  她因悲伤而麻木，用平板的声音回答记者的提问。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stomach</b></td><td>/ˈstʌmək/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>容忍</td><td>cannot stomach the insult<br>无法忍受这种侮辱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He could not stomach the insult and walked out of the meeting.  他无法忍受这种侮辱，径直退出会议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abide</td><td>/əˈbaɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>容忍；遵守；持续</td><td>cannot abide dishonesty<br>不能容忍不诚实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She cannot abide people who interrupt her while reading.  她无法容忍别人在她读书时打扰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brook</td><td>/brʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忍受，容许</td><td>brook no delay<br>不容拖延</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strict coach will brook no excuses for missing practice.  这位严厉的教练不容许任何缺席训练的借口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 countenance</td><td>/ˈkaʊntənəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>容忍；赞成，推崇</td><td>countenance such behavior<br>容忍这种行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The headmaster would not countenance cheating of any kind in his school.  校长绝不容忍校内任何形式的作弊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endure</td><td>/ɪnˈdʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>忍受；持续存在</td><td>endure great hardship<br>忍受巨大艰苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The settlers endured three winters without proper shelter.  移民们在没有像样住所的情况下熬过了三个冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 handle</td><td>/ˈhændəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>应付，处理</td><td>handle the pressure<br>应付压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She handled the criticism with surprising calm.  她以出人意料的镇定应对了批评。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stand</td><td>/ˈstænd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>容忍，忍受；站立</td><td>cannot stand the noise<br>受不了噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I cannot stand the noise from the construction site next door.  我受不了隔壁工地的噪音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tolerate</td><td>/ˈtɑːləreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>容忍，忍受</td><td>tolerate the noise<br>容忍噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He could not tolerate the noise from the street below.  他无法容忍下面街道的噪音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decline</td><td>/dɪˈklaɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝；下降，衰退</td><td>decline an invitation<br>谢绝邀请</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She politely declined the invitation to speak at the ceremony.  她礼貌地谢绝了在典礼上发言的邀请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refuse</td><td>/rɪˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，不肯；废弃物</td><td>refuse to cooperate<br>拒绝合作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness refused to answer any further questions in court.  证人拒绝在法庭上回答任何进一步的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reject</td><td>/rɪˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，驳回；废品</td><td>reject the proposal<br>否决提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee voted to reject the proposal without further discussion.  委员会投票否决了该提案，不再讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 repudiate</td><td>/rɪˈpjuːdieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝接受</td><td>repudiate a claim<br>否认一项主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author repudiated the views quoted in the article.  作者否认了文章中引用的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spurn</td><td>/spɜːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>摈弃，拒绝</td><td>spurn an offer<br>断然拒绝提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spurned the generous offer without giving any explanation at all.  她断然拒绝了这个优厚的提议，且不作任何解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stonewall</b></td><td>/ˌstoʊnˈwɔːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>拒绝（合作），阻挠</td><td>stonewall the investigation<br>阻挠调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Company lawyers stonewalled the investigation for more than two years.  公司律师阻挠调查两年多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blockade</td><td>/ˌblɑːˈkeɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>封锁，阻塞</td><td>impose a blockade<br>实施封锁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The navy set up a blockade around the island's main port.  海军对该岛主港实施了封锁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 filibuster</td><td>/ˈfɪlɪbʌstər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>（议会中）阻挠议事</td><td>launch a filibuster<br>发起议事阻挠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator launched a filibuster to delay the final vote.  那位参议员发起阻挠议事来拖延最终表决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hinder</td><td>/ˈhɪndər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>hinder progress<br>妨碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor lighting hindered the search for survivors.  照明不足妨碍了对生还者的搜寻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impede</td><td>/ɪmˈpiːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻碍</td><td>impede progress<br>阻碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick fog impeded the rescue team's progress.  浓雾阻碍了救援队的前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstruct</td><td>/əbˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻塞</td><td>obstruct justice<br>妨碍司法公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fallen tree obstructed the only road out of town.  一棵倒下的树堵住了唯一的出城道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 collaborate</td><td>/kəˈlæbəˌreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>合作，协作</td><td>collaborate on a book<br>合作写一本书</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two labs collaborated closely to finish the vaccine trial.  两个实验室紧密合作完成了疫苗试验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cooperate</td><td>/koʊˈɑːpəreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>合作，配合</td><td>cooperate with the police<br>配合警方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness agreed to cooperate fully with investigators.  证人同意完全配合调查人员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 6

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>stouthearted</b></del></td><td>/ˌstaʊtˈhɑːrtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>勇敢的</td><td>stouthearted defenders<br>勇敢的守卫者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A handful of stouthearted defenders held the bridge until dawn.  少数勇敢的守卫者守住桥梁直到黎明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bold</td><td>/ˈboʊld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大胆的，勇敢的；醒目的</td><td>a bold move<br>大胆的举动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was a bold decision to leave a secure job.  辞去稳定的工作是个大胆的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brave</td><td>/ˈbreɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>勇敢的</td><td>a brave decision<br>勇敢的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was brave of her to speak against the new policy.  她敢于反对新政策，很勇敢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 courageous</td><td>/kəˈreɪdʒəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>勇敢的，有胆量的</td><td>a courageous decision<br>勇敢的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was courageous of him to speak out against the policy.  他公开反对这项政策，很有勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dauntless</td><td>/ˈdɔntləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无畏的，勇敢的</td><td>dauntless courage<br>无畏的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dauntless nurse worked through shelling to reach the wounded.  这位无畏的护士在炮击中赶去救伤员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fearless</td><td>/ˈfɪrləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无畏的，大胆的</td><td>a fearless reporter<br>无畏的记者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was fearless in questioning the most powerful men present.  她敢于质问在场最有权势的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gallant</td><td>/ˈɡælənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>英勇的</td><td>a gallant defense<br>英勇的防守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gallant paratroopers jumped from the plane without a moment's hesitation.  英勇的伞兵们毫不犹豫地跳出了飞机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intrepid</td><td>/ɪnˈtrepɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大胆的</td><td>an intrepid explorer<br>无畏的探险家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The intrepid explorer crossed the frozen strait alone in the middle of winter.  这位大胆的探险家在隆冬时节独自穿越了结冰的海峡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stalwart</td><td>/ˈstɔːlwərt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定忠诚的；强壮的</td><td>a stalwart supporter<br>坚定的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained a stalwart supporter of the project through every setback.  历经挫折，她始终是这个项目的坚定支持者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stout</td><td>/ˈstaʊt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，勇敢的；粗壮的</td><td>stout resistance<br>顽强的抵抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The townspeople put up a stout defense against the raiders.  镇民对袭击者进行了顽强的抵抗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 valiant</td><td>/ˈvæliənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>勇敢的，英勇的</td><td>a valiant effort<br>英勇的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A valiant firefighter carried two children out of the burning building.  一位英勇的消防员把两个孩子抱出了燃烧的大楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 valorous</td><td>/ˈvælərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>勇猛的，英勇的</td><td>a valorous knight<br>英勇的骑士</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Songs still honor the valorous soldiers who held the bridge.  歌曲仍在颂扬守住那座桥的英勇士兵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cowardly</td><td>/ˈkaʊərdli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>怯懦的，胆小的</td><td>a cowardly attack<br>卑怯的袭击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was cowardly to blame the mistake on his assistant.  把错误推给助手是怯懦的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 craven</td><td>/ˈkreɪvn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非常懦弱的，因胆小而遭人鄙视的</td><td>a craven retreat<br>怯懦的撤退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His craven silence during the trial disappointed everyone who had trusted him.  他在庭审中懦弱的沉默让所有信任过他的人失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fearful</td><td>/ˈfɪrfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>害怕的，胆怯的</td><td>fearful of the dark<br>害怕黑暗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The child was fearful of every shadow in the hallway.  这孩子害怕走廊里的每一个影子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gutless</td><td>/ˈɡʌtləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>没胆量的，懦弱的</td><td>a gutless decision<br>懦弱的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team looked gutless in the second half of the match.  球队下半场表现得毫无斗志。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nerveless</td><td>/ˈnɜːrvləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>软弱无力的；镇定的</td><td>nerveless fingers<br>软弱无力的手指</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cup slipped from his nerveless fingers and broke.  杯子从他无力的手指间滑落打碎了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pusillanimous</td><td>/ˌpjuːsɪˈlænɪməs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>胆小的，懦弱的</td><td>a pusillanimous response<br>软弱的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the government's pusillanimous silence a betrayal of victims.  批评者称政府懦弱的沉默是对受害者的背叛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spineless</td><td>/ˈspaɪnləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>没骨气的，软弱的</td><td>a spineless coward<br>没骨气的胆小鬼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too spineless to stand up to his own manager.  他太没骨气，不敢反抗自己的经理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 spiritless</del></td><td>/ˈspɪrɪtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无精打采的，没有生气的</td><td>a spiritless performance<br>毫无生气的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team gave a spiritless performance in the second half.  球队在下半场表现得毫无生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 timorous</td><td>/ˈtɪmərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>胆小的</td><td>a timorous little voice<br>胆怯的小声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The timorous student finally raised her hand to answer the question.  那个胆怯的学生终于举手回答问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stratagem</b></td><td>/ˈstrætədʒəm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>谋略，策略</td><td>a military stratagem<br>一项军事谋略</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general won the pass by a simple stratagem.  将军用一个简单的谋略夺取了关隘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 artifice</td><td>/ˈɑːrtəfɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计，欺骗；巧妙手段</td><td>use clever artifice<br>使用巧妙的诡计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her apparent shyness was pure artifice designed to disarm her rivals.  她表面的羞怯纯属伪装，为的是麻痹对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 device</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>手段，策略；装置</td><td>a clever narrative device<br>巧妙的叙事手法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Flashbacks are a device she uses in every novel.  倒叙是她每部小说都用的手法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gambit</td><td>/ˈɡæmbɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>开局策略，手段</td><td>an opening gambit<br>开场的策略</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His opening gambit was to praise his rival's work.  他的开场策略是赞扬对手的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gimmick</td><td>/ˈɡɪmɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>花招，噱头</td><td>a marketing gimmick<br>营销花招</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Free shipping was just a gimmick to attract buyers.  免运费只是吸引买家的花招。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intrigue</td><td>/ˈɪntriːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>阴谋；激起…的兴趣</td><td>political intrigue<br>政治阴谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Court records reveal decades of political intrigue behind the sudden fall of the minister.  法庭记录揭示了这位大臣突然倒台背后数十年的政治阴谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maneuver</td><td>/məˈnuːvər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>巧妙地操纵</td><td>maneuver the car into place<br>把车巧妙地挪到位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She patiently maneuvered the car into a very narrow parking space.  她耐心地把车挪进一个非常狭窄的停车位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ruse</td><td>/ruːz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计</td><td>a clever ruse<br>一个巧妙的诡计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empty truck was a clever ruse to draw the guards away.  那辆空卡车是个巧妙的诡计，用来引开守卫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scheme</td><td>/ˈskiːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>阴谋，计划</td><td>a scheme to defraud investors<br>诈骗投资者的阴谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They devised a scheme to avoid paying any taxes.  他们设计了一个逃税的计谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trick</td><td>/ˈtrɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>欺骗，诱骗；诡计</td><td>trick someone into paying<br>诱骗某人付钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She tricked him into revealing the password to the account.  她骗他说出了账户密码。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wile</td><td>/ˈwaɪl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计，花招</td><td>use every wile<br>使尽各种花招</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She used every wile she knew to win his support.  她用尽一切手段来赢得他的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sonorous</b></td><td>/ˈsɑːnərəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音洪亮的</td><td>a sonorous bell<br>一口洪亮的钟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The priest read the passage in a slow, sonorous voice.  神父用缓慢洪亮的声音朗读那段经文。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 plangent</del></td><td>/ˈplændʒənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（声音）响亮而哀伤的</td><td>a plangent cry<br>哀伤的呼声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plangent cry of a gull echoed across the empty beach.  海鸥凄凉的叫声在空荡的海滩上回响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resounding</td><td>/rɪˈzaʊndɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>响亮的，回响的；巨大的</td><td>a resounding crash<br>响亮的撞击声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The door shut with a resounding bang behind him.  门在他身后砰然作响地关上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reverberant</td><td>/rɪˈvɜrbərənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>回响的，反射声音的</td><td>a reverberant hall<br>一个回声很大的大厅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their footsteps echoed in the reverberant stone corridor.  他们的脚步声在回声很大的石廊里回荡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resonant</td><td>/ˈrezənənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（声音）洪亮的，共鸣的</td><td>a resonant voice<br>一个洪亮的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His resonant voice filled the stone hall without any need for a microphone.  他洪亮的嗓音无需麦克风便充满了石砌大厅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vibrant</td><td>/ˈvaɪbrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的；鲜艳的</td><td>a vibrant city<br>一座充满活力的城市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The neighborhood became a vibrant hub for young artists.  这个街区成了年轻艺术家的活力中心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>strand</b></td><td>/strænd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遗弃，使置于困境</td><td>stranded on an island<br>被困在岛上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The storm stranded dozens of travelers on the small island overnight.  暴风雨使数十名旅客一夜困在小岛上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abandon</td><td>/əˈbændən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>放纵，尽情；放弃</td><td>with reckless abandon<br>肆意放纵地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She danced with wild abandon at the wedding reception.  她在婚宴上尽情忘我地跳舞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 desert</td><td>/dɪˈzɜːrt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抛弃，遗弃</td><td>desert one's post<br>擅离职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain refused to desert his crew in the storm.  船长拒绝在风暴中抛下船员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forsake</td><td>/fərˈseɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>彻底放弃，抛弃</td><td>forsake one's friends<br>抛弃自己的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would never forsake the people who raised him.  他绝不会抛弃养育他的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maroon</td><td>/məˈruːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>把…遗弃在孤岛上，使孤立无援</td><td>maroon on a desert island<br>遗弃在荒岛上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mutineers marooned the captain on a tiny uninhabited island.  叛变者把船长遗弃在一个荒无人烟的小岛上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quit</td><td>/ˈkwɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>退出，辞去；停止</td><td>quit the organization<br>退出该组织</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She quit the committee after the second disagreement.  第二次分歧之后她退出了委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 evacuate</td><td>/ɪˈvækjueɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤离</td><td>evacuate the building<br>撤离大楼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firefighters evacuated the entire building before the roof collapsed.  消防员在屋顶塌落前撤离了整栋大楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reclaim</td><td>/riˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>感化，使改造；开垦，回收</td><td>reclaim young offenders<br>感化少年犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity tries to reclaim addicts through work and counseling.  该慈善机构试图通过工作和咨询感化成瘾者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rescue</td><td>/ˈreskju/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>解救，营救</td><td>rescue the trapped miners<br>营救被困矿工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Helicopters rescued twelve people from the flooded village.  直升机从被淹的村庄救出十二人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stratify</b></td><td>/ˈstrætɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>将…分成各种等级</td><td>stratify society by income<br>按收入将社会分层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Economists often stratify society by income to study patterns of spending.  经济学家常按收入将社会分层，以研究消费模式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 assort</del></td><td>/əˈsɔːrt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分类，把…分门别类</td><td>assort goods by size<br>按尺寸分类货物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers assort the apples by size before packing them into crates.  工人们在装箱前按大小把苹果分类。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 categorize</td><td>/ˈkætəɡəraɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>将…分类，归类</td><td>categorize books by subject<br>按主题给书分类</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Librarians categorize every new book by subject and reading level.  图书馆员按主题和阅读难度给每本新书分类。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 classify</td><td>/ˈklæsəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分类，归类</td><td>classify plants into families<br>把植物分成科</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Biologists classify these strange creatures as neither plants nor animals.  生物学家把这些奇怪的生物归为既非植物也非动物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 differentiate</td><td>/ˌdɪfəˈrenʃieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>区分，区别；使不同</td><td>differentiate between species<br>区分不同物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is hard to differentiate the two brands by taste alone.  仅凭味道很难区分这两个品牌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinguish</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>区分，辨别；使显著</td><td>distinguish right from wrong<br>分辨是非</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young children cannot always distinguish fantasy from reality.  幼儿并不总能区分幻想与现实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 homogenize</td><td>/həˈmɑːdʒənaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使统一化</td><td>homogenize the curriculum<br>使课程统一化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 National testing tends to homogenize teaching across very different schools.  全国统考往往使差异很大的学校的教学趋于统一。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stray</b></td><td>/streɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>漫无目的的</td><td>stray thoughts<br>零散的念头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A few stray thoughts kept interrupting her careful reading of the contract.  几个零散的念头不断打断她对合同的细读。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aimless</td><td>/ˈeɪmləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无目的的，无方向的</td><td>an aimless life<br>漫无目的的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After graduation he drifted through several aimless years in the city.  毕业后他在城里虚度了几年漫无目的的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arbitrary</td><td>/ˈɑːrbətreri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>专横的，独断专行的；随意的</td><td>an arbitrary decision<br>武断的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rules seemed completely arbitrary to the students.  这些规定在学生看来完全是随意的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 desultory</td><td>/ˈdesəlˌtɔːri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>散漫的，无条理的</td><td>desultory conversation<br>散漫的谈话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They made desultory notes instead of a real research plan.  他们做了些散漫的笔记，而非真正的研究计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 erratic</td><td>/ɪˈrætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反复无常的，不规律的</td><td>erratic driving behavior<br>飘忽不定的驾驶行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His erratic driving behavior alarmed the passengers long before the police stopped him.  早在警察拦下他之前，他飘忽不定的驾驶行为就让乘客惊慌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 haphazard</td><td>/hæpˈhæzərd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>随意的；杂乱无章的</td><td>a haphazard manner<br>杂乱无章的方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The files were stacked in a haphazard pile on the office floor.  文件杂乱无章地堆在办公室的地板上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scattered</td><td>/ˈskætərd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>分散的，零星的</td><td>scattered showers<br>零星阵雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scattered books and papers covered every surface of his small desk.  零散的书和纸铺满了他小书桌的每一处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slapdash</td><td>/ˈslæpdæʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>马虎的</td><td>a slapdash job<br>马虎的活儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His report was so slapdash that the editor refused to publish it.  他的报告太马虎，编辑拒绝发表。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 methodical</td><td>/məˈθɑːdɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>井然有序的，有条理的</td><td>a methodical approach<br>有条理的方法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A methodical search of the archive finally produced the missing land records.  对档案的有条理搜查终于找出了缺失的土地记录。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 orderly</td><td>/ˈɔːrdərli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有秩序的，整齐的</td><td>an orderly queue<br>整齐的队伍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students left the hall in an orderly line.  学生们排成整齐的队伍离开礼堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 organized</td><td>/ˈɔːrɡəˌnaɪzd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有条理的，有组织的</td><td>highly organized files<br>条理分明的文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She keeps an organized notebook for every course she takes.  她为每门课都备有一本条理分明的笔记。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 regular</td><td>/ˈreɡjələr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>规律的，通常的</td><td>on a regular basis<br>定期地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He goes swimming on a regular basis every week.  他每周都定期去游泳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>strenuous</b></td><td>/ˈstrenjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，艰巨的；奋力的</td><td>strenuous exercise<br>费力的运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors advised him to avoid strenuous exercise for at least a month.  医生建议他至少一个月内避免费力的运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggressive</td><td>/əˈɡresɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的；积极进取的，强有力的</td><td>an aggressive marketing campaign<br>强势的营销活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog became aggressive whenever strangers approached the gate.  只要有陌生人靠近大门，这只狗就变得有攻击性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dynamic</td><td>/daɪˈnæmɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有活力的，动态的</td><td>a dynamic leader<br>有活力的领导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city has a dynamic arts scene that keeps changing.  这座城市有着不断变化的活跃艺术圈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 energetic</td><td>/ˌenərˈdʒetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的</td><td>an energetic performance<br>活力四射的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even at seventy she remains remarkably energetic and full of plans.  即使七十岁了，她依然精力充沛，计划满满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forceful</td><td>/ˈfɔːrsfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强有力的，有说服力的</td><td>a forceful argument<br>有力的论据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a forceful case for reforming the outdated grading system.  她为改革过时的评分制度提出了有力的论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vigorous</td><td>/ˈvɪɡərəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力旺盛的，强有力的</td><td>vigorous exercise<br>剧烈运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors recommend thirty minutes of vigorous exercise daily.  医生建议每天进行三十分钟的高强度运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arduous</td><td>/ˈɑːrdʒuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以做到的，费劲的</td><td>an arduous journey<br>艰苦的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The climb proved far more arduous than any of them expected.  这次攀登远比他们任何人预想的都费劲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 challenging</td><td>/ˈtʃæləndʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有挑战性的，费心力的</td><td>a challenging assignment<br>有挑战性的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor gave us a challenging assignment due next week.  教授给了我们一个下周交的有挑战性作业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demanding</td><td>/dɪˈmændɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难取悦的，难满足的；费时间花心思的</td><td>a demanding new job<br>费心费力的新工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Caring for three small children is demanding work with no holidays.  照顾三个小孩是费时间花心思且没有休假的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exacting</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæktɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，苛求的；费时间花心思的</td><td>exacting quality standards<br>严格的质量标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline maintains exacting standards for every part it installs.  这家航空公司对安装的每个部件都保持严格的标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 formidable</td><td>/ˈfɔːrmɪdəbl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以对付的，令人敬畏的</td><td>a formidable opponent<br>难以对付的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She faced a formidable opponent in the final round.  她在最后一轮遇到了难以对付的对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grueling</td><td>/ˈɡruːəlɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>累人的，折磨人的</td><td>a grueling schedule<br>折磨人的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team endured a grueling schedule of twelve games in three weeks.  球队熬过了三周十二场比赛这种折磨人的赛程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laborious</td><td>/ləˈbɔːriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，艰苦的；勤奋的</td><td>a laborious process<br>费力的过程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Copying the manuscript by hand proved a slow and laborious process.  手抄这份手稿是一个缓慢而费力的过程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 severe</td><td>/səˈvɪr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的；严重的</td><td>a severe penalty<br>严厉的处罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge imposed a severe penalty on the repeat offender.  法官对累犯施以严厉处罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 toilsome</del></td><td>/ˈtɔɪlsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>辛苦的，费力的</td><td>toilsome labor<br>辛苦的劳作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Copying manuscripts by hand was toilsome work that ruined many eyes.  手抄书稿是毁坏许多人眼睛的辛苦活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tough</td><td>/ˈtʌf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>艰难的，费力的；坚韧的</td><td>a tough assignment<br>艰巨的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editing that manuscript proved a tough assignment.  编辑那份手稿被证明是项艰巨的任务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 easy</td><td>/ˈiːzi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易的，不费力的</td><td>an easy question<br>简单的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The homework looked easy, but it took him three hours to finish.  作业看起来容易，可他花了三个小时才做完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 effortless</td><td>/ˈefərtləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻松的，不费力的</td><td>effortless grace<br>毫不费力的优雅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her swimming looked effortless even in the rough water.  即使在波涛中她的游泳也显得毫不费力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 facile</td><td>/ˈfæsl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易做到的；肤浅轻率的</td><td>a facile explanation<br>肤浅的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report offers a facile explanation for a very complex problem.  这份报告对一个非常复杂的问题给出了肤浅的解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undemanding</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈmændɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>要求不高的，轻松的</td><td>an undemanding course<br>要求不高的课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She chose an undemanding role so she could rest more.  她选了个轻松的角色以便多休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>striate</b></td><td>/ˈstraɪeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使有条纹；刻出沟纹</td><td>striate the rock surface<br>在岩面上加条纹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ancient glaciers striated the rock surface across the whole valley.  古代冰川在整个山谷的岩石表面刻出了条纹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 furrow</td><td>/ˈfɜːroʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>犁沟，皱纹；使起皱</td><td>furrow one's brow<br>皱起眉头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He furrowed his brow as he studied the confusing map.  他研究那张令人困惑的地图时皱起了眉头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 streak</td><td>/ˈstriːk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>条纹，一道；加条纹</td><td>a streak of light<br>一道光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A streak of lightning lit up the whole valley for a second.  一道闪电瞬间照亮了整个山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stripe</td><td>/ˈstraɪp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>条纹，斑纹</td><td>black and white stripes<br>黑白条纹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The zebra's black and white stripes make it hard to see at dusk.  斑马的黑白条纹使它在黄昏时难以被发现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stricture</b></td><td>/ˈstrɪktʃər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>责难，批评</td><td>harsh strictures on<br>对…的严厉批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The review contained harsh strictures on the author's careless research.  评论对作者草率的研究提出严厉批评。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 censure</td><td>/ˈsenʃər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>公开表示反对，谴责</td><td>a vote of censure<br>谴责性投票</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council voted to censure the mayor for his conduct.  议会投票谴责市长的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 condemnation</td><td>/ˌkɑːndemˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谴责，指责</td><td>widespread condemnation<br>广泛的谴责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The raid drew swift condemnation from neighboring states.  这次袭击迅速招致邻国的谴责。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 denunciation</td><td>/dɪˌnʌnsiˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谴责，公开指责</td><td>a public denunciation<br>公开的谴责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her speech was a fierce denunciation of the new policy.  她的演讲是对新政策的猛烈谴责。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excoriation</td><td>/ɪkˌskɔːriˈeɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严厉指责，痛斥</td><td>public excoriation<br>公开的痛斥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editorial was an excoriation of the mayor's housing policy.  这篇社论是对市长住房政策的痛斥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 obloquy</del></td><td>/ˈɑːbləkwi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谩骂，诽谤</td><td>suffer public obloquy<br>遭受公众谩骂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The former minister suffered public obloquy for years after the scandal broke.  丑闻爆发后，这位前部长多年遭受公众谩骂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rebuke</td><td>/rɪˈbjuːk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>斥责，责难</td><td>a sharp rebuke<br>严厉的斥责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge rebuked the lawyer for interrupting the witness.  法官斥责律师打断证人发言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprimand</td><td>/ˈreprəˌmænd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>训斥，正式谴责</td><td>reprimand an employee<br>训斥员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was reprimanded for leaving his post early.  这名警官因提早离岗而受到训斥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reproach</td><td>/rɪˈproʊtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批评，责备</td><td>reproach oneself for<br>因某事自责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She reproached him for never returning her calls.  她责备他从不回她的电话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reproof</td><td>/rɪˈpruːf/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>批评，责备</td><td>a gentle reproof<br>温和的责备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single glance from her served as a silent reproof.  她的一个眼神就成了无声的责备。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 commendation</td><td>/ˌkɑːmənˈdeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>表扬，嘉奖</td><td>receive a commendation for bravery<br>因勇敢获得嘉奖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer received a commendation for rescuing the trapped driver.  这名警官因救出被困司机而获嘉奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eulogy</td><td>/ˈjuːlədʒi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>悼词；颂词，赞辞</td><td>deliver a moving eulogy<br>致感人的悼词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She delivered a moving eulogy at her grandfather's funeral.  她在祖父的葬礼上致了感人的悼词。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>soothsayer</b></td><td>/ˈsuːθseɪər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>预言家</td><td>consult a soothsayer<br>求问预言家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nervous king consulted a soothsayer before launching the long campaign.  紧张的国王在发动这场持久战役前求问预言家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 augur</td><td>/ˈɔːɡər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>预言家，占卜官；预言</td><td>augur well for the future<br>预示前景良好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The Roman augur studied the flight of birds before every battle.  这位罗马占卜官在每场战役前都观察鸟的飞行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diviner</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪnər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>占卜者，预言者</td><td>a village diviner<br>村里的占卜者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tribe asked the old diviner to read the pattern of bones.  部落请老占卜者解读骨头的排列。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forecaster</td><td>/ˈfɔːrˌkæstər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>预报员，预测者</td><td>a weather forecaster<br>天气预报员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The weather forecaster warned that a heavy storm would arrive by dawn.  天气预报员警告说一场暴风雨将在黎明前到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 foreteller</del></td><td>/fɔːrˈtelər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>预言者</td><td>a foreteller of doom<br>灾难的预言者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics dismissed him as a foreteller of disasters that never came.  批评者把他当成预言从未发生的灾难的人而不予理睬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prognosticator</td><td>/prɑːɡˈnɑːstɪkeɪtər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>预言家，预测者</td><td>a market prognosticator<br>市场预测者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every prognosticator on television predicted a different winner this year.  电视上每位预测者今年都预测了不同的赢家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prophet</td><td>/ˈprɑːfət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>先知，预言家</td><td>a prophet of doom<br>末日预言者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old prophet warned the city that its walls would fall.  老先知警告这座城市，它的城墙将会倒塌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 visionary</td><td>/ˈvɪʒəneri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有远见的；不切实际的，空想的</td><td>a visionary founder<br>有远见的创始人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their visionary founder predicted the mobile market twenty years early.  他们有远见的创始人提前二十年预见了移动市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 7

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>strident</b></td><td>/ˈstraɪdnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺耳的</td><td>a strident voice<br>刺耳的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her strident voice cut through the noise of the crowded hall.  她刺耳的嗓音穿透拥挤大厅里的喧闹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grating</td><td>/ˈɡreɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺耳的，令人不适的</td><td>a grating voice<br>刺耳的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The grating noise of the saw filled the workshop.  锯子刺耳的声音充满了作坊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harsh</td><td>/hɑːrʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的</td><td>harsh criticism<br>严厉的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge's harsh sentence surprised even the prosecutors in the courtroom.  法官严厉的判决连法庭上的检察官都感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hoarse</td><td>/ˈhɔːrs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>嘶哑的，粗哑的</td><td>shout oneself hoarse<br>喊得嗓子沙哑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After cheering all evening, her voice was hoarse and barely audible.  欢呼了一整晚后，她的声音沙哑得几乎听不见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jarring</td><td>/ˈdʒɑːrɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺耳的</td><td>a jarring noise<br>刺耳的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A jarring noise from the construction site woke the whole neighborhood early.  工地传来的刺耳噪音很早就吵醒了整个街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rasping</td><td>/ˈræspɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺耳的，沙哑的</td><td>a rasping cough<br>沙哑的咳嗽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A rasping whisper came from behind the heavy curtain.  厚窗帘后传来一声沙哑的低语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 raucous</td><td>/ˈrɔːkəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺耳的，喧闹的</td><td>raucous laughter<br>刺耳的大笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Raucous laughter spilled out of the crowded pub.  刺耳的笑声从挤满人的酒吧里传出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 squawky</del></td><td>/ˈskwɔːki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音刺耳的，尖叫般的</td><td>a squawky loudspeaker<br>刺耳的扩音器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The squawky old radio made every song sound painful to hear.  那台刺耳的旧收音机让每首歌听起来都很难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mellifluous</td><td>/meˈlɪfluəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（声音）甜美流畅的</td><td>a mellifluous voice<br>甜美悦耳的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The narrator's mellifluous voice made even the dullest chapters pleasant to listen to.  叙述者优美悦耳的嗓音让最枯燥的章节也变得动听。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>strike</b></td><td>/straɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>攻击；撞击</td><td>strike a heavy blow<br>猛击一下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boxer tried to strike a heavy blow before the bell rang.  拳手想在铃响前猛击一拳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggression</td><td>/əˈɡreʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>侵略，侵犯；攻击性</td><td>an act of aggression<br>侵略行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The border raid was widely condemned as naked aggression.  这次边境突袭被广泛谴责为赤裸裸的侵略。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assault</td><td>/əˈsɔːlt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>攻击，袭击</td><td>launch an assault<br>发动攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Troops launched a night assault on the fortress.  部队对要塞发动了夜间进攻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 charge</td><td>/ˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>指控；费用；充电</td><td>face criminal charges<br>面临刑事指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prosecutor charged him with fraud after months of investigation.  经过数月调查，检察官指控他欺诈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 offense</td><td>/əˈfens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>冒犯，得罪；犯罪</td><td>take offense at<br>因……而生气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took offense at his joke about her accent.  她因他拿她的口音开玩笑而生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 onset</td><td>/ˈɑːnset/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>起始，开始</td><td>the onset of winter<br>冬天的开始</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The onset of winter caught the expedition without adequate shelter or fuel.  冬天的到来让探险队没有足够的庇护所和燃料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 onslaught</td><td>/ˈɑːnˌslɔːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>猛攻，猛烈袭击</td><td>a fierce onslaught<br>猛烈的攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village could not survive another onslaught from the enemy cavalry.  村庄无法再承受敌方骑兵的一次猛攻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 raid</td><td>/ˈreɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>突袭，袭击</td><td>an air raid<br>一次空袭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soldiers raided the camp shortly after midnight.  士兵在午夜过后不久突袭了营地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rush</td><td>/rʌʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>匆忙，急速行动；催促</td><td>rush to the airport<br>赶往机场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They rushed to the airport and barely caught the flight.  他们赶往机场，勉强赶上了航班。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bat</td><td>/ˈbæt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>反复讨论；球棒，击球</td><td>bat an idea around<br>反复讨论一个想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We batted the proposal around for an hour without deciding.  我们把这个提案反复讨论了一小时也没决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beat</td><td>/ˈbiːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>打，击；击败</td><td>beat him mercilessly<br>无情地打他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guards beat the prisoner until the officer finally intervened.  卫兵殴打囚犯，直到那位军官出面干预。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hammer</td><td>/ˈhæmər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>屡次重申</td><td>hammer home the point<br>反复强调这一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach hammered home the importance of defense at every single practice.  教练在每一次训练中都反复强调防守的重要性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hit</td><td>/ˈhɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>成功而流行的事物；打击</td><td>a smash hit<br>轰动一时的成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first single became an unexpected hit across three countries.  她的首支单曲在三个国家意外大热。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 knock</td><td>/ˈnɑːk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>批评，指责；敲击</td><td>knock his performance<br>批评他的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics knocked the film for its careless historical detail.  评论者批评这部电影历史细节草率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nail</td><td>/ˈneɪl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>钉住；猛击；钉子</td><td>nail the board down<br>把木板钉牢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He nailed the loose board down before someone tripped over it.  他在有人绊倒之前把松动的木板钉牢了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 punch</td><td>/ˈpʌntʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>用拳猛击</td><td>punch a hole in it<br>在上面打个洞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He punched the wall and broke two knuckles.  他猛击墙壁，弄断了两根指骨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swat</td><td>/ˈswɑːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>猛击，拍打</td><td>swat a fly<br>拍打苍蝇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She swatted the mosquito that had been buzzing near her ear.  她拍死了一直在她耳边嗡嗡叫的蚊子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assail</td><td>/əˈseɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抨击，严厉批评</td><td>assail a policy<br>抨击某项政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Opponents assailed the plan as reckless and unfair.  反对者抨击该计划轻率且不公。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beset</td><td>/bɪˈset/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使苦恼，骚扰；攻击，袭击</td><td>beset by doubts<br>为疑虑所困</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young author was beset by doubts about his second novel.  这位年轻作者被对第二部小说的疑虑所困扰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 storm</td><td>/ˈstɔːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>暴风雨；一阵猛烈（如抗议）</td><td>a storm of protest<br>一阵抗议风暴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The decision caused a storm of protest on social media.  这一决定在社交媒体上引发抗议风暴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 set on</td><td>/set ˈɑːn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>攻击，袭击</td><td>set on a stranger<br>袭击陌生人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guard dogs were set on the intruder the moment he climbed in.  他一翻进来，看守犬就被放出去攻击他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bang</td><td>/ˈbæŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>猛击，猛撞；砰的一声</td><td>bang on the door<br>猛敲门</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Someone began to bang on the door in the middle of the night.  半夜有人开始猛敲门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bash</td><td>/bæʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抨击，严厉批评</td><td>bash the media<br>抨击媒体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Politicians love to bash the press at rallies.  政客们喜欢在集会上抨击新闻界。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bump</td><td>/ˈbʌmp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>碰撞，撞击</td><td>bump into a table<br>撞到桌子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He bumped into the table and knocked over two glasses of wine.  他撞到桌子，打翻了两杯酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collide</td><td>/kəˈlaɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>碰撞，相撞</td><td>collide with a truck<br>与卡车相撞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two cyclists collided at the corner but neither was badly hurt.  两个骑车人在拐角处相撞，但都没受重伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crash</td><td>/ˈkræʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>暴跌；撞毁</td><td>share prices crash<br>股价暴跌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Share prices crashed within hours of the announcement.  公告发布后几小时内股价暴跌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impact</td><td>/ˈɪmpækt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>撞击，冲击；影响</td><td>the point of impact<br>撞击点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The meteor's impact left a crater clearly visible from the air.  流星的撞击留下了一个从空中清晰可见的坑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impinge</td><td>/ɪmˈpɪndʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>撞击；侵犯，妨碍</td><td>impinge on our rights<br>侵犯我们的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New regulations impinge on the freedom of small local newspapers.  新规定侵犯了地方小报的自由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ram</td><td>/ˈræm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>猛撞，猛击</td><td>ram the gate open<br>撞开大门</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The truck rammed the barrier and crashed into the ditch beyond.  卡车撞穿护栏，冲进了后面的沟里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slam</td><td>/ˈslæm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉批评；猛击</td><td>slam the proposal<br>猛烈批评该提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics slammed the film for its careless plotting.  评论家因情节草率而猛烈批评这部电影。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smash</td><td>/ˈsmæʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>轰动的成功；打碎</td><td>a box-office smash<br>票房大热之作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The low-budget comedy became a box-office smash overnight.  这部低成本喜剧一夜之间成为票房大热。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swipe</td><td>/ˈswaɪp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>猛击，挥击</td><td>take a swipe at<br>朝…挥击一下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bear took a swipe at the fish but missed completely.  熊朝那条鱼挥了一爪，却完全没打中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thud</td><td>/ˈθʌd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>砰的一声重击；发出重击声</td><td>land with a thud<br>砰的一声落地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heavy book fell off the shelf and landed with a thud.  那本厚书从书架上掉下来，砰的一声落地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stringent</b></td><td>/ˈstrɪndʒənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的</td><td>stringent safety standards<br>严格的安全标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory now follows stringent safety standards set by the government.  这家工厂现在遵守政府制定的严格安全标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 draconian</td><td>/drəˈkoʊniən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极其残酷的；十分严厉的</td><td>draconian punishment<br>严酷的惩罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such draconian punishment for a minor offense shocked the entire village.  对轻微过错施以如此严酷的惩罚，震惊了全村。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exacting</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæktɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，苛求的；费时间花心思的</td><td>exacting quality standards<br>严格的质量标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline maintains exacting standards for every part it installs.  这家航空公司对安装的每个部件都保持严格的标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 ironhanded</del></td><td>/ˌaɪərnˈhændɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>铁腕的，严酷的</td><td>ironhanded rule<br>铁腕统治</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The governor kept order through ironhanded control of the press.  这位总督通过铁腕控制新闻来维持秩序。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rigorous</td><td>/ˈrɪɡərəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（天气）严酷的；严格的</td><td>a rigorous climate<br>严酷的气候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few crops survive the rigorous winters of the plateau.  很少有作物能度过高原严酷的冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 severe</td><td>/səˈvɪr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的；严重的</td><td>a severe penalty<br>严厉的处罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge imposed a severe penalty on the repeat offender.  法官对累犯施以严厉处罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strict</td><td>/ˈstrɪkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，严厉的</td><td>strict rules<br>严格的规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents were strict about homework and bedtime.  她父母对作业和睡觉时间很严格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncompromising</td><td>/ʌnˈkɑːmprəmaɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不妥协的，坚定的</td><td>an uncompromising stance<br>毫不妥协的立场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took an uncompromising stand against corruption in the department.  她对部门内的腐败采取了毫不妥协的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lenient</td><td>/ˈliːniənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽大仁慈的</td><td>a lenient sentence<br>从轻的判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge handed down a lenient sentence because of the boy's age.  由于这个男孩年纪小，法官作出了从轻的判决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>strip</b></td><td>/strɪp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>剥去，剥光（装饰等）</td><td>strip the walls bare<br>把墙剥得光秃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers stripped the walls bare before applying the fresh coat of paint.  工人把墙剥得光秃，然后刷上新漆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 denude</del></td><td>/dɪˈnuːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>脱去，使赤裸</td><td>denude the hillside of trees<br>使山坡树木尽失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long drought denuded the hillside of grass and young trees.  长期干旱使山坡上的草和幼树消失殆尽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disrobe</td><td>/dɪsˈroʊb/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>脱衣，使脱去衣服</td><td>disrobe before bathing<br>沐浴前脱衣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patients were asked to disrobe behind the curtain.  病人被要求在帘子后面脱下衣服。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doff</td><td>/dɑːf/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>脱下</td><td>doff one's hat<br>脱帽致意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Visitors were expected to doff their hats upon entering the small chapel.  参观者进入这座小礼拜堂时须脱帽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unclothe</del></td><td>/ʌnˈkloʊð/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使脱衣，剥去覆盖物</td><td>unclothe the sleeping child<br>给睡着的孩子脱衣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gently unclothed the sleeping baby to change his diaper.  她轻轻给睡着的婴儿脱下衣服换尿布。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undress</td><td>/ʌnˈdres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>脱衣；使显露</td><td>undress before the mirror<br>在镜子前脱衣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She undressed quickly and slipped into the warm bath.  她迅速脱掉衣服，滑进温暖的浴缸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dress</td><td>/ˈdres/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>装饰，布置；穿衣</td><td>dress the shop window<br>布置商店橱窗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dressed the shop window with silk scarves and dried flowers.  她用丝巾和干花布置了商店橱窗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gown</td><td>/ˈɡaʊn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>长袍；给…穿上长袍</td><td>a long evening gown<br>长款晚礼服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurses gowned the surgeon before he entered the operating room.  护士们在外科医生进手术室前为他穿上手术袍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 robe</td><td>/ˈroʊb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>长袍，罩衣；给…穿袍</td><td>a judge's black robe<br>法官的黑袍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge put on his black robe before entering the courtroom.  法官进入法庭前穿上了黑色法袍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>strut</b></td><td>/strʌt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>趾高气扬地走</td><td>strut across the stage<br>趾高气扬地走过舞台</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The winner strutted across the stage with his trophy held high.  获胜者高举奖杯，趾高气扬地走过舞台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parade</td><td>/pəˈreɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>炫耀；游行</td><td>parade one's knowledge<br>炫耀自己的学识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She never paraded her achievements before her classmates.  她从不在同学面前炫耀成绩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prance</td><td>/ˈpræns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>昂首阔步走；欢跃</td><td>prance around the room<br>在房间里昂首走动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The winner pranced around the room showing off his new trophy.  获胜者在房间里趾高气扬地走来走去，炫耀新奖杯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stalk</td><td>/ˈstɔːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>高视阔步地走；潜行追踪</td><td>stalk out of the room<br>气冲冲地走出房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stalked out of the meeting without answering a single question.  他没回答任何问题就气冲冲地走出了会议室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swagger</td><td>/ˈswæɡər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>趾高气扬地走；自夸</td><td>swagger about one's success<br>炫耀自己的成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He swaggered into the room after winning the bowling championship.  赢得保龄球锦标赛后，他大摇大摆地走进房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spike</b></td><td>/ˈspaɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>激起，使猛增</td><td>spike public interest<br>激起公众兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary spiked public interest in the region's vanishing wildlife.  这部纪录片激起公众对该地区濒危野生动物的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brace</td><td>/ˈbreɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使振作，鼓励；支撑</td><td>brace yourself for impact<br>做好承受冲击准备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cup of hot tea braced him for the long night.  一杯热茶让他为漫长的夜晚振作起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 energize</td><td>/ˈenərdʒaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使充满活力，激励</td><td>energize the whole team<br>使整个团队振奋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new coach energized a team that had lost hope.  新教练让失去希望的球队重新振奋起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enliven</td><td>/ɪnˈlaɪvn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使活跃，使生动</td><td>enliven a dull party<br>让沉闷的聚会活跃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her jokes enlivened an otherwise dull afternoon meeting.  她的玩笑让本来沉闷的下午会议活跃起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invigorate</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪɡəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使精神，使强壮，鼓舞激励</td><td>invigorate the local economy<br>振兴当地经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk in cold air always invigorates my tired mind.  在冷空气中短暂散步总能让我疲惫的头脑重新振作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 liven</td><td>/ˈlaɪvən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使活跃，使有生气</td><td>liven up the party<br>使聚会活跃起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A little music would liven up this dull afternoon meeting.  一点音乐会让这个沉闷的下午会议活跃起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 animate</td><td>/ˈænəmeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使有生气，激励；有活力的</td><td>animate the discussion<br>让讨论活跃起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A few well-chosen questions can animate an otherwise dull discussion.  几个精心挑选的问题就能让本来沉闷的讨论活跃起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stimulate</td><td>/ˈstɪmjəˌleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，激发；促进</td><td>stimulate economic growth<br>促进经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Warm rain stimulated the seeds to sprout within a week.  温暖的雨水刺激种子一周内发芽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vitalize</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赋予活力，激励</td><td>vitalize the local economy<br>使当地经济充满活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New investment vitalized the sleepy town within just two years.  新投资在两年内使这个沉寂的小镇焕发活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 vivify</del></td><td>/ˈvɪvɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使生动，赋予生命</td><td>vivify a dull narrative<br>使乏味的叙述生动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vivid dialogue vivifies an otherwise dull historical narrative.  生动的对话使原本乏味的历史叙述活了起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 damp</td><td>/ˈdæmp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>抑制，使沮丧；潮湿的</td><td>damp their enthusiasm<br>打消他们的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain did not damp our enthusiasm for the trip.  下雨并没有打消我们对这次旅行的热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dampen</td><td>/ˈdæmpən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，压抑（感情、精力等），泼冷水</td><td>dampen his enthusiasm<br>打击他的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Losing the first game did nothing to dampen the team's spirits.  首场失利丝毫没有打击球队的士气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deaden</td><td>/ˈdedən/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使减弱，使麻木</td><td>deaden the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick carpets deaden the sound of footsteps upstairs.  厚地毯减弱了楼上的脚步声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>stultify</b></del></td><td>/ˈstʌltɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使无效，抑制</td><td>stultify a reform effort<br>使改革努力失效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Endless paperwork stultified every reform effort the new director proposed.  没完没了的文书工作使新主任提出的每项改革都失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 constipate</del></td><td>/ˈkɑːnstəˌpeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使便秘；使呆滞，抑制</td><td>constipate the whole process<br>使整个流程僵滞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Endless paperwork constipated the once lively research department.  无尽的文书工作使曾经活跃的研究部门变得呆滞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stagnate</td><td>/ˈstæɡˌneɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>停滞，不发展</td><td>stagnate for years<br>多年停滞不前</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wages stagnated for a decade while housing prices kept climbing.  工资停滞了十年，而房价却持续攀升。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stifle</td><td>/ˈstaɪfl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制（声音、呼吸等）；阻止，扼杀</td><td>stifle a yawn<br>忍住哈欠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She tried to stifle a yawn while the speaker droned on.  演讲者絮絮不休，她努力忍住哈欠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trammel</td><td>/ˈtræməl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>束缚，限制</td><td>the trammels of custom<br>习俗的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old rules trammel the creativity of young designers here.  旧规矩束缚了这里年轻设计师的创造力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 encourage</td><td>/ɪnˈkɜːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓励，激励；促进</td><td>encourage students to read<br>鼓励学生阅读</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers encourage students to ask difficult questions.  好老师鼓励学生提出有难度的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foster</td><td>/ˈfɔːstər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促进，鼓励，培养</td><td>foster cooperation<br>促进合作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new program fosters cooperation between local schools and museums.  新项目促进当地学校与博物馆之间的合作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nourish</td><td>/ˈnɜːrɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>培养，促进</td><td>nourish young talent<br>培养年轻人才</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The foundation exists to nourish young talent in music and the visual arts.  这家基金会的存在是为了培养音乐和视觉艺术方面的年轻人才。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stupor</b></td><td>/ˈstuːpər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迟钝，麻痹，无知觉</td><td>fall into a stupor<br>陷入麻木状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fell into a stupor and did not respond to our questions.  他陷入麻木状态，对我们的问题毫无反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coma</td><td>/ˈkoʊmə/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>昏迷，深度无知觉</td><td>fall into a coma<br>陷入昏迷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient fell into a coma after the second surgery.  病人在第二次手术后陷入昏迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dullness</td><td>/ˈdʌlnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>乏味，单调；迟钝</td><td>the dullness of the lecture<br>讲座的乏味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dullness of the report hid several important findings.  这份报告的乏味掩盖了几项重要发现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 languor</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>懒惰；衰弱</td><td>summer languor<br>夏日的倦怠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heavy heat brought a languor that made work nearly impossible.  酷热带来一种倦怠，几乎使人无法工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lethargy</td><td>/ˈleθərdʒi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>无生气，倦怠，昏睡</td><td>shake off his lethargy<br>摆脱他的倦怠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk helped him shake off his afternoon lethargy.  短短的散步帮他摆脱了午后的倦怠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lassitude</td><td>/ˈlæsɪtuːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>疲乏，无精打采</td><td>a feeling of lassitude<br>一种倦怠感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange lassitude kept him in bed all afternoon.  一种奇怪的倦怠让他整个下午都躺在床上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 listlessness</td><td>/ˈlɪstləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倦怠，无精打采</td><td>an air of listlessness<br>一副无精打采的样子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange listlessness settled over the office in August.  八月里办公室弥漫着一种奇怪的倦怠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 torpidity</del></td><td>/tɔːrˈpɪdəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迟钝，麻木</td><td>sink into torpidity<br>陷入麻木迟钝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Winter torpidity kept the frogs buried in the mud.  冬日的麻木状态让青蛙埋在泥中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torpor</td><td>/ˈtɔːrpər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏兴趣；（肉体或精神上的）迟钝，懒散</td><td>sink into torpor<br>陷入迟钝麻木</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat sank the whole village into a heavy afternoon torpor.  暑热让整个村子陷入沉沉的午后麻木。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 alertness</td><td>/əˈlɜːrtnəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>警觉，机敏</td><td>a state of alertness<br>警觉状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strong coffee restored his alertness during the long night shift.  浓咖啡在漫长的夜班中恢复了他的警觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vigilance</td><td>/ˈvɪdʒələns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>警惕，警戒</td><td>constant vigilance<br>时刻保持警惕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant vigilance kept the small border town safe for years.  持续的警惕使这个边境小镇多年平安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stunt</b></td><td>/stʌnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍（成长）</td><td>stunt the growth<br>阻碍生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor nutrition can stunt the growth of children in their early years.  营养不良会阻碍儿童早年的生长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 check</td><td>/tʃek/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使突然停止，阻止；检查，核对</td><td>check the spread of disease<br>阻止疾病蔓延</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strict quarantine measures checked the spread of the disease.  严格的隔离措施阻止了疾病的蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 curb</td><td>/ˈkɜːrb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>控制，约束</td><td>curb government spending<br>控制政府开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city introduced new rules to curb traffic downtown.  市政府出台新规以限制市区交通。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dwarf</td><td>/ˈdwɔːrf/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使显得矮小；抑制生长</td><td>dwarf nearby buildings<br>使附近建筑显得矮小</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new tower dwarfs every other building in the old district.  新塔楼使老城区其他所有建筑都显得矮小。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suppress</td><td>/səˈpres/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>用暴力终止，镇压；封锁，删除</td><td>suppress a rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime used the army to suppress a rebellion in the capital.  该政权动用军队镇压首都的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 advance</td><td>/ədˈvæns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>前进；促进；预付款</td><td>advance a theory<br>提出一种理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The army advanced slowly through the thick forest at dawn.  黎明时军队缓慢穿过密林前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 boost</td><td>/ˈbuːst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>提高，促进；推动</td><td>boost sales<br>促进销售</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax cut is expected to boost consumer spending.  减税预计会促进消费者支出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foster</td><td>/ˈfɔːstər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促进，鼓励，培养</td><td>foster cooperation<br>促进合作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new program fosters cooperation between local schools and museums.  新项目促进当地学校与博物馆之间的合作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nourish</td><td>/ˈnɜːrɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>培养，促进</td><td>nourish young talent<br>培养年轻人才</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The foundation exists to nourish young talent in music and the visual arts.  这家基金会的存在是为了培养音乐和视觉艺术方面的年轻人才。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nurture</td><td>/ˈnɜːrtʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>培育，培养</td><td>nurture young talent<br>培养年轻人才</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers nurture curiosity rather than simply rewarding correct answers.  好老师培育好奇心，而不只是奖励正确答案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 promote</td><td>/prəˈmoʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促进，助长；晋升</td><td>promote economic growth<br>促进经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign aims to promote healthy eating among children.  该活动旨在促进儿童健康饮食。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sturdy</b></td><td>/ˈstɜːrdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强健的，结实的</td><td>a sturdy wooden table<br>结实的木桌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sturdy wooden table stood in the middle of the farmhouse kitchen.  农舍厨房中间摆着一张结实的木桌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 durable</td><td>/ˈdʊrəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>耐用的，持久的</td><td>durable materials<br>耐用的材料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These boots are made of remarkably durable leather.  这双靴子由极其耐用的皮革制成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 firm</td><td>/ˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，坚固的</td><td>a firm decision<br>坚定的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained firm despite enormous pressure from the board.  尽管董事会施加巨大压力，她依然坚定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 robust</td><td>/roʊˈbʌst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的；强壮的，健康的</td><td>robust good health<br>强健的好身体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains robust enough to work in the garden daily.  他八十岁了，身体依然强健，每天还能在园中劳作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rugged</td><td>/ˈrʌɡəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚毅的；粗糙不平的</td><td>rugged terrain<br>险峻的地形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only rugged travelers attempt that trail in the winter.  只有意志坚毅的旅行者才会在冬天走那条小径。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stalwart</td><td>/ˈstɔːlwərt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定忠诚的；强壮的</td><td>a stalwart supporter<br>坚定的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained a stalwart supporter of the project through every setback.  历经挫折，她始终是这个项目的坚定支持者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stout</td><td>/ˈstaʊt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，勇敢的；粗壮的</td><td>stout resistance<br>顽强的抵抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The townspeople put up a stout defense against the raiders.  镇民对袭击者进行了顽强的抵抗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strong</td><td>/strɔːŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强壮的，坚固的</td><td>strong arms<br>强壮的臂膀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge is strong enough to carry heavy trucks.  这座桥足够坚固，能承载重型卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sound</td><td>/saʊnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>牢固的；健康强壮的；（逻辑）严谨的</td><td>sound financial advice<br>稳妥的理财建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lawyer gave sound financial advice that saved the family thousands.  她的律师给出稳妥的理财建议，为家里省下数千元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tough</td><td>/ˈtʌf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>艰难的，费力的；坚韧的</td><td>a tough assignment<br>艰巨的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editing that manuscript proved a tough assignment.  编辑那份手稿被证明是项艰巨的任务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vigorous</td><td>/ˈvɪɡərəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力旺盛的，强有力的</td><td>vigorous exercise<br>剧烈运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors recommend thirty minutes of vigorous exercise daily.  医生建议每天进行三十分钟的高强度运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delicate</td><td>/ˈdeləkət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微妙的，精细的；脆弱的</td><td>a delicate distinction<br>微妙的区别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a delicate ear for the subtle differences between dialects.  她对方言之间的细微差别有很灵敏的耳朵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 feeble</td><td>/ˈfiːbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的</td><td>a feeble voice<br>微弱的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man answered in a feeble voice from the bed.  老人从床上用微弱的声音回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frail</td><td>/freɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的；脆弱的</td><td>a frail old woman<br>体弱的老妇人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A frail old woman waited alone at the crowded bus stop.  一位虚弱的老妇人独自等在拥挤的公交站。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 weak</td><td>/ˈwiːk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脆弱的，虚弱的</td><td>a weak structure<br>脆弱的结构</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge has a weak structure and cannot bear trucks.  这座桥结构脆弱，无法承载卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wimpy</td><td>/ˈwɪmpi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>软弱的，无胆量的</td><td>a wimpy handshake<br>软弱无力的握手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His wimpy excuse convinced nobody in the crowded room.  他软弱无力的借口没能说服拥挤房间里的任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stygian</b></td><td>/ˈstɪdʒiən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极阴暗的</td><td>stygian darkness<br>极度的黑暗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stygian darkness filled the cave once our last lamp went out.  最后一盏灯熄灭后，洞里一片极度黑暗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 black</td><td>/ˈblæk/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>污黑的，肮脏的；黑色的</td><td>black with soot<br>被煤灰弄得污黑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The miners' hands were black with coal dust.  矿工的手被煤尘弄得污黑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 caliginous</del></td><td>/kəˈlɪdʒɪnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>昏暗的，朦胧的</td><td>a caliginous cellar<br>昏暗的地下室</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They groped through the caliginous tunnel with a single candle.  他们举着一支蜡烛在昏暗的隧道里摸索前行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dark</td><td>/ˈdɑːrk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴郁的；黑暗的</td><td>a dark mood<br>阴郁的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fell into a dark mood after reading the letter.  读了那封信后他陷入阴郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dim</td><td>/ˈdɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>暗淡的，模糊的</td><td>a dim light<br>昏暗的灯光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room was too dim for reading anything but large print.  房间太暗，只能看大字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gloomy</td><td>/ˈɡluːmi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>a gloomy mood<br>忧郁的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew gloomy whenever the winter rain lasted more than a week.  每当冬雨连下一周以上，她就变得忧郁起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pitch-black</td><td>/ˌpɪtʃ ˈblæk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>漆黑的</td><td>a pitch-black night<br>漆黑的夜晚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The power failed and the whole corridor went pitch-black.  停电了，整条走廊变得漆黑一片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bright</td><td>/ˈbraɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快活的，开朗的；明亮的</td><td>a bright smile<br>开朗的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She greeted every customer with a bright and genuine smile.  她用开朗真诚的笑容迎接每位顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 brilliant</td><td>/ˈbrɪljənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>聪颖的；辉煌的</td><td>a brilliant student<br>才华出众的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was a brilliant mathematician long before she turned twenty.  她还没到二十岁就已是出色的数学家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
