<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 illuminated</td><td>/ɪˈluːməneɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>被照亮的，明亮的</td><td>brightly illuminated streets<br>灯火明亮的街道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The illuminated cathedral could be seen from across the river.  被灯光照亮的大教堂在河对岸就能看见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 illumined</td><td>/ɪˈluːmənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>被照亮的，照明的</td><td>a softly illumined room<br>光线柔和的房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The great hall was softly illumined by dozens of candles.  大厅被数十支蜡烛柔和地照亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 light</td><td>/ˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻微的，不严重的</td><td>a light meal<br>一顿便餐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He suffered only light injuries in the accident.  他在事故中只受了轻伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 lightsome</del></td><td>/ˈlaɪtsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻快的，快活的</td><td>a lightsome step<br>轻快的步子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She crossed the room with a lightsome, dancing step.  她以轻快如舞的步子穿过房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lucent</td><td>/ˈluːsɪnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>明亮的，透明的</td><td>lucent morning light<br>明亮的晨光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lake lay lucent under a cloudless autumn sky.  湖水在无云的秋空下清亮透明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lucid</td><td>/ˈluːsɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达清晰的，易懂的</td><td>a lucid explanation<br>清晰的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor offered a lucid summary of a difficult argument.  教授对一个艰深论证做了清晰的总结。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 luminous</td><td>/ˈluːmɪnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>发光的，明亮的</td><td>a luminous dial<br>夜光表盘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The luminous dial of his watch glowed faintly in the dark.  他手表的夜光表盘在黑暗中微微发亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>stymie</b></td><td>/ˈstaɪmi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>stymie an investigation<br>阻碍调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Missing records stymied the investigation for months.  档案缺失使调查停滞了数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encumber</td><td>/ɪnˈkʌmbər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍；妨碍</td><td>encumber the project<br>妨碍这个项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too many rules encumber small businesses in this country.  太多规定阻碍了这个国家的小企业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fetter</td><td>/ˈfetər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>束缚</td><td>the fetters of tradition<br>传统的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy family obligations fettered her ambition for many long years.  沉重的家庭责任多年来束缚了她的雄心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 handcuff</td><td>/ˈhændˌkʌf/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>束缚；给戴手铐</td><td>handcuff the suspect<br>给嫌疑人戴上手铐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officers handcuffed the man before leading him away.  警官在带走这名男子前给他戴上手铐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 handicap</td><td>/ˈhændikæp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>妨碍，使不利；障碍</td><td>handicap the team<br>使球队处于不利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor equipment handicapped the researchers from the very start.  设备不足从一开始就妨碍了研究人员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hinder</td><td>/ˈhɪndər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>hinder progress<br>妨碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor lighting hindered the search for survivors.  照明不足妨碍了对生还者的搜寻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impede</td><td>/ɪmˈpiːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻碍</td><td>impede progress<br>阻碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick fog impeded the rescue team's progress.  浓雾阻碍了救援队的前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inhibit</td><td>/ɪnˈhɪbɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，阻碍</td><td>inhibit the growth of bacteria<br>抑制细菌生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cold weather inhibits the growth of these tropical plants.  寒冷天气抑制了这些热带植物的生长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 manacle</del></td><td>/ˈmænəkl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给…戴上手铐；束缚，限制</td><td>manacle the prisoner<br>给囚犯戴上手铐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy debts manacle the young couple, leaving them no freedom to change careers.  沉重的债务束缚了这对年轻夫妇，让他们没有转行的自由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstruct</td><td>/əbˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻塞</td><td>obstruct justice<br>妨碍司法公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fallen tree obstructed the only road out of town.  一棵倒下的树堵住了唯一的出城道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shackle</td><td>/ˈʃækl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>束缚；限制，阻碍</td><td>shackle free trade<br>束缚自由贸易</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New tariffs would shackle trade between the two neighboring countries.  新关税会束缚这两个邻国之间的贸易。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trammel</td><td>/ˈtræməl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>束缚，限制</td><td>the trammels of custom<br>习俗的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old rules trammel the creativity of young designers here.  旧规矩束缚了这里年轻设计师的创造力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aid</td><td>/ˈeɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>帮助，援助</td><td>come to someone's aid<br>前来帮助某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors came to her aid within minutes of the fire.  火灾发生几分钟内邻居们就来帮忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assist</td><td>/əˈsɪst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，协助</td><td>assist with the work<br>协助工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two nurses assisted the surgeon during the operation.  两名护士在手术中协助医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 facilitate</td><td>/fəˈsɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变容易，促进</td><td>facilitate the discussion<br>促进讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new airport will facilitate tourism throughout the whole region.  新机场将促进整个地区的旅游业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 help</td><td>/ˈhelp/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>帮助，助益</td><td>a great help<br>很大的帮助</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your advice was a great help during the difficult week.  在那困难的一周里你的建议帮了大忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>subdue</b></td><td>/səbˈduː/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使顺从；征服</td><td>subdue the rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Government forces moved south to subdue the rebellion within a week.  政府军南下，一周内镇压了叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conquer</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋkər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>征服，攻克</td><td>conquer a vast territory<br>征服广阔领土</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rome conquered most of the Mediterranean world within two centuries.  罗马在两个世纪内征服了地中海世界的大部分地区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defeat</td><td>/dɪˈfiːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>失败；击败</td><td>admit defeat<br>承认失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After three hours of argument he finally admitted defeat.  争论三小时后他终于承认失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squelch</td><td>/skweltʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压制，镇压（运动）</td><td>squelch the rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Troops moved in quickly to squelch the rebellion in the province.  军队迅速开进，镇压该省的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subjugate</td><td>/ˈsʌbdʒuɡeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>征服，镇压</td><td>subjugate a rebellious province<br>征服叛乱的行省</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empire sent legions to subjugate a rebellious northern province.  帝国派出军团征服叛乱的北方行省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vanquish</td><td>/ˈvæŋkwɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打败，征服</td><td>vanquish the invading army<br>击败入侵的军队</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A smaller force managed to vanquish the invading army in one day.  一支人数较少的部队一天之内就击败了入侵的军队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 capitulate</td><td>/kəˈpɪtʃuleɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>投降，默许</td><td>capitulate to their demands<br>屈从他们的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After weeks of protest the company capitulated to the workers' demands.  抗议数周后，公司向工人的要求屈服了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 surrender</td><td>/səˈrendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>交出，放弃，投降</td><td>surrender to the police<br>向警方投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief finally surrendered to the police after a long chase.  经过长时间追捕，小偷终于向警方投降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>subject</b></td><td>/ˈsʌbdʒekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>取决于（其他因素）的，受制于</td><td>subject to approval<br>须经批准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The entire plan is subject to approval by the city council.  整个方案须经市议会批准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affected</td><td>/əˈfektɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>装出来的，做作的</td><td>an affected accent<br>装出来的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke with an affected accent that fooled nobody present.  她用装出来的口音说话，没骗过在场任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conditional</td><td>/kənˈdɪʃənl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有条件的，受制约的</td><td>a conditional offer<br>有条件的录取通知</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She received a conditional offer from her favorite university.  她收到了心仪大学的有条件录取通知。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contingent</td><td>/kənˈtɪndʒənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>视条件而定的；分遣队</td><td>contingent on approval<br>取决于批准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bonus is contingent on the company meeting its yearly targets.  奖金取决于公司是否达成年度目标。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dependent</td><td>/dɪˈpendənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>依赖的，取决于…的</td><td>dependent on foreign aid<br>依赖外国援助</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village remains dependent on rainfall for its entire harvest.  这个村子的全部收成仍然取决于降雨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reliant</td><td>/rɪˈlaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>依靠的，依赖的</td><td>heavily reliant on imports<br>严重依赖进口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company is heavily reliant on a single overseas supplier.  这家公司严重依赖一个海外供应商。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subordinate</td><td>/səˈbɔːrdənət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>次要的，下级的；使服从</td><td>a subordinate role<br>次要的角色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She refused to accept a subordinate role in the research project.  她不愿在研究项目中接受次要的角色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 independent</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈpendənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>独立的，不受制于…的</td><td>independent of outside funding<br>不依赖外部资金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her research stayed independent of any corporate funding or influence.  她的研究不受任何企业资金或影响的支配。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unconditional</td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈdɪʃənəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无条件的，绝对的</td><td>unconditional surrender<br>无条件投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty demanded the unconditional withdrawal of all foreign troops.  条约要求所有外国军队无条件撤离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>subjugate</b></td><td>/ˈsʌbdʒuɡeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>征服，镇压</td><td>subjugate a rebellious province<br>征服叛乱的行省</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empire sent legions to subjugate a rebellious northern province.  帝国派出军团征服叛乱的北方行省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conquer</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋkər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>征服，攻克</td><td>conquer a vast territory<br>征服广阔领土</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rome conquered most of the Mediterranean world within two centuries.  罗马在两个世纪内征服了地中海世界的大部分地区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dominate</td><td>/ˈdɑːməˌneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>支配，统治；俯视</td><td>dominate the market<br>支配市场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two firms dominate the industry and set nearly every price.  两家公司支配着这个行业，几乎决定所有价格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overpower</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈpaʊər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>制服，压倒</td><td>overpower the guards<br>制服守卫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Three officers finally overpowered the struggling suspect in the hallway.  三名警官最终在走廊里制服了挣扎的嫌疑人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pacify</td><td>/ˈpæsɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使平静，安慰</td><td>pacify a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse sang softly to pacify the frightened child before the injection.  打针前，护士轻声唱歌安抚受惊的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quash</td><td>/kwɔːʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除；镇压，阻止</td><td>quash a conviction<br>撤销有罪判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The appeal court quashed his conviction last week.  上诉法院上周撤销了对他的定罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squelch</td><td>/skweltʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压制，镇压（运动）</td><td>squelch the rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Troops moved in quickly to squelch the rebellion in the province.  军队迅速开进，镇压该省的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subdue</td><td>/səbˈduː/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使顺从；征服</td><td>subdue the rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Government forces moved south to subdue the rebellion within a week.  政府军南下，一周内镇压了叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vanquish</td><td>/ˈvæŋkwɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打败，征服</td><td>vanquish the invading army<br>击败入侵的军队</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A smaller force managed to vanquish the invading army in one day.  一支人数较少的部队一天之内就击败了入侵的军队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 capitulate</td><td>/kəˈpɪtʃuleɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>投降，默许</td><td>capitulate to their demands<br>屈从他们的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After weeks of protest the company capitulated to the workers' demands.  抗议数周后，公司向工人的要求屈服了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 surrender</td><td>/səˈrendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>交出，放弃，投降</td><td>surrender to the police<br>向警方投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief finally surrendered to the police after a long chase.  经过长时间追捕，小偷终于向警方投降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sublime</b></td><td>/səˈblaɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>崇高的；壮丽的；卓越的</td><td>a sublime landscape<br>崇高壮丽的景色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the summit we watched a sublime landscape unfold below us.  从山顶我们看着一片壮丽的景色在下方展开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chivalrous</td><td>/ˈʃɪvəlrəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有骑士风度的，（尤指对女性）彬彬有礼的</td><td>a chivalrous gesture<br>有骑士风度的举动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He made a chivalrous offer to carry her heavy suitcase.  他很有骑士风度地提出替她拿沉重的行李箱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elevated</td><td>/ˈeləˌveɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高尚的，崇高的；升高的</td><td>elevated language<br>高雅的语言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speech used elevated language that moved the crowd.  这篇演讲用高雅的语言感动了人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gallant</td><td>/ˈɡælənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>英勇的</td><td>a gallant defense<br>英勇的防守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gallant paratroopers jumped from the plane without a moment's hesitation.  英勇的伞兵们毫不犹豫地跳出了飞机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 greathearted</del></td><td>/ˌɡreɪtˈhɑːrtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽宏大量的，勇敢的</td><td>a greathearted friend<br>宽宏大量的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her greathearted forgiveness surprised even her critics.  她宽宏的原谅甚至让批评者惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lofty</td><td>/ˈlɔːfti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>崇高的；自大的</td><td>a lofty tone<br>自大的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dismissed our idea with a lofty wave of her hand.  她自大地一挥手就否定了我们的想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 magnanimous</td><td>/mæɡˈnænəməs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽宏大量的，大度的</td><td>a magnanimous concession<br>大度的让步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was magnanimous in victory and praised her rival.  她胜而不骄，还称赞了对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noble</td><td>/ˈnoʊbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>高贵的，贵族的</td><td>a noble bearing<br>高贵的气派</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The horse moved with a noble grace across the field.  那匹马以高贵的优雅姿态穿过田野。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 base</td><td>/ˈbeɪs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑劣的，低下的</td><td>base motives<br>卑劣的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He acted from base motives of greed and envy.  他出于贪婪和嫉妒的卑劣动机行事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debased</td><td>/dəˈbeɪst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>堕落的，贬损的</td><td>debased taste<br>低劣的品味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He complained about the debased state of public debate.  他抱怨公共辩论的堕落状态。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 degenerate</td><td>/dɪˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>衰退的，堕落的；恶化</td><td>degenerate into chaos<br>恶化为混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peaceful protest began to degenerate into shouting and shoving.  和平抗议开始恶化为叫骂和推挤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 degraded</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪdəd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>堕落的，卑下的；退化的</td><td>a degraded lifestyle<br>堕落的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel depicts a once proud family in degraded circumstances.  小说描写了一个曾经显赫的家族沦落的境况。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ignoble</td><td>/ɪɡˈnoʊbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑鄙的，不高尚的</td><td>an ignoble motive<br>卑劣的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His generosity hid an ignoble desire for public praise.  他的慷慨背后藏着对公众赞誉的卑劣渴望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 low</td><td>/ˈloʊ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>低落的，沮丧的；低的</td><td>feel a bit low<br>感觉有点低落</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has been feeling low since her closest friend moved away.  自从最好的朋友搬走，她一直情绪低落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>subliminal</b></td><td>/ˌsʌbˈlɪmɪnl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>下意识的，潜在意识的</td><td>subliminal advertising messages<br>潜意识广告信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers question whether subliminal advertising messages influence what shoppers actually buy.  研究者质疑潜意识广告信息是否真会影响购买。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 concealed</td><td>/kənˈsiːld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐藏的，暗藏的</td><td>a concealed entrance<br>一个隐蔽的入口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A concealed door behind the shelves led to the cellar.  书架后一扇隐蔽的门通向地窖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hidden</td><td>/ˈhɪdən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐藏的，隐蔽的</td><td>hidden costs<br>隐藏的费用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract contained hidden fees no one had mentioned.  合同里含有没人提过的隐藏费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subconscious</td><td>/səbˈkɑːnʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>下意识的，潜意识的</td><td>a subconscious fear<br>潜意识的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A subconscious fear of failure kept him from applying at all.  对失败的潜意识恐惧让他根本没去申请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 apparent</td><td>/əˈperənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的；表面上的</td><td>for no apparent reason<br>没有明显原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It soon became apparent that the machine had never worked.  很快就明显看出这台机器从未运转过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 evident</td><td>/ˈevɪdənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，显然的</td><td>clearly evident<br>显而易见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her talent for music was evident from a very early age.  她的音乐才华从很小就显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 manifest</td><td>/ˈmænɪfest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>显然的，明显易懂的；显现，显露</td><td>manifest injustice<br>明显的不公</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His discomfort at the question was manifest to everyone seated around the table.  他对这个问题的不适让围坐桌边的每个人都看得明明白白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obvious</td><td>/ˈɑːbviəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，显而易见的</td><td>an obvious mistake<br>明显的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The answer seemed obvious once she explained the method.  她解释了方法后，答案似乎显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 patent</td><td>/ˈpeɪtnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>显而易见的，明显的；专利</td><td>a patent lie<br>显而易见的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The excuse was a patent lie and everyone knew it.  这个借口是显而易见的谎言，人人都知道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>submerge</b></td><td>/səbˈmɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使淹没</td><td>submerge the whole valley<br>淹没整个山谷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new dam will submerge the whole valley and three old villages.  新水坝将淹没整个山谷和三个古村。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dip</td><td>/ˈdɪp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下降，下沉；蘸</td><td>a dip in sales<br>销量的下滑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Temperatures dip sharply once the sun sets over the desert.  沙漠上日落后气温骤降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 douse</td><td>/daʊs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扑灭（火或灯）；浇透</td><td>douse the flames<br>扑灭火焰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firefighters worked all night to douse the flames on the upper floors.  消防员整夜奋战，扑灭了高层的火焰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drench</td><td>/drentʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使湿透，浸透</td><td>drenched to the skin<br>浑身湿透</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sudden storm drenched us to the skin before we reached shelter.  一场突来的暴雨在我们找到避处前就把我们浇得浑身湿透。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drown</td><td>/ˈdraʊn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>溺死；淹没</td><td>drown out the noise<br>把噪音压过去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud applause drowned out the speaker's closing words completely.  响亮的掌声完全淹没了演讲者的结束语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immerse</td><td>/ɪˈmɜːrs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沉浸；浸入</td><td>immerse herself in research<br>使自己沉浸于研究</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He immersed himself in the language by living with a local family.  他住在当地人家中，让自己沉浸在语言环境里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inundate</td><td>/ˈɪnʌndeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>淹没</td><td>inundated with requests<br>被请求淹没</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers inundated the newspaper with letters after the controversial editorial appeared.  那篇有争议的社论刊出后，读者的来信淹没了报社。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sop</td><td>/sɑːp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>浸湿；吸干（液体）；安慰物</td><td>sop up the spilled milk<br>吸干洒出的牛奶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He used a towel to sop up the milk spilled on the floor.  他用毛巾吸干洒在地上的牛奶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 submerse</del></td><td>/səbˈmɜːrs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使浸没，淹没</td><td>submerse the roots in water<br>把根浸入水中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gardeners submerse the bare roots in water before planting them.  园丁在栽种前把裸根浸入水中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 emerge</td><td>/ɪˈmɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>浮现，出现</td><td>emerge from the water<br>从水中浮现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The diver emerged from the dark water holding a rusty helmet.  潜水员从黑暗的水中浮出，手里拿着一个生锈的头盔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>submissive</b></td><td>/səbˈmɪsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>a submissive attitude<br>顺从的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a submissive tone that surprised her colleagues.  她以顺从的语气说话，令同事惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amenable</td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 biddable</td><td>/ˈbɪdəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，听话的</td><td>a biddable pupil<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The biddable pupil did every task without complaint.  那听话的学生毫无抱怨地完成每项任务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conformable</td><td>/kənˈfɔrməbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的；一致的</td><td>conformable to the rules<br>符合规定的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The design must be conformable to the city's new safety code.  设计必须符合城市新的安全规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 obeisant</del></td><td>/oʊˈbiːsənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>恭顺的，卑躬屈膝的</td><td>an obeisant servant<br>恭顺的仆人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The obeisant clerk bowed twice before daring to speak.  那个卑躬屈膝的职员鞠了两次躬才敢开口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obsequious</td><td>/əbˈsiːkwiəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谄媚的，奴性的</td><td>obsequious flattery<br>谄媚的奉承</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waiter's obsequious flattery made the whole table distinctly uncomfortable.  侍者谄媚的奉承让整桌人都明显不自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 servile</td><td>/ˈsɜːrvl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑屈的，奴性的</td><td>servile flattery<br>奴性的谄媚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His servile manner toward the boss annoyed everyone.  他对老板卑躬屈膝的态度让大家反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subservient</td><td>/səbˈsɜːrviənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>奉承的，屈从的</td><td>subservient to his boss<br>对上司百般奉承</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She refused to be subservient to anyone merely for a promotion.  她拒绝为了升职而对任何人卑躬屈膝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tractable</td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 contumacious</del></td><td>/ˌkɑːntuˈmeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，倔强的</td><td>a contumacious witness<br>不服从的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contumacious witness was fined for defying the judge.  那名抗命的证人因违抗法官而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 defiant</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公然违抗的，挑战的</td><td>a defiant attitude<br>挑战的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defiant student refused to apologize to his teacher.  这个公然抗拒的学生拒绝向老师道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obstreperous</td><td>/əbˈstrepərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不听话的，任性的</td><td>an obstreperous child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obstreperous child kept kicking the seat in front of him.  一个吵闹不驯的孩子一直踢着前排的座椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rebellious</td><td>/rɪˈbeljəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反叛的，难管束的</td><td>a rebellious teenager<br>叛逆的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her rebellious streak showed early, long before high school.  她的叛逆性格早早就显露，远在高中之前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recalcitrant</td><td>/rɪˈkælsɪtrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽抗的，不顺从的</td><td>a recalcitrant employee<br>不听劝的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A recalcitrant employee refused every instruction his new supervisor gave him.  一名顽抗的员工拒绝了新主管给他的每一条指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unruly</td><td>/ʌnˈruːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难驾驭的，不守规矩的</td><td>an unruly crowd<br>不守规矩的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teachers struggled to calm the unruly class.  两位老师费力地安抚那个不守规矩的班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>subordinate</b></td><td>/səˈbɔːrdənət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>次要的，下级的；使服从</td><td>a subordinate role<br>次要的角色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She refused to accept a subordinate role in the research project.  她不愿在研究项目中接受次要的角色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inferior</td><td>/ɪnˈfɪriər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>低等的，次等的</td><td>inferior in quality<br>质量较差</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Customers complained that the new fabric was clearly inferior.  顾客抱怨新面料明显质量较差。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 junior</td><td>/ˈdʒuːnjər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>下级的，资历较浅的</td><td>a junior clerk<br>下级职员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A junior partner handled the paperwork while the seniors argued.  一位资历较浅的合伙人处理文书，而资深者们在争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lower</td><td>/ˈloʊər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>降低，减少；较低的</td><td>lower the temperature<br>降低温度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bank lowered interest rates twice in one year.  银行一年内两次降低利率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 minor</td><td>/ˈmaɪnər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>较小的，不重要的</td><td>a minor problem<br>一个小问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only minor repairs were needed after the small accident.  小事故后只需要一些小修理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 secondary</td><td>/ˈsekənˌderi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>次要的，从属的</td><td>of secondary importance<br>重要性次之</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cost was secondary to safety in the final decision.  在最终决定中，成本次于安全。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subject</td><td>/ˈsʌbdʒekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>取决于（其他因素）的，受制于</td><td>subject to approval<br>须经批准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The entire plan is subject to approval by the city council.  整个方案须经市议会批准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tributary</td><td>/ˈtrɪbjəˌteri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>附属的；支流</td><td>a tributary river<br>一条支流</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This small tributary joins the great river below the old bridge.  这条小支流在老桥下方汇入大河。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conquer</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋkər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>征服，攻克</td><td>conquer a vast territory<br>征服广阔领土</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rome conquered most of the Mediterranean world within two centuries.  罗马在两个世纪内征服了地中海世界的大部分地区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dominate</td><td>/ˈdɑːməˌneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>支配，统治；俯视</td><td>dominate the market<br>支配市场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two firms dominate the industry and set nearly every price.  两家公司支配着这个行业，几乎决定所有价格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overpower</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈpaʊər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>制服，压倒</td><td>overpower the guards<br>制服守卫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Three officers finally overpowered the struggling suspect in the hallway.  三名警官最终在走廊里制服了挣扎的嫌疑人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pacify</td><td>/ˈpæsɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使平静，安慰</td><td>pacify a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse sang softly to pacify the frightened child before the injection.  打针前，护士轻声唱歌安抚受惊的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subdue</td><td>/səbˈduː/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使顺从；征服</td><td>subdue the rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Government forces moved south to subdue the rebellion within a week.  政府军南下，一周内镇压了叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subjugate</td><td>/ˈsʌbdʒuɡeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>征服，镇压</td><td>subjugate a rebellious province<br>征服叛乱的行省</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empire sent legions to subjugate a rebellious northern province.  帝国派出军团征服叛乱的北方行省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vanquish</td><td>/ˈvæŋkwɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打败，征服</td><td>vanquish the invading army<br>击败入侵的军队</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A smaller force managed to vanquish the invading army in one day.  一支人数较少的部队一天之内就击败了入侵的军队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 greater</td><td>/ˈɡreɪtər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>更重要的，更大的</td><td>of greater importance<br>更为重要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Safety is of greater importance than speed on this narrow road.  在这条窄路上安全比速度更重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 higher</td><td>/ˈhaɪər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>更高级的，更高的</td><td>higher education<br>高等教育</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These courses demand higher reasoning than introductory ones.  这些课程要求比入门课更高层次的推理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 major</td><td>/ˈmeɪdʒər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的，主要的</td><td>a major concern<br>主要的关切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water shortage remains a major concern for the region.  缺水仍是该地区的主要关切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 primary</td><td>/ˈpraɪˌmeri/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>首要的，主要的</td><td>the primary concern<br>首要的关切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Safety was the primary concern in redesigning the bridge.  重新设计桥梁时安全是首要关切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prime</td><td>/ˈpraɪm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>首要的，最重要的</td><td>the prime concern<br>首要关切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting costs became the prime concern of the new management team.  削减成本成了新管理团队的首要关切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 senior</td><td>/ˈsiːnjər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>上级的，资深的</td><td>a senior official<br>高级官员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A senior official signed the order without consulting his subordinates.  一位高级官员没有征询下属就签署了命令。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 superior</td><td>/suːˈpɪriər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>更好的，占优势的；上级</td><td>superior to the rest<br>优于其余各方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This model is clearly superior to the older version.  这个型号明显优于旧版。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 capitulate</td><td>/kəˈpɪtʃuleɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>投降，默许</td><td>capitulate to their demands<br>屈从他们的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After weeks of protest the company capitulated to the workers' demands.  抗议数周后，公司向工人的要求屈服了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 surrender</td><td>/səˈrendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>交出，放弃，投降</td><td>surrender to the police<br>向警方投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief finally surrendered to the police after a long chase.  经过长时间追捕，小偷终于向警方投降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 The biddable pupil did every task without complaint.  那听话的学生毫无抱怨地完成每项任务。</td></tr>
