<tr><td colspan="7">例 The design must be conformable to the city's new safety code.  设计必须符合城市新的安全规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 obeisant</del></td><td>/oʊˈbiːsənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>恭顺的，卑躬屈膝的</td><td>an obeisant servant<br>恭顺的仆人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The obeisant clerk bowed twice before daring to speak.  那个卑躬屈膝的职员鞠了两次躬才敢开口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obsequious</td><td>/əbˈsiːkwiəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谄媚的，奴性的</td><td>obsequious flattery<br>谄媚的奉承</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waiter's obsequious flattery made the whole table distinctly uncomfortable.  侍者谄媚的奉承让整桌人都明显不自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 servile</td><td>/ˈsɜːrvl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑屈的，奴性的</td><td>servile flattery<br>奴性的谄媚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His servile manner toward the boss annoyed everyone.  他对老板卑躬屈膝的态度让大家反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 submissive</td><td>/səbˈmɪsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>a submissive attitude<br>顺从的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a submissive tone that surprised her colleagues.  她以顺从的语气说话，令同事惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tractable</td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 contumacious</del></td><td>/ˌkɑːntuˈmeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，倔强的</td><td>a contumacious witness<br>不服从的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contumacious witness was fined for defying the judge.  那名抗命的证人因违抗法官而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 defiant</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公然违抗的，挑战的</td><td>a defiant attitude<br>挑战的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defiant student refused to apologize to his teacher.  这个公然抗拒的学生拒绝向老师道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obstreperous</td><td>/əbˈstrepərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不听话的，任性的</td><td>an obstreperous child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obstreperous child kept kicking the seat in front of him.  一个吵闹不驯的孩子一直踢着前排的座椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rebellious</td><td>/rɪˈbeljəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反叛的，难管束的</td><td>a rebellious teenager<br>叛逆的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her rebellious streak showed early, long before high school.  她的叛逆性格早早就显露，远在高中之前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recalcitrant</td><td>/rɪˈkælsɪtrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽抗的，不顺从的</td><td>a recalcitrant employee<br>不听劝的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A recalcitrant employee refused every instruction his new supervisor gave him.  一名顽抗的员工拒绝了新主管给他的每一条指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unruly</td><td>/ʌnˈruːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难驾驭的，不守规矩的</td><td>an unruly crowd<br>不守规矩的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teachers struggled to calm the unruly class.  两位老师费力地安抚那个不守规矩的班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>subside</b></td><td>/səbˈsaɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>减弱，平息；下沉</td><td>the pain finally subsided<br>疼痛终于减轻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The noise subsided as soon as the teacher walked in.  老师一进来，喧闹声就平息了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abate</td><td>/əˈbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，减弱；减少</td><td>the storm gradually abated<br>风暴逐渐减弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fever finally abated after two days of medication.  服药两天后，烧终于退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decline</td><td>/dɪˈklaɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝；下降，衰退</td><td>decline an invitation<br>谢绝邀请</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She politely declined the invitation to speak at the ceremony.  她礼貌地谢绝了在典礼上发言的邀请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decrease</td><td>/dɪˈkriːs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减少，下降</td><td>a sharp decrease<br>急剧下降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic accidents decreased noticeably after the new speed limits appeared.  新限速出台后交通事故明显减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diminish</td><td>/dɪˈmɪnɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）减少；轻视，贬低</td><td>diminish in importance<br>重要性下降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His influence diminished steadily after he left the board.  他离开董事会后影响力持续减弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drop</td><td>/ˈdrɑːp/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下降，减少；落下</td><td>drop sharply<br>急剧下降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales dropped by half after the price increase.  涨价后销量下降了一半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dwindle</td><td>/ˈdwɪndl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>逐渐减少</td><td>dwindle to nothing<br>逐渐减少至无</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their savings dwindled during the months of unemployment.  失业的几个月里他们的积蓄逐渐减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ebb</td><td>/ˈeb/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>衰退，减弱；退潮</td><td>his strength ebbed away<br>他的体力渐渐衰退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public enthusiasm for the project ebbed over the winter.  公众对这个项目的热情在冬天渐渐消退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fall</td><td>/ˈfɔːl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下降，落下；秋天</td><td>a sharp fall in prices<br>价格急剧下跌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Share prices fell again after the disappointing earnings report.  令人失望的财报公布后股价再次下跌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lessen</td><td>/ˈlesən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减少，减轻</td><td>lessen the risk<br>减少风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple exercises lessen the pain in his stiff shoulder.  简单的锻炼减轻了他僵硬肩膀的疼痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moderate</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>缓和，减轻；适度的</td><td>moderate one's demands<br>降低自己的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He agreed to moderate his tone during the debate.  他同意在辩论中缓和语气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recede</td><td>/rɪˈsiːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>退去，消退，减弱</td><td>recede into the distance<br>渐渐远去消失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The floodwaters slowly receded from the village streets.  洪水缓缓从村庄的街道退去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wane</td><td>/weɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>减少，衰退</td><td>wax and wane<br>盈亏消长，兴衰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public interest in the scandal waned within a month.  公众对这桩丑闻的兴趣一个月内就减退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 balloon</td><td>/bəˈluːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>迅速增加</td><td>costs have ballooned<br>成本迅速膨胀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repair costs ballooned from two thousand to twenty thousand dollars.  维修费用从两千美元猛增到两万美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 burgeon</td><td>/ˈbɜːrdʒən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>迅速成长扩大，蓬勃发展</td><td>a burgeoning market<br>迅速发展的市场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city's tech sector burgeoned after the new university opened.  新大学开办后，该市的科技产业迅速壮大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 escalate</td><td>/ˈeskəleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）（战争等）升级，扩大</td><td>escalate into violence<br>升级为暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The border dispute quickly escalated into a full-scale armed conflict.  边界争端迅速升级为全面武装冲突。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expand</td><td>/ɪkˈspænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，扩张；详述</td><td>expand the business<br>扩大业务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to expand into three new markets.  公司计划扩张到三个新市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 grow</td><td>/ˈɡroʊ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>种植，生长；成长</td><td>grow vegetables<br>种蔬菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They grow tomatoes and beans on a small rooftop garden.  他们在小小的屋顶花园里种番茄和豆子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 increase</td><td>/ɪnˈkriːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>增加，增长</td><td>increase production sharply<br>大幅提高产量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales increased steadily after the store extended its opening hours.  延长营业时间后销量稳步增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intensify</td><td>/ɪnˈtensɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，激化</td><td>intensify the pressure<br>加大压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain intensified just as we reached the summit.  我们刚到山顶，雨就更大了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rise</td><td>/ˈraɪz/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>上升，兴起</td><td>the rise of an empire<br>一个帝国的兴起</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians still debate the rise of that short-lived empire.  历史学家仍在争论那个短命帝国的兴起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 snowball</td><td>/ˈsnoʊˌbɔːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>迅速增长，滚雪球般扩大</td><td>snowball into a crisis<br>演变成一场危机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A small complaint snowballed into a national controversy.  一个小小的投诉迅速演变成全国性争议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soar</td><td>/ˈsɔːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>猛增，飞升</td><td>costs soar<br>成本猛增</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Building costs soared during the second year of construction.  施工第二年建筑成本猛增。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wax</td><td>/wæks/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>增大，增强</td><td>wax and wane<br>盛衰交替，此消彼长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His influence in the party waxed steadily during the long campaign.  在漫长的竞选中，他在党内的影响力稳步增强。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>subsidiary</b></td><td>/səbˈsɪdieri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>附属的，次要的</td><td>a subsidiary issue<br>次要的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company sold its subsidiary business to focus on core products.  公司出售了其附属业务，以专注于核心产品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 minor</td><td>/ˈmaɪnər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>较小的，不重要的</td><td>a minor problem<br>一个小问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only minor repairs were needed after the small accident.  小事故后只需要一些小修理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 secondary</td><td>/ˈsekənˌderi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>次要的，从属的</td><td>of secondary importance<br>重要性次之</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cost was secondary to safety in the final decision.  在最终决定中，成本次于安全。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subordinate</td><td>/səˈbɔːrdənət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>次要的，下级的；使服从</td><td>a subordinate role<br>次要的角色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She refused to accept a subordinate role in the research project.  她不愿在研究项目中接受次要的角色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tributary</td><td>/ˈtrɪbjəˌteri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>附属的；支流</td><td>a tributary river<br>一条支流</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This small tributary joins the great river below the old bridge.  这条小支流在老桥下方汇入大河。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 capital</td><td>/ˈkæpətəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>中心地，中心；首都</td><td>the fashion capital<br>时尚之都</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Milan is often called the fashion capital of Italy.  米兰常被称为意大利的时尚之都。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cardinal</td><td>/ˈkɑːrdənl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>主要的，非常重要的</td><td>a cardinal rule<br>一条首要的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Honesty is a cardinal rule in this small hospital.  诚实是这家小医院的首要准则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 central</td><td>/ˈsentrəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>中心的，最重要的</td><td>the central issue<br>核心问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water shortage is the central issue facing the whole region.  缺水是整个地区面临的核心问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 chief</td><td>/ˈtʃiːf/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>主要的，首要的；首领</td><td>the chief reason<br>主要原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cost was the chief reason they abandoned the project.  成本是他们放弃这个项目的主要原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 key</td><td>/ˈkiː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>关键的；钥匙</td><td>a key factor<br>关键因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trust is the key element in any long working relationship.  信任是任何长期工作关系中的关键要素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 leading</td><td>/ˈliːdɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>主要的，领先的</td><td>a leading expert<br>顶尖专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is a leading authority on ancient Chinese bronzes.  她是中国古代青铜器的顶尖权威。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 main</td><td>/ˈmeɪn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>主要的，最重要的</td><td>the main point<br>主要论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The main problem is that we have almost no time.  主要问题是我们几乎没有时间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 paramount</td><td>/ˈpærəmaʊnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>最重要的</td><td>of paramount importance<br>至为重要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Safety is paramount when workers handle these highly flammable chemicals.  工人处理这些极易燃的化学品时，安全最为重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 predominant</td><td>/prɪˈdɑːmənənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>主要的，占优势的</td><td>the predominant view<br>占主导的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rice remains the predominant crop in the southern valleys.  稻米仍是南部谷地的主要作物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 primary</td><td>/ˈpraɪˌmeri/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>首要的，主要的</td><td>the primary concern<br>首要的关切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Safety was the primary concern in redesigning the bridge.  重新设计桥梁时安全是首要关切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 principal</td><td>/ˈprɪnsəpəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>主要的，首要的；校长</td><td>the principal cause<br>首要原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tourism is the principal source of income on the island.  旅游业是这个岛上的主要收入来源。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 supreme</td><td>/səˈpriːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>最高的，至上的</td><td>of supreme importance<br>极其重要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court holds supreme authority over all such disputes.  该法院对所有此类争议拥有最高裁判权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>subsidy</b></td><td>/ˈsʌbsədi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>补助金，津贴</td><td>a government subsidy<br>政府补助金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A generous government subsidy keeps the local bus service running.  政府的丰厚补助金维持着当地公交运营。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allotment</td><td>/əˈlɑːtmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>分配额，配给量</td><td>a monthly allotment<br>每月的配给额</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Each family received a small allotment of grain that winter.  那个冬天每家都领到了少量的粮食配给。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allowance</td><td>/əˈlaʊəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>限额；津贴</td><td>a baggage allowance<br>行李限额</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Each passenger has a baggage allowance of two small bags.  每位乘客的行李限额是两个小包。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appropriation</td><td>/əˌproʊpriˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>拨款；占用</td><td>a budget appropriation<br>预算拨款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Congress approved a large appropriation for rural road repairs.  国会批准了一笔用于乡村道路维修的大额拨款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grant</td><td>/ˈɡrænt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>承认；授予，同意给予</td><td>grant that he is right<br>承认他是对的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I grant that the plan has some obvious weaknesses.  我承认这个计划有些明显的弱点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subvention</td><td>/səbˈvɛnʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>补助金，津贴</td><td>a state subvention<br>国家补助金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The theater survives on a modest annual subvention from the city.  剧院靠市政府每年适度的补助金维持运营。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>substantial</b></td><td>/səbˈstænʃl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，可观的；实质的</td><td>a substantial increase<br>大幅的增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company reported a substantial increase in profits this quarter.  公司报告本季度利润大幅增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consequential</td><td>/ˌkɑːnsəˈkwenʃəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的；随之而来的</td><td>a consequential ruling<br>一项重要的裁决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That quiet vote proved the most consequential act of the session.  那次不起眼的投票成了本届会议最重要的举动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 considerable</td><td>/kənˈsɪdərəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（数量上）可观（而值得注意）的</td><td>a considerable sum<br>一笔可观的钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The renovation required a considerable amount of money and nearly two years of work.  这次翻修耗费了可观的资金和近两年的工期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 earthshaking</del></td><td>/ˈɜːrθˌʃeɪkɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极重大的，惊天动地的</td><td>an earthshaking discovery<br>惊天动地的发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her paper was solid but hardly an earthshaking contribution.  她的论文很扎实，但算不上惊天动地的贡献。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 important</td><td>/ˌɪmˈpɔːrtənt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的，重大的</td><td>an important decision<br>一个重要的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sleep is as important as diet for a young athlete.  对年轻运动员来说，睡眠和饮食一样重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meaningful</td><td>/ˈmiːnɪŋfəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有意义的，意味深长的</td><td>meaningful work<br>有意义的工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She left banking to find more meaningful work.  她离开银行业去寻找更有意义的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 momentous</td><td>/moʊˈmentəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重大的，重要的</td><td>a momentous decision<br>一个重大的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board reached a momentous decision that changed the company's direction entirely.  董事会做出了一个重大决定，彻底改变了公司的方向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 significant</td><td>/səɡˈnɪfɪkənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的；显著的</td><td>a significant difference<br>显著的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a significant drop in the pollution levels.  研究者发现污染水平显著下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weighty</td><td>/ˈweɪti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的，重大的；沉重的</td><td>weighty matters<br>重大的事务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The elders debated weighty questions of land and water.  长者们讨论着土地与水源的重大问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconsequential</td><td>/ˌɪnkɑːnsɪˈkwenʃəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不重要的，微不足道的</td><td>an inconsequential remark<br>无关紧要的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The difference between the two prices is quite inconsequential.  两个价格之间的差别微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconsiderable</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈsɪdərəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的</td><td>an inconsiderable sum<br>微不足道的一笔钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The risk is small but by no means inconsiderable.  风险虽小，却绝非微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insignificant</td><td>/ˌɪnsɪɡˈnɪfɪkənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关紧要的，微不足道的</td><td>a seemingly insignificant change<br>看似微小的改动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An insignificant error early on ruined the entire calculation.  早期一个微不足道的错误毁了整个计算。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 little</td><td>/ˈlɪtəl/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微小的，少的</td><td>a little detail<br>一个小细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She noticed one little detail that everyone else had missed.  她注意到一个别人都漏掉的小细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 minor</td><td>/ˈmaɪnər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>较小的，不重要的</td><td>a minor problem<br>一个小问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only minor repairs were needed after the small accident.  小事故后只需要一些小修理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 negligible</td><td>/ˈneɡlədʒəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的，可忽略的</td><td>a negligible difference<br>可忽略的差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The extra cost is negligible compared with the savings.  与节省的钱相比，额外成本微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slight</td><td>/slaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>轻微的，不重要的；轻视，怠慢</td><td>slight her contribution<br>轻视她的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt slighted when nobody mentioned his contribution during the ceremony.  典礼上无人提及他的贡献，他觉得受到了轻视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trifling</td><td>/ˈtraɪflɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>细微的，不重要的</td><td>a trifling sum<br>微不足道的一笔钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He dismissed the missing coins as a trifling sum not worth reporting.  他把丢失的硬币当作微不足道的小钱，不值得上报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trivial</td><td>/ˈtrɪviəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无足轻重的，不重要的</td><td>a trivial detail<br>无关紧要的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor refused to argue over such a trivial difference in wording.  编辑不愿为措辞上如此无足轻重的差别争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>substantiate</b></td><td>/səbˈstænʃieɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实</td><td>substantiate the claim<br>证实这一说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No evidence has emerged to substantiate his serious accusations.  没有证据能证实他严重的指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attest</td><td>/əˈtest/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，为…作证</td><td>attest to his good character<br>证明他品行良好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several neighbors will attest to his good character in court.  几位邻居将在法庭上证明他品行良好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authenticate</td><td>/ɔˈθentəˌkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，鉴定为真</td><td>authenticate the painting<br>鉴定画作真伪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Experts were called in to authenticate the painting's signature.  专家被请来鉴定这幅画签名的真伪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confirm</td><td>/kənˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认，进一步确定</td><td>confirm the findings<br>证实这些发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Later tests confirmed what the first team had suspected.  后来的检测证实了第一个团队的猜测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 corroborate</td><td>/kəˈrɑːbəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>用证据或权威证实；为…提供证据，支持</td><td>corroborate the witness's story<br>证实证人的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two neighbors corroborated the driver's account of the accident that night.  两位邻居证实了司机对那晚事故的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 justify</td><td>/ˈdʒʌstɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证明…的合理性，为…辩解</td><td>justify his decision<br>证明他的决定合理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing in the report can justify his decision to conceal the results.  报告里没有任何内容能证明他隐瞒结果的决定是合理的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 validate</td><td>/ˈvælədeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认；使有效</td><td>validate the results<br>验证这些结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Independent labs validated the results within three months.  独立实验室在三个月内验证了这些结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 verify</td><td>/ˈverɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，核实</td><td>verify the data<br>核实这些数据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two independent labs will verify the results before publication.  两家独立实验室将在发表前核实这些结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 controvert</del></td><td>/ˈkɑntrəvɜrt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反驳，否证</td><td>controvert the claim<br>反驳该说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New fossil data controvert the long-accepted theory of migration.  新的化石数据反驳了长期公认的迁徙理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disprove</td><td>/ˌdɪsˈpruːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反驳，证明为假</td><td>disprove the theory<br>驳倒这一理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Magellan's voyage finally disproved the belief in a flat earth.  麦哲伦的航行最终驳倒了地球是平的这一信念。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rebut</td><td>/rɪˈbʌt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反驳，驳斥</td><td>rebut the accusation<br>反驳这项指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She rebutted every charge with documents and dated photographs.  她用文件和标注日期的照片反驳了每项指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refute</td><td>/rɪˈfjuːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驳倒，反驳</td><td>refute the claim<br>驳斥这一说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scientist used new data to refute the claim about rising temperatures.  这位科学家用新数据反驳了关于气温上升的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>substantive</b></td><td>/ˈsʌbstəntɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>本质的，关键的</td><td>substantive changes<br>实质性的改变</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee made substantive changes to the draft rather than cosmetic ones.  委员会对草案作了实质性改动，而非表面修饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 constitutional</td><td>/ˌkɑːnstəˈtuːʃənəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>体质上的，内在固有的</td><td>a constitutional weakness<br>体质上的虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His shyness seems constitutional rather than a habit he learned.  他的害羞似乎是天性使然，而非后天习得的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elemental</td><td>/ˌeləˈmentəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>基本的，根本的</td><td>elemental human needs<br>人的基本需求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The story explores elemental fears of darkness and loss.  这个故事探讨对黑暗与失去的原始恐惧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 essential</td><td>/ɪˈsenʃəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>本质的，必不可少的</td><td>an essential quality<br>本质的特性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patience is an essential part of her character.  耐心是她性格中本质的一部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fundamental</td><td>/ˌfʌndəˈmentəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>基本的；根本原理</td><td>a fundamental principle<br>一条根本原则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trust is fundamental to any lasting partnership.  信任对任何持久的合作都是根本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 substantial</td><td>/səbˈstænʃl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，可观的；实质的</td><td>a substantial increase<br>大幅的增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company reported a substantial increase in profits this quarter.  公司报告本季度利润大幅增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vital</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的；极重要的</td><td>full of vital energy<br>充满生命活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise keeps older people vital and mentally alert.  经常锻炼使老年人保持活力和头脑清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconsequential</td><td>/ˌɪnkɑːnsɪˈkwenʃəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不重要的，微不足道的</td><td>an inconsequential remark<br>无关紧要的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The difference between the two prices is quite inconsequential.  两个价格之间的差别微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconsiderable</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈsɪdərəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的</td><td>an inconsiderable sum<br>微不足道的一笔钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The risk is small but by no means inconsiderable.  风险虽小，却绝非微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insignificant</td><td>/ˌɪnsɪɡˈnɪfɪkənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关紧要的，微不足道的</td><td>a seemingly insignificant change<br>看似微小的改动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An insignificant error early on ruined the entire calculation.  早期一个微不足道的错误毁了整个计算。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insubstantial</td><td>/ˌɪnsəbˈstænʃəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非实在的；虚弱的</td><td>insubstantial evidence<br>站不住脚的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His case rested on insubstantial rumor rather than real documents.  他的论据靠的是虚无的传言而非真实文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 negligible</td><td>/ˈneɡlədʒəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的，可忽略的</td><td>a negligible difference<br>可忽略的差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The extra cost is negligible compared with the savings.  与节省的钱相比，额外成本微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nominal</td><td>/ˈnɑːmɪnl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>名义上的；微不足道的</td><td>a nominal fee<br>象征性的费用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum charges a nominal fee that barely covers the cost of printing tickets.  博物馆收取象征性的费用，几乎只够印门票的成本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>subterfuge</b></td><td>/ˈsʌbtərfjuːdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计</td><td>resort to subterfuge<br>采用诡计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The spy resorted to subterfuge to enter the heavily guarded building.  间谍采用诡计进入戒备森严的大楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 artifice</td><td>/ˈɑːrtəfɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计，欺骗；巧妙手段</td><td>use clever artifice<br>使用巧妙的诡计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her apparent shyness was pure artifice designed to disarm her rivals.  她表面的羞怯纯属伪装，为的是麻痹对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cheat</td><td>/ˈtʃiːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>欺骗，作弊；骗子</td><td>cheat on an exam<br>考试作弊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cheated the old couple out of their entire life savings.  他骗走了这对老夫妇一生的积蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chicanery</td><td>/ʃɪˈkeɪnəri/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计多端，欺骗</td><td>political chicanery<br>政治骗术</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The election was decided by chicanery rather than by honest debate.  这次选举靠的是欺骗手段而非诚实辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deception</td><td>/dɪˈsepʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>欺骗，骗术</td><td>practice deception<br>行骗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He obtained the money by deception and was later arrested.  他骗取了这笔钱，后来被捕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fraud</td><td>/ˈfrɔːd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>骗子；欺诈行为</td><td>expose a fraud<br>揭露骗局</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The so-called healer turned out to be an outright fraud.  那位所谓的治疗师原来是个彻头彻尾的骗子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trickery</td><td>/ˈtrɪkəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>欺骗，诡计</td><td>resort to trickery<br>使用诡计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He won the contract through outright trickery.  他完全靠欺诈手段拿下了这份合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wile</td><td>/ˈwaɪl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计，花招</td><td>use every wile<br>使尽各种花招</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She used every wile she knew to win his support.  她用尽一切手段来赢得他的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>subtle</b></td><td>/ˈsʌtl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微妙的，难以感知的</td><td>a subtle difference<br>细微的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a subtle difference between these two shades of blue.  这两种蓝色之间有细微的差别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delicate</td><td>/ˈdeləkət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微妙的，精细的；脆弱的</td><td>a delicate distinction<br>微妙的区别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a delicate ear for the subtle differences between dialects.  她对方言之间的细微差别有很灵敏的耳朵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elusive</td><td>/ɪˈluːsɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以捉摸的；难抓到的</td><td>an elusive meaning<br>难以捉摸的含义</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The precise meaning of the poem remains elusive to critics.  这首诗的确切含义仍让评论家难以捉摸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evasive</td><td>/ɪˈveɪzɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>含糊其辞的，回避的</td><td>an evasive answer<br>含糊的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister gave an evasive answer to every question.  部长对每个问题都给出含糊的回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 faint</td><td>/ˈfeɪnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的；微弱的</td><td>a faint voice<br>微弱的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt faint after standing in the sun too long.  在太阳下站太久后她感到虚弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fine</td><td>/ˈfaɪn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>细微的，精细的</td><td>a fine distinction<br>细微的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He drew a fine distinction between courage and recklessness.  他在勇敢与莽撞之间划出了细微的区别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impalpable</td><td>/ɪmˈpælpəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以察觉的，无形的</td><td>an impalpable difference<br>难以察觉的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A faint, impalpable tension hung over the negotiating table.  谈判桌上笼罩着一种难以察觉的紧张气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intangible</td><td>/ɪnˈtændʒəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法感知的，无形的</td><td>intangible assets<br>无形资产</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A company's reputation is an intangible asset that no balance sheet captures.  公司声誉是一项资产负债表无法体现的无形资产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nice</td><td>/ˈnaɪs/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>规矩得体的；令人愉快的</td><td>nice manners<br>得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a nice boy who never gives his parents trouble.  他是个规矩的男孩，从不给父母添麻烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slight</td><td>/slaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>轻微的，不重要的；轻视，怠慢</td><td>slight her contribution<br>轻视她的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt slighted when nobody mentioned his contribution during the ceremony.  典礼上无人提及他的贡献，他觉得受到了轻视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blatant</td><td>/ˈbleɪtnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，公然的</td><td>a blatant lie<br>赤裸裸的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report exposed a blatant disregard for safety rules.  报告揭露了对安全规则的公然无视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conspicuous</td><td>/kənˈspɪkjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>显而易见的；吸引人的</td><td>conspicuous consumption<br>炫耀性消费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His bright red jacket made him conspicuous in the gray winter crowd.  他鲜红的夹克让他在灰暗的冬日人群中格外显眼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obvious</td><td>/ˈɑːbviəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，显而易见的</td><td>an obvious mistake<br>明显的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The answer seemed obvious once she explained the method.  她解释了方法后，答案似乎显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 patent</td><td>/ˈpeɪtnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>显而易见的，明显的；专利</td><td>a patent lie<br>显而易见的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The excuse was a patent lie and everyone knew it.  这个借口是显而易见的谎言，人人都知道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>subvert</b></td><td>/səbˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>颠覆</td><td>subvert the government<br>颠覆政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The group was accused of plotting to subvert the elected government.  该团体被指控阴谋颠覆经选举产生的政府。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overthrow</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈθroʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>推翻，颠覆</td><td>overthrow the government<br>推翻政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rebels tried to overthrow the government within a single night.  叛军试图在一夜之间推翻政府。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overturn</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈtɜːrn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>推翻，颠倒</td><td>overturn a court ruling<br>推翻法院判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The higher court overturned the verdict after new evidence appeared.  新证据出现后，上级法院推翻了原判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 topple</td><td>/ˈtɑːpəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>推翻，使倒塌</td><td>topple a dictator<br>推翻独裁者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Mass protests toppled the dictator after only three weeks.  大规模抗议仅三周后就推翻了那位独裁者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reinforce</td><td>/ˌriːɪnˈfɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加强，强化</td><td>reinforce the message<br>强化这一信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated praise reinforced the child's interest in reading.  反复的表扬强化了孩子对阅读的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>succinct</b></td><td>/səkˈsɪŋkt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简明的，简洁的</td><td>a succinct summary<br>简洁的总结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her succinct summary covered the entire report in three short paragraphs.  她简洁的总结用三小段概括了整份报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 apothegmatic</del></td><td>/ˌæpəθeɡˈmætɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>格言式的，简练精辟的</td><td>an apothegmatic style<br>格言式的文风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His apothegmatic prose fits neatly on a postcard.  他格言式的文字正好能写满一张明信片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brief</td><td>/ˈbriːf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简短的，简洁的</td><td>a brief summary<br>简短的总结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a brief account of the accident to the police.  他向警方简短叙述了事故经过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compact</td><td>/ˈkɑːmpækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>契约，协议；紧凑的</td><td>sign a compact<br>签订协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two nations signed a compact to protect the shared river.  两国签订协议保护共有的河流。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compendious</td><td>/kəmˈpɛndiəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简明扼要的</td><td>a compendious reference guide<br>简明扼要的参考手册</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wrote a compendious history of the city in ninety pages.  他用九十页写成了一部简明的城市史。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 concise</td><td>/kənˈsaɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简明的，简洁的</td><td>a concise explanation<br>简明的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Keep your answer concise; the interviewer has only ten minutes.  回答要简洁，面试官只有十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laconic</td><td>/ləˈkɑːnɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简洁（以至于显得粗鲁或难以理解）的</td><td>a laconic reply<br>简短生硬的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His laconic reply told us nothing about what had actually happened.  他简短生硬的回答没有告诉我们实际发生了什么。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pithy</td><td>/ˈpɪθi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简练的，言简意赅的</td><td>a pithy remark<br>精练的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her pithy summary captured the whole debate in two sentences.  她简练的总结用两句话概括了整场辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 summary</td><td>/ˈsʌməri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>概括的，简略的；摘要</td><td>a summary of the report<br>报告的摘要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please attach a one-page summary to the front of the proposal.  请在提案前面附上一页摘要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 telegraphic</td><td>/ˌtelɪˈɡræfɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简短扼要的，电报式的</td><td>a telegraphic reply<br>简短扼要的回复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sent a telegraphic note: arriving Tuesday, bring keys.  她发来一条电报式的短信：周二到，带钥匙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 terse</td><td>/tɜːrs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简洁的，简明的</td><td>a terse reply<br>简洁的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His terse note said only that the deal had collapsed.  他简短的字条只说交易破裂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 circuitous</td><td>/sərˈkjuːɪtəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迂回的，不直接的</td><td>a circuitous route<br>迂回的路线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We took a circuitous route to avoid the flooded highway.  我们绕了一条迂回的路以避开被淹的公路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 circumlocutory</del></td><td>/ˌsɜrkəmˈlɑkjətɔri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迂回冗赘的，绕弯子的</td><td>circumlocutory replies<br>绕弯子的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His circumlocutory answers hid a very simple refusal.  他绕弯子的答复掩盖了一个简单的拒绝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 diffuse</td><td>/dɪfˈjuːs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长散漫的；扩散的</td><td>a diffuse and wordy essay<br>散漫冗长的文章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument was far too diffuse to convince a careful reader.  他的论证过于散漫，无法说服细心的读者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 long</td><td>/ˈlɔːŋ/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>不及物动词、形容词</td><td>渴望；长的</td><td>long for company<br>渴望有人陪伴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After months at sea he longed for his family's cooking.  在海上数月后，他渴望家人做的饭菜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prolix</td><td>/ˈproʊlɪks/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>啰嗦的，冗长的</td><td>prolix legal prose<br>冗长的法律文字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge criticized the prolix brief for burying its argument.  法官批评这份冗长的书状埋没了自己的论点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rambling</td><td>/ˈræmblɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>漫无目的的，散漫的</td><td>a rambling explanation<br>散漫的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a rambling answer that satisfied nobody present.  他给出散漫的回答，在场无人满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 verbose</td><td>/vɜːrˈboʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长的，啰嗦的</td><td>a verbose explanation<br>冗长的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her verbose report could have said the same thing in two pages.  她冗长的报告本可以用两页说完同样的内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 windy</td><td>/ˈwɪndi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长的</td><td>a windy speech<br>冗长空洞的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His windy speech said very little in more than an hour.  他冗长的演讲讲了一个多小时却几乎没什么内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wordy</td><td>/ˈwɜːrdi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长的，啰嗦的</td><td>a wordy introduction<br>啰嗦的引言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor cut the wordy opening by half.  编辑把啰嗦的开头删掉一半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>succor</b></td><td>/ˈsʌkər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>救援，援助</td><td>bring succor to the wounded<br>救助伤员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers hurried to succor the families left homeless by the flood.  志愿者们赶去救助因洪水而无家可归的家庭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aid</td><td>/ˈeɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>帮助，援助</td><td>come to someone's aid<br>前来帮助某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors came to her aid within minutes of the fire.  火灾发生几分钟内邻居们就来帮忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assist</td><td>/əˈsɪst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，协助</td><td>assist with the work<br>协助工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two nurses assisted the surgeon during the operation.  两名护士在手术中协助医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 help</td><td>/ˈhelp/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>帮助，助益</td><td>a great help<br>很大的帮助</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your advice was a great help during the difficult week.  在那困难的一周里你的建议帮了大忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relieve</td><td>/rɪˈliːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓解；换班替换</td><td>relieve the pressure<br>缓解压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk relieved the tension in her shoulders.  短暂散步缓解了她肩膀的紧张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 support</td><td>/səˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，支撑</td><td>support a proposal<br>支持一项提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most members voted to support the new proposal.  大多数成员投票支持这项新提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>suffocate</b></td><td>/ˈsʌfəkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使窒息</td><td>suffocate in the smoke<br>在烟中窒息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several residents nearly suffocated in the thick smoke from the fire.  几名居民在火灾的浓烟中几乎窒息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 asphyxiate</td><td>/əsˈfɪksieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使窒息，使闷死</td><td>asphyxiate in thick smoke<br>在浓烟中窒息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two workers nearly asphyxiated in the unventilated storage tank.  两名工人在不通风的储罐里几乎窒息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 choke</td><td>/ˈtʃoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>使窒息；压制</td><td>choke on the smoke<br>被烟呛住</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick smoke nearly choked the firefighters in the hallway.  浓烟几乎使走廊里的消防员窒息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smother</td><td>/ˈsmʌðər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制（表达、说出），压制</td><td>smother a laugh<br>忍住不笑出声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He turned away to smother a laugh at her odd remark.  他转过身去，忍住对她那句怪话的笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stifle</td><td>/ˈstaɪfl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制（声音、呼吸等）；阻止，扼杀</td><td>stifle a yawn<br>忍住哈欠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She tried to stifle a yawn while the speaker droned on.  演讲者絮絮不休，她努力忍住哈欠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strangle</td><td>/ˈstræŋɡəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扼制，压制；勒死</td><td>strangle dissent<br>压制异议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy regulation strangled the small firms within two years.  繁重的管制两年内就压垮了这些小公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>suffuse</b></del></td><td>/səˈfjuːz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（色彩等）弥漫，染遍，充满</td><td>suffused with warm light<br>沐浴在暖光中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Warm evening light suffused the quiet room with a golden glow.  傍晚的暖光把安静的房间染成金色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flush</td><td>/flʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>充裕的，有钱的；冲洗</td><td>flush with money<br>钱很充裕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the bonus he was flush with money for months.  拿到奖金后他有好几个月钱都很充裕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fill</td><td>/ˈfɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>填满，充满</td><td>fill the glass with water<br>把杯子倒满水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He filled the glass with cold water and drank it quickly.  他倒满一杯冷水，很快喝掉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imbue</td><td>/ɪmˈbjuː/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>灌输，使充满</td><td>imbue students with confidence<br>使学生充满自信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her teachers imbued her with a lasting love of poetry.  老师们使她对诗歌怀有持久的热爱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infuse</td><td>/ɪnˈfjuːz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>灌输，使…充满</td><td>infuse new energy into<br>注入新的活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new members infused fresh energy into the struggling drama club.  新成员为陷入困境的戏剧社注入了新的活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 interpenetrate</del></td><td>/ˌɪntərˈpɛnətreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>相互渗透，贯穿</td><td>interpenetrate two cultures<br>使两种文化相互渗透</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Folk melodies and classical forms interpenetrate throughout his early symphonies.  民间旋律与古典形式在他早期交响曲中相互渗透。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 percolate</td><td>/ˈpɜːrkəˌleɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>渗透，慢慢扩散</td><td>percolate through the soil<br>渗透过土壤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rainwater slowly percolated through the sandy soil into the well.  雨水缓慢渗过沙质土壤流入井中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pervade</td><td>/pərˈveɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>弥漫，充满</td><td>pervade the whole house<br>弥漫整座房子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A faint smell of lavender pervaded the whole house that summer.  那个夏天，淡淡的薰衣草香弥漫了整座房子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transfuse</td><td>/trænsˈfjuːz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>注入，灌输；输血</td><td>transfuse new energy<br>注入新的活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young coach transfused fresh confidence into the struggling team.  这位年轻教练把新的信心注入陷入困境的球队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sulk</b></td><td>/sʌlk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>生气，愠怒</td><td>sulk in silence<br>闷声生气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy sulked in silence for hours after losing the game.  输了比赛后，男孩闷声生气了好几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frown</td><td>/ˈfraʊn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>不悦，不赞成；皱眉</td><td>frown on the practice<br>不赞成这种做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most universities frown on students sharing their assignments.  大多数大学不赞成学生互相分享作业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grump</td><td>/ˈɡrʌmp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>生闷气；脾气坏的人</td><td>grump about the weather<br>对天气发牢骚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grumped about the weather all the way to the station.  他一路到车站都在抱怨天气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mope</td><td>/ˈmoʊp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>闷闷不乐，郁闷</td><td>mope around the house<br>在屋里闷闷不乐地转</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She moped around the house for days after the news.  听到消息后她在屋里闷闷不乐地转了好几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pout</td><td>/paʊt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（尤指撅嘴或板着脸）表示不悦</td><td>pout in silence<br>板着脸不出声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She began to pout when her parents refused to buy the toy.  父母不肯买那个玩具时，她开始撅嘴表示不悦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 crow</td><td>/kroʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>得意地夸耀，自鸣得意</td><td>crow about the win<br>为获胜而得意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He crowed about his victory until even his closest friends grew tired.  他为胜利得意个不停，连最亲近的朋友都听厌了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delight</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使高兴，使愉快；愉悦</td><td>delight the audience<br>使观众高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist delighted the audience with a lively encore.  这位年轻钢琴家用一首活泼的加演曲取悦了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 exuberate</del></td><td>/ɪɡˈzubəˌreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>兴高采烈，极其欢欣</td><td>exuberate over the news<br>为这消息欢欣不已</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village exuberated when the harvest proved unusually rich.  收成出奇地好，全村人欢天喜地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 jubilate</td><td>/ˈdʒubəleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欢欣，喜悦</td><td>jubilate over the news<br>为这消息而欢欣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole town began to jubilate when the rescue team returned safely.  救援队平安归来时，全城开始欢庆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rejoice</td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欣喜，喜悦</td><td>rejoice at the news<br>为消息而欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village rejoiced at the news of the safe return.  听到平安归来的消息，全村都欣喜不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 triumph</td><td>/ˈtraɪəmf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>巨大成功，胜利</td><td>a stunning military triumph<br>惊人的军事胜利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new bridge is a triumph of modern engineering and careful planning.  这座新桥是现代工程与周密规划的巨大成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sullen</b></td><td>/ˈsʌlən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闷闷不乐的</td><td>a sullen silence<br>闷闷不乐的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sullen silence fell over the table when the topic was raised.  提到这个话题时，餐桌上陷入闷闷不乐的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bleak</td><td>/ˈbliːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（天气）阴冷的；荒凉的</td><td>a bleak winter day<br>阴冷的冬日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The moor looked bleak under grey November skies.  灰暗的十一月天空下，荒原显得阴冷荒凉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cheerless</td><td>/ˈtʃɪrləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴郁的，令人沮丧的</td><td>a cheerless room<br>阴沉的房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent a cheerless evening in the cold, empty apartment.  他们在冰冷空荡的公寓里度过了一个阴郁的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dark</td><td>/ˈdɑːrk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴郁的；黑暗的</td><td>a dark mood<br>阴郁的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fell into a dark mood after reading the letter.  读了那封信后他陷入阴郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 depressing</td><td>/dɪˈpresɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人沮丧的</td><td>a depressing forecast<br>令人沮丧的预测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rainy weather and empty streets felt deeply depressing.  阴雨天气和空荡的街道让人非常压抑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dire</td><td>/ˈdaɪər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可怕的，恐怖的；迫切的</td><td>dire consequences<br>可怕的后果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report warned of dire consequences if the dam were not repaired soon.  报告警告说，若不尽快修复大坝，后果将十分可怕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gloomy</td><td>/ˈɡluːmi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>a gloomy mood<br>忧郁的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew gloomy whenever the winter rain lasted more than a week.  每当冬雨连下一周以上，她就变得忧郁起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glum</td><td>/ˈɡlʌm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴郁的，忧闷的</td><td>a glum expression<br>阴郁的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat in the corner with a glum expression all evening.  他整晚坐在角落里，一脸阴郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gray</td><td>/ˈɡreɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴沉的，灰暗的</td><td>a gray winter morning<br>阴沉的冬日清晨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gray November sky matched everyone's low spirits that week.  十一月阴沉的天空与那周大家的低落情绪相配。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lugubrious</td><td>/ləˈɡuːbriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（故作夸张的）悲哀的</td><td>a lugubrious expression<br>夸张悲哀的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wore a lugubrious expression whenever anyone mentioned his ruined garden.  每次有人提到他毁掉的花园，他就露出夸张悲哀的表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 morose</td><td>/məˈroʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>grow morose and silent<br>变得忧郁沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After losing the election he grew morose and rarely left his apartment.  落选之后他变得忧郁，很少离开公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 saturnine</td><td>/ˈsætərnaɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的，阴沉的</td><td>a saturnine temperament<br>阴沉的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His saturnine expression discouraged anyone from starting a cheerful conversation.  他阴沉的表情让人不敢开启轻松的话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sulky</td><td>/ˈsʌlki/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>赌气的，生气不说话的</td><td>a sulky teenager<br>赌气的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sulky child refused to speak for the whole long afternoon.  那个赌气的孩子整个下午都不肯说话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surly</td><td>/ˈsɜːrli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气不好的</td><td>a surly waiter<br>一个态度恶劣的侍者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A surly waiter took our order without once looking up.  一个态度恶劣的侍者点单时始终没抬头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bright</td><td>/ˈbraɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快活的，开朗的；明亮的</td><td>a bright smile<br>开朗的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She greeted every customer with a bright and genuine smile.  她用开朗真诚的笑容迎接每位顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cheerful</td><td>/ˈtʃɪrfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，愉快的</td><td>a cheerful greeting<br>愉快的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed cheerful even after the long delay.  即使长时间延误后他仍保持愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cheering</td><td>/ˈtʃɪrɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>欢呼的；令人振奋的</td><td>the cheering crowd<br>欢呼的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cheering fans waited outside the stadium for two hours.  欢呼的球迷在体育场外等了两个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cordial</td><td>/ˈkɔːrdʒəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情友好的；令人振奋的</td><td>a cordial welcome<br>热情的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two leaders exchanged cordial greetings before the talks.  两位领导人在会谈前互致亲切的问候。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 festive</td><td>/ˈfestɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>欢乐的，节日的</td><td>a festive mood<br>欢乐的气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole street took on a festive air by evening.  到傍晚整条街都染上了节日气息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gay</td><td>/ˈɡeɪ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，欢快的</td><td>gay laughter<br>欢快的笑声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gay music and bright lanterns filled the village square that evening.  那晚欢快的音乐和明亮的灯笼充满了村中广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lighthearted</td><td>/ˌlaɪtˈhɑːrtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻松愉快的，无忧无虑的</td><td>a lighthearted comedy<br>轻松的喜剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We wanted a lighthearted film after such a stressful week.  在如此紧张的一周后，我们想看部轻松的电影。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>secular</b></td><td>/ˈsekjələr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>世俗的，尘世的</td><td>secular education<br>世俗教育</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country insists on secular education in all its public schools.  该国坚持在所有公立学校实行世俗教育。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carnal</td><td>/ˈkɑːrnl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肉体的，物质的；世俗的</td><td>carnal pleasures<br>肉体的享乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk warned his students against carnal pleasures and idle talk.  僧人告诫学生远离肉体享乐和闲谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 earthborn</del></td><td>/ˈɜrθbɔrn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>生于尘世的，凡人的</td><td>earthborn creatures<br>尘世间的生灵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poem contrasts heavenly spirits with weak earthborn men.  这首诗把天上的神灵与脆弱的凡人相对照。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 earthbound</td><td>/ˈɜːrθˌbaʊnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>地上的，世俗的</td><td>earthbound concerns<br>世俗的关切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His poems ignore heaven and dwell on earthbound troubles.  他的诗不谈天国，只写世俗的烦恼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fleshly</td><td>/ˈflɛʃli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肉体的；世俗的</td><td>fleshly desires<br>肉体的欲望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk warned his students against fleshly temptations.  那位僧人告诫学生要远离肉体的诱惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 material</td><td>/məˈtɪriəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>物质的；重要的；材料</td><td>material comforts<br>物质上的享受</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cared little for material wealth and lived very simply.  他不太在意物质财富，生活极为简朴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mundane</td><td>/mʌnˈdeɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平凡的</td><td>mundane daily chores<br>平凡的日常杂务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent the morning on mundane daily chores like laundry and dishes.  他整个上午都在做洗衣洗碗这类平凡的日常杂务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 sublunary</del></td><td>/sʌbˈluːnəri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>月下的，尘世的</td><td>sublunary affairs<br>尘世的事务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk claimed to have risen above all sublunary desires.  那位僧人自称已超脱一切尘世欲望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temporal</td><td>/ˈtempərəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>世俗的，尘世的</td><td>temporal power<br>世俗权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Medieval popes wielded both spiritual and temporal power in Europe.  中世纪的教皇在欧洲同时掌握宗教权力与世俗权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 terrene</del></td><td>/təˈriːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>尘世的，地上的</td><td>terrene pleasures<br>尘世的欢乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hermit despised terrene comforts and slept on bare stone.  隐士鄙视尘世的舒适，睡在光石头上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 terrestrial</td><td>/təˈrestriəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>陆地的；地球的；世俗的</td><td>terrestrial life<br>陆地生物；尘世生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Astronomers compare Mars with terrestrial deserts to picture its climate.  天文学家把火星与地球上的沙漠作比较来想象其气候。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worldly</td><td>/ˈwɜːrldli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>世间的，世俗的；老练的</td><td>worldly possessions<br>世俗财产</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave away all his worldly possessions and entered the monastery.  他捐出全部世俗财产，进了修道院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heavenly</td><td>/ˈhevənli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>天上的；美妙无比的</td><td>heavenly bodies<br>天体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Astronomers charted the heavenly bodies visible that clear night.  天文学家绘制了那个晴夜可见的天体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unearthly</td><td>/ʌnˈɜːrθli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非尘世的，诡异恐怖的</td><td>an unearthly silence<br>诡异的寂静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An unearthly cry came from somewhere deep in the forest.  森林深处传来一声诡异的叫声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unworldly</td><td>/ʌnˈwɜːrldli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>超脱世俗的，不谙世事的</td><td>an unworldly scholar<br>不谙世事的学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained unworldly, indifferent to both money and fame.  他一直超脱世俗，对金钱和名声都不在意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>summit</b></td><td>/ˈsʌmɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>顶点</td><td>reach the summit<br>登上顶峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The climbers reached the summit just before the storm closed in.  登山者在风暴逼近前登上顶峰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acme</td><td>/ˈækmi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>顶点，极点</td><td>the acme of perfection<br>完美的极致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That performance marked the acme of her long career.  那场演出标志着她漫长职业生涯的顶峰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apex</td><td>/ˈeɪˌpeks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>顶点，最高点</td><td>the apex of his career<br>他事业的顶峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At the apex of her fame she suddenly left the stage.  在名声的顶峰，她突然离开了舞台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 climax</td><td>/ˈklaɪˌmæks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>高潮，顶点</td><td>the climax of the story<br>故事的高潮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film builds slowly toward a violent and unexpected climax.  影片缓慢推向一个暴烈而意外的高潮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crescendo</td><td>/krəˈʃendoʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（渐强之后到达的）顶峰</td><td>reach a crescendo<br>达到顶峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Complaints about the choking smoke rose to a deafening crescendo.  对令人窒息的烟雾的抱怨达到了震耳欲聋的顶点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crest</td><td>/krest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>顶部，浪尖，山顶</td><td>the crest of a wave<br>浪尖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The surfers waited patiently for the crest of the next big wave.  冲浪者们耐心等待下一个大浪的浪尖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crown</td><td>/ˈkraʊn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使达到顶点；王冠</td><td>crown a career<br>为职业生涯添彩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prize crowned a long career of quiet, patient research.  这个奖为漫长而耐心的研究生涯锦上添花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 culmination</td><td>/ˌkʌlməˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>顶点，高潮，最终结果</td><td>the culmination of efforts<br>努力的最终成果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prize was the culmination of twenty years of research.  这个奖项是二十年研究的最高成果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meridian</td><td>/məˈrɪdiən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>顶点，全盛期；子午线</td><td>at the meridian of his career<br>在他事业顶峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The composer died at the meridian of his creative powers.  这位作曲家在创作力顶峰时去世。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peak</td><td>/ˈpiːk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>顶峰，最高点</td><td>at the peak of<br>在…的顶峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales peaked in December and then fell sharply in January.  销售在十二月达到顶峰，一月急剧下滑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pinnacle</td><td>/ˈpɪnəkl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>顶峰，巅峰</td><td>the pinnacle of success<br>成功的顶峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At thirty she had reached the pinnacle of her profession.  三十岁时她已达到职业生涯的巅峰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 top</td><td>/ˈtɑːp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>顶点，最高处</td><td>at the top of the list<br>在名单最上方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her name sat at the top of the shortlist.  她的名字排在候选名单最上方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 zenith</td><td>/ˈzenɪθ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>最高点，巅峰</td><td>at the zenith of<br>在…的巅峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empire stood at the zenith of its power around that year.  大约在那一年，这个帝国正处于国力的巅峰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bottom</td><td>/ˈbɑːtəm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>底部，最低点</td><td>at the bottom<br>在底部</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their team finished at the bottom of the league again.  他们的球队又一次排在联赛末位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nadir</td><td>/ˈneɪdɪr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>最低点，谷底</td><td>reach the nadir<br>到达最低点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His confidence reached its nadir after the third rejection.  第三次被拒后他的信心降到了最低点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>summon</b></td><td>/ˈsʌmən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>召集，召唤</td><td>summon the committee<br>召集委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general summoned all his officers before launching the attack.  将军在发动进攻前召集了所有的军官。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assemble</td><td>/əˈsembəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>聚集，集合；组装</td><td>assemble a crowd<br>聚集起人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers assembled in the square to hear the announcement.  村民聚集在广场上听那则公告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 convene</td><td>/kənˈviːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>召开，召集</td><td>convene a meeting<br>召开会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board convened an emergency session to discuss the sudden resignation.  董事会召开紧急会议讨论这次突然辞职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 convoke</td><td>/kənˈvoʊk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>召集开会</td><td>convoke a special session<br>召集特别会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The president will convoke an emergency session of the council.  总统将召集理事会举行紧急会议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muster</td><td>/ˈmʌstər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>集合，召集；一群人</td><td>muster the troops<br>召集部队</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain mustered his crew on the upper deck.  船长把船员集合到上层甲板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rally</td><td>/ˈræli/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>恢复，重振；集合；集会</td><td>rally after surgery<br>手术后恢复过来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient rallied surprisingly quickly after a week of treatment.  经过一周治疗，病人恢复得出人意料地快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dismiss</td><td>/dɪˈsmɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解散，打发走；解雇；不予考虑</td><td>dismiss the class<br>宣布下课</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher dismissed the class ten minutes ahead of time.  老师提前十分钟宣布下课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sumptuous</b></td><td>/ˈsʌmptʃuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>豪华的，奢侈的</td><td>a sumptuous banquet<br>一场豪华的宴会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hotel served a sumptuous banquet for the visiting delegation.  酒店为来访代表团摆了一场奢华的宴会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deluxe</td><td>/dəˈlʌks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>豪华的，高级的</td><td>a deluxe suite<br>豪华套房</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They booked a deluxe suite overlooking the harbor.  他们订了一间可俯瞰港口的豪华套房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lavish</td><td>/ˈlævɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>奢侈的，大量的，无节制的；挥霍，浪费</td><td>a lavish banquet<br>奢华的宴会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They hosted a lavish banquet for three hundred guests in the garden.  他们在花园里为三百位客人举办了一场奢华的宴会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lush</td><td>/lʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>茂盛的，繁茂的</td><td>lush vegetation<br>茂盛的植被</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lush vegetation covered the valley floor after weeks of steady rain.  连续数周的降雨之后，茂盛的植被覆盖了谷底。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 luxurious</td><td>/lʌɡˈʒʊriəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>奢侈的</td><td>a luxurious hotel suite<br>豪华的酒店套房</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent their anniversary in a luxurious hotel suite overlooking the harbor.  他们在一间俯瞰港口的豪华酒店套房里度过了结婚纪念日。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 opulent</td><td>/ˈɑːpjələnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>富裕的；华丽的</td><td>an opulent hotel lobby<br>豪华的酒店大厅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The opulent ballroom was lined with gold mirrors and velvet chairs.  这间华丽的舞厅摆满了金框镜子和天鹅绒椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 palatial</td><td>/pəˈleɪʃl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宫殿般奢华的</td><td>a palatial hotel lobby<br>宫殿般的酒店大堂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old bank had a palatial marble hall.  那家老银行有一座宫殿般的大理石厅堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resplendent</td><td>/rɪˈsplendənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>华丽辉煌的</td><td>resplendent in uniform<br>身着华丽制服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bride appeared resplendent in a gown embroidered with hundreds of tiny pearls.  新娘身着绣满数百颗小珍珠的礼服，光彩夺目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 splendid</td><td>/ˈsplendɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>壮丽的；极好的</td><td>a splendid marble hall<br>壮丽的大理石厅堂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the roof we had a splendid view of the harbor.  从屋顶望去，港口的景色极为壮丽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 superb</td><td>/sʊˈpɜːrb/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极好的，卓越的</td><td>a superb performance<br>一场卓越的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young violinist gave a superb performance that night.  那晚这位年轻小提琴手献上了卓越的表演。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ascetic</td><td>/əˈsetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>苦行的，禁欲的；苦行者</td><td>an ascetic lifestyle<br>苦修的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk led an ascetic life on bread and cold water.  这位僧人以面包和冷水过着自我克制的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 austere</td><td>/ɔːˈstɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>朴素的，朴实无华的</td><td>an austere room<br>简朴的房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He slept in an austere room with a bed and one chair.  他睡在一间只有一张床和一把椅子的朴素房间里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humble</td><td>/ˈhʌmbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑微的，地位低下的；谦逊的</td><td>humble origins<br>卑微的出身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rose from humble origins to lead the company.  他出身卑微，最终领导了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spartan</td><td>/ˈspɑːrtn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简朴的，节约的</td><td>spartan living conditions<br>简朴的生活条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks lived in spartan rooms with only a bed and a lamp.  僧侣们住在只有一床一灯的简朴房间里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
